if("ResizeObserver" in window) new ResizeObserver(measureDock).observe(document.getElementById("combatDock"));

/* ---------- INTERFAZ COMPACTA: preferencias opcionales por personaje ---------- */
const UI_TABS=[["combat","Combate"],["character","Personaje"],["magic","Magia"],["inventory","Inventario"],["equipment","Equipamiento"],["shop","Tienda"],["navigation","Navegación"]];
function uiPreferences(s){
  if(!s.ui || typeof s.ui!=="object" || Array.isArray(s.ui))s.ui={};
  if(!UI_TABS.some(([id])=>id===s.ui.tab))s.ui.tab="combat";
  if(!s.ui.panels || typeof s.ui.panels!=="object" || Array.isArray(s.ui.panels))s.ui.panels={};
  return s.ui;
}
function organizeWorkspace(){
  const prefs=uiPreferences(state);
  document.getElementById("uiWorkspace")?.remove();
  const root=document.createElement("div");root.id="uiWorkspace";
  root.innerHTML=`<div class="workspaceHeader"><div class="identity"><b>${esc(state.name)}</b> · ${esc(classById(state.cls).name)} · ${esc(raceById(state.race).name)}<br><span id="compactLevelGold" class="muted"></span></div><button class="small" data-act="rest">Descansar</button><button class="small" data-act="switchCharacter">Cambiar personaje</button></div><div class="workspaceTabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Secciones del juego">${UI_TABS.map(([id,label])=>`<button id="tab_${id}" role="tab" aria-controls="page_${id}" data-ui-tab="${id}">${label}</button>`).join("")}</div><button id="compactNavSummary" class="navCompact hidden" data-ui-nav="true"></button>${UI_TABS.map(([id,label])=>`<section id="page_${id}" class="workspacePage hidden" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab_${id}" aria-label="${label}"></section>`).join("")}`;
  document.getElementById("gameScreen").append(root);
  const place=(source,mapping)=>{
    const nodes=Array.from(source.children);let body=null;
    for(const node of nodes){
      if(node.tagName==="H2"){
        const label=node.textContent.trim(),spec=mapping(label);
        const panel=document.createElement("details");panel.dataset.uiPanel=spec.key;
        const summary=document.createElement("summary");summary.textContent=label;panel.append(summary);
        body=document.createElement("div");body.className="sectionBody";panel.append(body);
        panel.open=typeof prefs.panels[spec.key]==="boolean"?prefs.panels[spec.key]:spec.open;
        document.getElementById("page_"+spec.tab).append(panel);node.remove();
      }else if(body)body.append(node);
    }
  };
  place(document.getElementById("pChar"),label=>{
    if(label==="Habilidades")return {tab:"character",key:"skills",open:true};
    if(label==="Equipo")return {tab:"equipment",key:"equipment",open:true};
    if(label==="Inventario")return {tab:"inventory",key:"inventory",open:true};
    if(label==="Guardado")return {tab:"character",key:"save",open:false};
    return {tab:"character",key:"attributes",open:true};
  });
  place(document.getElementById("pCombat"),label=>{
    if(label.startsWith("Hechizos"))return {tab:"magic",key:"magic",open:true};
    if(label==="Navegación")return {tab:"navigation",key:"navigation",open:false};
    if(label==="Tienda")return {tab:"shop",key:"shop",open:true};
    if(label==="Registro de combate")return {tab:"combat",key:"log",open:false};
    return {tab:"combat",key:"combat",open:true};
  });
  root.querySelectorAll("details[data-ui-panel]").forEach(panel=>{
    const key=panel.dataset.uiPanel;
    if(typeof prefs.panels[key]==="boolean")panel.open=prefs.panels[key];
    panel.addEventListener("toggle",()=>{
      if(!panel.isConnected||!state||busy||managing)return;
      const p=uiPreferences(state);
      if(p.panels[key]===panel.open)return;
      p.panels[key]=panel.open;persist();
    });
  });
  selectWorkspaceTab(prefs.tab,false);
}
function selectWorkspaceTab(id,save=true){
  if(!state||!UI_TABS.some(([key])=>key===id))return;
  uiPreferences(state).tab=id;
  for(const [key] of UI_TABS){
    const selected=key===id,tab=document.getElementById("tab_"+key),page=document.getElementById("page_"+key);
    if(tab){tab.setAttribute("aria-selected",String(selected));tab.tabIndex=selected?0:-1;}
    if(page)page.classList.toggle("hidden",!selected);
  }
  if(save)persist();
}
function updateWorkspaceLive(){
  const label=document.getElementById("compactLevelGold");
  if(label)label.textContent=`Nivel ${levelFromXp(state.xp)} · ${fmt(state.gold)} oro`;
  const button=document.getElementById("compactNavSummary");if(!button)return;
  const has=navHas(state);button.classList.toggle("hidden",!has);
  if(has){const n=state.nav,route=NAV.find(a=>a.id===n.id);button.textContent=`⚓ ${route?route.name:n.id} · ${n.paused?"Pausada":"Navegando"} · Restan ${fmtDur(Math.ceil(n.remainingMs/1000))} · Ver expedición`;}
}
document.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const button=e.target.closest("[data-ui-tab],[data-ui-nav]");
  if(!button||busy||managing||!state||!ready)return;
  if(button.dataset.uiNav){
    const panel=document.querySelector('details[data-ui-panel="navigation"]');
    if(panel){panel.open=true;uiPreferences(state).panels.navigation=true;}
    selectWorkspaceTab("navigation");
  }else selectWorkspaceTab(button.dataset.uiTab);
});


/* ---------- PERSONAJES Y PERSISTENCIA v7 ---------- */
const MAX_CHARACTERS=5;
const copyJSON=value=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
let roster={version:7,revision:0,savedAt:0,activeId:null,characters:[]};
let storageReady=false,managing=false,writeQueue=Promise.resolve(),lastStamp=0;
function newCharacterId(){
  return globalThis.crypto&&crypto.randomUUID?crypto.randomUUID():"pc-"+Date.now().toString(36)+"-"+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
}
function freezeCharacter(s){
  s.combat.active=false;pauseNav(s);
  // restLeft, cooldowns, parálisis y avance parcial no cambian mientras está inactivo.
  s.savedAt=0;
}
function finiteNonnegative(n){return typeof n==="number"&&Number.isFinite(n)&&n>=0;}
function safeCharacter(s){
  if(!validState(s))return false;
  if(![s.xp,s.gold,s.hp,s.skill.xp].every(finiteNonnegative))return false;
  if(s.mp!==undefined&&!finiteNonnegative(s.mp))return false;
  for(const key of ["pT","eT","enemyHp","enemyPara"]){
    if(s.combat[key]!==undefined&&!finiteNonnegative(s.combat[key]))return false;
  }
  for(const key of ["restLeft","restCd","para"]){if(s[key]!==undefined&&!finiteNonnegative(s[key]))return false;}
  for(const key of ["skills","inventory","defeated","owned","equip","spells","cds"]){
    if(s[key]!==undefined&&(!s[key]||typeof s[key]!=="object"||Array.isArray(s[key])))return false;
  }
  if(s.skills&&Object.values(s.skills).some(k=>!k||!finiteNonnegative(k.xp)))return false;
  for(const key of ["inventory","defeated","cds"]){if(s[key]&&Object.values(s[key]).some(v=>!finiteNonnegative(v)))return false;}
  return true;
}
function decodeRoster(raw){
  if(!raw||typeof raw!=="object")return null;
  if(raw.version===7){
    if(!Array.isArray(raw.characters)||raw.characters.length>MAX_CHARACTERS)return null;
    const ids=new Set();
    for(const entry of raw.characters){
      if(!entry||typeof entry.id!=="string"||!entry.id||ids.has(entry.id)||!safeCharacter(entry.state))return null;
      ids.add(entry.id);
    }
    if(raw.activeId!==null&&!ids.has(raw.activeId))return null;
    const result=copyJSON(raw);
    result.revision=Number.isSafeInteger(raw.revision)&&raw.revision>=0?raw.revision:0;
    result.savedAt=finiteNonnegative(raw.savedAt)?raw.savedAt:0;
    for(const entry of result.characters){
      // Normalización idempotente: no entrega premios ni calcula tiempo.
      entry.state=migrateState(entry.state,7);
      if(entry.id!==result.activeId)freezeCharacter(entry.state);
    }
    return result;
  }
  if(![1,2,3,4,5,6].includes(raw.version)||!safeCharacter(raw.state))return null;
  const s=unwrap(raw);if(!s)return null;
  const id=newCharacterId();
  return {version:7,revision:0,savedAt:finiteNonnegative(raw.savedAt)?raw.savedAt:(s.savedAt||0),activeId:id,characters:[{id,state:s}]};
}
async function durableBackup(raw,reason){
  const key=(gameMode==="dev"?"argentum-idle-dev-backup-v":"argentum-idle-backup-v")+raw.version+"-"+reason+"-"+Date.now()+"-"+newCharacterId();
  const text=JSON.stringify(raw);let localOK=false,idbOK=false;
  try{localStorage.setItem(key,text);localOK=localStorage.getItem(key)===text;}catch(_){}
  try{
    const db=await openDB();
    await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      const tx=db.transaction("saves","readwrite");tx.objectStore("saves").put(copyJSON(raw),key);
      tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error);tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||Error("respaldo abortado"));
    });idbOK=true;
  }catch(_){}
  if(!localOK&&!idbOK){const error=Error("No se pudo crear el respaldo previo. No se reemplazó el guardado.");error.backupFailure=true;throw error;}
}
function makeRosterRecord(){
  const now=Math.max(Date.now(),lastStamp+1,Number(roster.savedAt||0)+1);lastStamp=now;
  if(state&&roster.activeId){
    const entry=roster.characters.find(c=>c.id===roster.activeId);
    if(!entry)throw Error("El personaje activo no pertenece al conjunto.");
    entry.state=state;state.savedAt=now;
  }
  roster.version=7;roster.savedAt=now;roster.revision++;
  return copyJSON(roster);
}
function saveRoster(){
  if(!storageReady)return Promise.resolve(false);
  const record=makeRosterRecord();
    const job=async()=>{
    let localOK=false,idbOK=false;
    try{localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY,JSON.stringify(record));localOK=true;}catch(_){}
    try{await idbPut(record);idbOK=true;}catch(_){}
    if(!localOK&&!idbOK){setStatus("No se pudo guardar: exportá tu partida antes de cerrar.",true);return false;}
    lastSave=new Date(record.savedAt).toLocaleTimeString("es-AR");
    setStatus("Guardado v7 · "+roster.characters.length+" / 5 · "+lastSave+" · "+(localOK&&idbOK?"IndexedDB + localStorage":localOK?"localStorage":"IndexedDB"),false);
    return true;
  };
  const task=writeQueue.then(job,job);writeQueue=task.catch(()=>false);return task;
}
function restoreRosterSnapshot(raw){
  roster=copyJSON(raw);
  state=roster.activeId?roster.characters.find(c=>c.id===roster.activeId).state:null;
  ready=!!state;runtimeAt=ready?Date.now():0;runtimeRemainder=0;
}
function showCharacters(){
  for(const id of ["createScreen","gameScreen","combatDock"])document.getElementById(id).classList.add("hidden");
  document.getElementById("characterScreen").classList.remove("hidden");
  document.getElementById("characterCount").textContent=roster.characters.length;
  document.getElementById("newCharacterBtn").disabled=roster.characters.length>=MAX_CHARACTERS||!storageReady;
  document.getElementById("characterList").innerHTML=roster.characters.map(entry=>{
    const s=entry.state;
    return `<div class="panel"><b>${esc(s.name)}</b><div class="muted">${esc(classById(s.cls).name)} · ${esc(raceById(s.race).name)} · Nivel ${levelFromXp(s.xp)}</div><div class="muted">${fmt(s.gold)} oro${navHas(s)?" · Expedición pausada":""}${s.restLeft>0?" · Descanso pendiente":""}</div><div class="actions"><button class="primary" data-act="selectCharacter" data-id="${esc(entry.id)}">Jugar</button><button class="danger small" data-act="deleteCharacter" data-id="${esc(entry.id)}">Borrar</button></div></div>`;
  }).join("")||'<p class="muted">Todavía no hay personajes. Creá el primero.</p>';
  measureDock();
}
async function changeRoster(action,id){
  if(managing||busy||!storageReady)return;
  managing=true;let previous=null;
  try{
    if(state&&ready)syncRuntime();
    if(busy)throw Error("Esperá a que termine el cálculo offline y volvé a intentar.");
    previous=makeRosterRecord();
    if(action==="leave"){
      if(typeof e210StopFishing==='function')e210StopFishing();
      if(state)freezeCharacter(state);
      roster.activeId=null;state=null;ready=false;runtimeAt=0;runtimeRemainder=0;
    }else if(action==="select"){
      if(roster.activeId)throw Error("Volvé primero a la selección.");
      const entry=roster.characters.find(c=>c.id===id);if(!entry)throw Error("Personaje inexistente.");
      freezeCharacter(entry.state);roster.activeId=id;state=entry.state;ready=true;runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;
    }else if(action==="create"){
      if(roster.characters.length>=MAX_CHARACTERS)throw Error("Límite de 5 personajes.");
      if(roster.activeId||!classById(draft.cls)||!raceById(draft.race)||draft.name.trim().length<2)throw Error("Completá la creación del personaje.");
      draft.name=draft.name.trim().slice(0,18);
      const s=newState(),newId=newCharacterId();roster.characters.push({id:newId,state:s});
      roster.activeId=newId;state=s;ready=true;runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;
    }else if(action==="delete"){
      const entry=roster.characters.find(c=>c.id===id);if(!entry)return;
      if(!confirm(`¿Borrar a «${entry.state.name}» (nivel ${levelFromXp(entry.state.xp)})? Solo se elimina este personaje.`))return;
      await durableBackup(previous,"before-delete");
      if(roster.activeId===id)e210StopFishing();
      roster.characters=roster.characters.filter(c=>c.id!==id);
      if(roster.activeId===id){roster.activeId=null;state=null;ready=false;runtimeAt=0;runtimeRemainder=0;}
    }
    if(!await saveRoster())throw Error("No se pudo guardar el cambio. Se conservó la sesión anterior.");
    if(state){showGame();renderGame();}else showCharacters();
  }catch(e){
    if(previous)restoreRosterSnapshot(previous);
    toast(e.message);if(state){showGame();renderGame();}else showCharacters();
  }finally{managing=false;}
}
// Captura: evita ejecutar los antiguos manejadores de un solo personaje.
document.addEventListener("click",async e=>{
  const button=e.target.closest("button");if(!button)return;
  const act=button.dataset.act;
  const handled=button.id==="createBtn"||["switchCharacter","selectCharacter","deleteCharacter","reset","newCharacter","cancelCreate","importRoster","rosterTests","export"].includes(act);
  if(!handled)return;
  e.stopImmediatePropagation();e.preventDefault();
  if(button.disabled||busy||managing||!storageReady)return;
  if(button.id==="createBtn")return changeRoster("create");
  if(act==="switchCharacter")return changeRoster("leave");
  if(act==="selectCharacter")return changeRoster("select",button.dataset.id);
  if(act==="deleteCharacter"||act==="reset")return changeRoster("delete",button.dataset.id||roster.activeId);
  if(act==="export")return exportJSON();
  if(act==="rosterTests")return runRosterTests();
  if(act==="importRoster")return document.getElementById("rosterImportFile").click();
  if(act==="newCharacter"){
    if(roster.characters.length>=5)return;
    draft={name:"",race:null,cls:null};document.getElementById("charName").value="";showCreate();
  }else if(act==="cancelCreate")showCharacters();
},true);
document.addEventListener("change",e=>{
  if(e.target.id==="rosterImportFile"&&e.target.files[0]){importJSON(e.target.files[0]);e.target.value="";}
});

/* Pruebas puras: no escriben IndexedDB/localStorage ni cambian la sesión real. */
function runRosterTests(){
  const output=[];
  const assert=(label,fn)=>{try{if(!fn())throw Error("resultado inesperado");output.push("✓ "+label);}catch(e){output.push("✗ "+label+": "+e.message);}};
  const fixture=()=>{
    const prev=draft;try{draft={name:"Prueba",race:"humano",cls:"mago"};return newState();}finally{draft=prev;}
  };
  const base=()=>({version:7,revision:1,savedAt:1000,activeId:"a",characters:[{id:"a",state:fixture()},{id:"b",state:fixture()}]});
  assert("Migración v6 conserva recursos, equipo, hechizos y expedición",()=>{
    const s=fixture();s.gold=987;s.xp=4321;s.skills.navegacion.xp=2222;s.spells.apocalipsis=true;s.owned.hacha=true;s.equip.weapon="hacha";
    s.nav={id:"costa",left:3600,totalMs:7200000,remainingMs:3600000,elapsedMs:3600000,paused:false,legacy:false,earnedGold:60,earnedXp:400};
    s.navCarry={gold:123,xp:456};const raw={version:6,savedAt:1000,state:s},before=JSON.stringify(raw),r=decodeRoster(raw),t=r.characters[0].state;
    return JSON.stringify(raw)===before&&t.gold===987&&t.xp===4321&&t.skills.navegacion.xp===2222&&t.spells.apocalipsis&&t.equip.weapon==="hacha"&&t.nav.remainingMs===3600000&&t.navCarry.gold===123&&r.activeId===r.characters[0].id;
  });
  assert("Compatibilidad de formatos v1 a v6",()=>[1,2,3,4,5,6].every(version=>!!decodeRoster({version,savedAt:1000,state:fixture()})));
  assert("Nombres repetidos, identificadores únicos",()=>!!decodeRoster(base()));
  assert("Máximo 5 y rechazo de IDs duplicados",()=>{const r=base();r.characters[1].id="a";if(decodeRoster(r))return false;r.characters=Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=>({id:String(i),state:fixture()}));r.activeId="0";return !decodeRoster(r);});
  assert("Estado independiente después de serializar",()=>{const r=decodeRoster(base());r.characters[0].state.gold+=500;r.characters[0].state.inventory.material_rata=3;return r.characters[1].state.gold===100&&!r.characters[1].state.inventory.material_rata;});
  assert("Cambiar congela combate, navegación y descanso pendiente",()=>{const s=fixture();s.combat.active=true;s.nav={id:"costa",remainingMs:1000,paused:false};s.restLeft=9;freezeCharacter(s);return !s.combat.active&&s.nav.paused&&s.restLeft===9;});
  assert("Selección no reactiva combate ni navegación",()=>{const r=base();r.activeId=null;r.characters[0].state.combat.active=true;const t=decodeRoster(r);return !t.characters[0].state.combat.active&&t.activeId===null;});
  assert("Guardar/cargar conserva todos los personajes",()=>{const r=base(),t=decodeRoster(copyJSON(r));return t.characters.length===2&&t.activeId==="a"&&t.characters[0].state.name===r.characters[0].state.name;});
  assert("Importación pausada no genera recompensas",()=>{const t=decodeRoster(base());t.characters.forEach(e=>freezeCharacter(e.state));t.activeId=null;return t.characters.every(e=>!e.state.combat.active&&!navActive(e.state)&&e.state.gold===100);});
  assert("Offline solo corresponde al personaje activo",()=>{const r=base();r.characters[0].state.combat.active=true;r.characters[1].state.combat.active=true;const t=decodeRoster(r);return t.characters[0].state.combat.active&&!t.characters[1].state.combat.active;});
  assert("Cierre desde selección: cero actividad offline",()=>{const r=base();r.activeId=null;const t=decodeRoster(r);return !t.activeId&&t.characters.every(e=>!e.state.combat.active&&!navActive(e.state));});
  assert("Offline navegación: tope 24 h, sin premio duplicado",()=>{const s=fixture();s.nav={id:"costa",remainingMs:86400000,totalMs:86400000,elapsedMs:0,paused:false,legacy:false,earnedGold:0,earnedXp:0};advanceNav(s,Math.min(172800,CFG.OFFLINE_MAX_SEC)*1000);const saved=copyJSON(s);normalizeNav(saved);advanceNav(saved,86400000);return saved.gold===1540&&saved.skills.navegacion.xp===9600&&!navHas(saved);});
  assert("Viaje legado conserva su recompensa única",()=>{const s=fixture();s.nav={id:"costa",left:10};normalizeNav(s);advanceNav(s,10000,()=>0);const t=copyJSON(s);normalizeNav(t);advanceNav(t,10000,()=>0);return t.gold===103&&t.skills.navegacion.xp===30;});
  assert("Borrado de un ID no elimina al otro",()=>{const r=base();r.characters=r.characters.filter(e=>e.id!=="a");return r.characters.length===1&&r.characters[0].id==="b";});
  assert("Rechaza NaN y estados corruptos sin descartar otros silenciosamente",()=>{const r=base();r.characters[1].state.gold=NaN;return !decodeRoster(r);});
  const box=document.getElementById("rosterTestOutput");box.textContent=output.join("\n")+"\n\n"+output.filter(x=>x.startsWith("✓")).length+" / "+output.length+" aprobadas.\nPruebas puras, ejecutadas solo al pulsar este botón. No prueban almacenamiento real, eventos del navegador ni cierre de pestaña.";
}

/* ---------- ARRANQUE v7 ---------- */
async function bootRoster(){
  try{
    // All inline extensions must be parsed before restoring a character and rendering it.
    if(document.readyState==='loading')await new Promise(resolve=>document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',resolve,{once:true}));
    const saved=await loadSaved();
    roster=saved?saved.data:roster;
    storageReady=true;
    if(roster.activeId){
      state=roster.characters.find(c=>c.id===roster.activeId).state;ready=true;
      showGame();renderGame();
      const elapsed=Math.max(0,(Date.now()-roster.savedAt)/1000);
      await simulateOffline(elapsed);
      // Persiste el resultado antes de habilitar acciones de cambio de personaje.
      runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;
      if(!await saveRoster())throw Error("El progreso calculado no pudo guardarse. No cierres sin exportar una copia.");
      renderGame();
    }else{
      state=null;ready=false;showCharacters();
      if(saved&&saved.raw.version!==7&&!await saveRoster())throw Error("No se pudo completar la migración.");
      setStatus("Selección de personajes · nadie genera progreso offline",false);
    }
    setInterval(tick,1000);
    setInterval(()=>{if(storageReady&&!managing&&!busy)persist();},CFG.AUTOSAVE_MS);
    window.addEventListener("beforeunload",()=>{
      if(!storageReady||busy||managing)return;
      try{if(state&&ready)syncRuntime();if(!busy)localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY,JSON.stringify(makeRosterRecord()));}catch(_){}
    });
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange",()=>{if(document.hidden&&storageReady&&!busy&&!managing)persist();});
  }catch(e){
    storageReady=false;ready=false;
    setStatus("No se habilitó la escritura: "+e.message,true);
    const panel=document.createElement("div");panel.className="panel";panel.textContent="Inicio detenido para proteger los guardados. "+e.message+" No borres los datos del navegador. Podés recargar para reintentar.";document.querySelector("main").prepend(panel);
  }
}
if(navigator.locks&&navigator.locks.request){
  navigator.locks.request("argentum-idle-v7-owner",{ifAvailable:true},async lock=>{
    if(!lock){setStatus("El juego está abierto en otra pestaña. Cerrala y recargá esta.",true);return;}
    await bootRoster();await new Promise(()=>{});
  }).catch(e=>setStatus("No se pudo obtener el bloqueo de guardado: "+e.message,true));
}else bootRoster();

/* E1.4: mejoras de interfaz y estadísticas; no modifica el motor ni el formato v7. */
const e13Sessions = new WeakMap();
function e13Session(s){let x=e13Sessions.get(s);if(!x){x={seconds:0,kills:0,xp:0,gold:0};e13Sessions.set(s,x);}return x;}
const e13OriginalStep=step;
step=function(){
 const s=state,live=!!(s&&ready&&!OFF.active&&!busy&&!managing&&s.combat.active&&!navActive(s)&&s.restLeft===0);
 if(live)e13Session(s).seconds++;
 return e13OriginalStep();
};
const e13OriginalKill=onKill;
onKill=function(en){
 const s=state,eligible=!!(s&&ready&&!OFF.active&&!busy&&!managing),xp=s?.xp||0,gold=s?.gold||0;
 const result=e13OriginalKill(en);
 if(eligible&&s===state){const a=e13Session(s);a.kills++;a.xp+=Math.max(0,s.xp-xp);a.gold+=Math.max(0,s.gold-gold);}
 return result;
};
function e13FmtSeconds(n){const m=Math.floor(n/60),h=Math.floor(m/60);return h?`${h} h ${m%60} min`:`${m} min`;}
function e13Refresh(){
 if(!state||!ready)return;
 const s=state,d=derive(s),en=enemyById(s.combat.enemyId),lo=xpFor(d.L),hi=xpFor(d.L+1),pct=clamp(100*(s.xp-lo)/(hi-lo),0,100);
 const set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el&&el.textContent!==String(v))el.textContent=v;};
 set('dockPlayerLevel','Nv '+d.L);set('dockPlayerGold','🪙 '+fmt(s.gold));set('dockPlayerXpText',pct.toFixed(1)+'% · faltan '+fmt(hi-s.xp)+' XP');
 const bar=document.getElementById('dockPlayerXpBar');if(bar)bar.style.width=pct+'%';
 set('dockEnemyRewards','+'+fmt(en.xp)+' XP · '+fmt(en.goldMin)+'–'+fmt(en.goldMax)+' oro');
 const x=e13Session(s),box=document.getElementById('e13SessionBox');
 if(box){const hours=x.seconds/3600,rate=hours>0&&x.kills>=2?x.xp/hours:0,grate=hours>0&&x.kills>=2?x.gold/hours:0;
 const vals={e13Time:e13FmtSeconds(x.seconds),e13Kills:fmt(x.kills),e13Xp:fmt(x.xp),e13Gold:fmt(x.gold),e13XpHour:rate?fmt(rate):'Calculando…',e13GoldHour:grate?fmt(grate):'Calculando…',e13Eta:rate?e13FmtSeconds(Math.ceil((hi-s.xp)/rate*3600)):'Calculando…'};
 for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(vals))set(k,v);
 }
 e13EconomyRefresh();
}
const e13PreviousLive=updateLive;
updateLive=function(){e13PreviousLive();e13Refresh();};
const e13PreviousRender=renderGame;
renderGame=function(){e13PreviousRender();e13InstallPanels();e13Refresh();};
function e13InstallPanels(){
 const main=document.querySelector('.hybridMain')||document.getElementById('pCombat');
 if(main&&!document.getElementById('e13SessionBox')){
 const panel=document.createElement('details');panel.id='e13SessionBox';panel.className='panel e13Session';
 panel.innerHTML='<summary>📈 Estadísticas de sesión (solo combate en vivo)</summary><div class="e13SessionGrid">'+[
 ['e13Time','Tiempo combatiendo'],['e13Kills','Enemigos derrotados'],['e13Xp','XP obtenida'],['e13Gold','Oro ganado'],['e13XpHour','XP/h'],['e13GoldHour','Oro/h'],['e13Eta','Tiempo al próximo nivel']
 ].map(([id,label])=>`<div><small>${label}</small><b id="${id}">—</b></div>`).join('')+'</div><p class="muted">Ritmo promedio de la sesión actual, desde 2 bajas. No incluye navegación, progreso offline ni gastos.</p><button class="small" data-e13-reset>Reiniciar estadísticas</button>';
 const combat=main.querySelector('#pCombat')||main;combat.prepend(panel);
 }
 const goal=document.getElementById('visibleProgressBox');
 if(goal&&!document.getElementById('e13Economy')){
 const d=document.createElement('details');d.className='e13Economy';d.id='e13Economy';d.innerHTML='<summary>🪙 Próximas compras recomendadas</summary><div id="e13EconomyList"></div>';
 goal.append(d);
 }
}
function e13EconomyRefresh(){
 const host=document.getElementById('e13EconomyList');if(!host||!state)return;
 const s=state,candidates=[];
 for(const it of EQUIP){if(s.owned[it.id]||s.equip[it.slot]===it.id)continue;
 const can=reqOk(s,it.req),equipped=s.equip[it.slot]&&itemById(s.equip[it.slot]);
 const old=equipped?((equipped.attack||0)+(equipped.defense||0)+(equipped.magicRes||0)+(equipped.mp||0)/5):0;
 const val=(it.attack||0)+(it.defense||0)+(it.magicRes||0)+(it.mp||0)/5;
 if(val<=old)continue;
 candidates.push({id:it.id,name:it.name,price:it.price,why:statsText(it),req:can?'Disponible':reqText(it.req),available:can});
 }
 for(const sp of SPELLS){if(sp.price<=0||s.spells[sp.id])continue;candidates.push({id:sp.id,name:sp.name,price:sp.price,why:sp.desc,req:skl(s,'magia')>=sp.req?'Disponible':'Magia nivel '+sp.req,available:skl(s,'magia')>=sp.req});}
 candidates.sort((a,b)=>(b.available-a.available)||Math.abs(a.price-s.gold)-Math.abs(b.price-s.gold));
 const picks=candidates.slice(0,4),sig=picks.map(x=>x.id).join('|');
 if(host.dataset.signature!==sig){host.dataset.signature=sig;host.innerHTML=picks.map(x=>`<article data-e13-item="${x.id}"><b>${esc(x.name)}</b><div class="muted">${esc(x.why)} · ${esc(x.req)}</div><div class="bar xp"><div></div></div><small></small></article>`).join('')||'<p>Ya tenés todas las mejoras disponibles.</p>';}
 for(const x of picks){const el=host.querySelector('[data-e13-item="'+x.id+'"]');if(!el)continue;
 el.querySelector('.bar > div').style.width=clamp(s.gold/x.price*100,0,100)+'%';
 el.querySelector('small').textContent=s.gold>=x.price?`Tenés ${fmt(s.gold)} de ${fmt(x.price)} oro. ${x.available?'Podés comprarlo en Tienda.':'Todavía falta cumplir el requisito.'}`:`Tenés ${fmt(s.gold)} de ${fmt(x.price)} oro; faltan ${fmt(x.price-s.gold)}. ${x.available?'Buscá oro derrotando enemigos o navegando.':'Además: '+x.req+'.'}`;
 }
}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
 const reset=e.target.closest('[data-e13-reset]');if(reset&&state){e13Sessions.set(state,{seconds:0,kills:0,xp:0,gold:0});e13Refresh();return;}
 const btn=e.target.closest('[data-e13-enemy-info]');if(!btn||!state)return;
 const en=enemyById(btn.dataset.e13EnemyInfo);if(!en)return;
 let dialog=document.getElementById('e13EnemyDialog');if(!dialog){dialog=document.createElement('dialog');dialog.id='e13EnemyDialog';document.body.append(dialog);}
 const dg=dungeonById(en.dungeonId),un=enemyUnlocked(en,derive(state).L)&&dungeonOk(dg,state);
 dialog.innerHTML=`<h2>${esc(en.name)} · Nv ${en.level}</h2><p>${un?'✓ Desbloqueado':'🔒 Requiere nivel '+Math.max(dg.minLevel,en.level-3)+(dg.navReq?' y Navegación '+dg.navReq:'')}</p><p>HP ${fmt(en.hp)} · Ataque ${en.attack} · Defensa ${en.defense} · Res. mágica ${en.magicRes}</p><p>${en.magic?'Ataque mágico':'Ataque físico'} · ${en.boss?'Jefe':'Enemigo normal'}</p><p>Recompensa: ${fmt(en.xp)} XP y ${fmt(en.goldMin)}–${fmt(en.goldMax)} oro.</p><p>Dificultad orientativa: <b>${derive(state).L>=en.level+8?'Fácil':derive(state).L>=en.level-2?'Adecuado':derive(state).L>=en.level-7?'Difícil':'Muy difícil'}</b> (solo nivel, no garantiza supervivencia).</p><button data-e13-close>Cerrar</button>`;
 if(typeof dialog.showModal==='function')dialog.showModal();else dialog.setAttribute('open','');
});
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest('[data-e13-close]')){const d=document.getElementById('e13EnemyDialog');if(d?.close)d.close();else d?.removeAttribute('open');}});


/* ===== E1.4: pociones recargables y tienda (guardado opcional en ui, v7 intacto) ===== */
const E14_POTION_COOLDOWN_MS=60000; // E1.4.3: 60 segundos por carga
const E14_POTION_CAP=2;
const E14_UPGRADES=[
 {level:1,hp:10,mp:8,price:0},
 {level:2,hp:18,mp:14,price:180},
 {level:3,hp:30,mp:24,price:600},
 {level:4,hp:50,mp:40,price:1800}
];
function e14Potions(s){
 const ui=uiPreferences(s);
 if(!ui.potions || typeof ui.potions!=='object' || Array.isArray(ui.potions))
   ui.potions={level:1,hp:E14_POTION_CAP,mp:E14_POTION_CAP,hpAt:Date.now(),mpAt:Date.now()};
 const p=ui.potions;
 p.level=Number.isInteger(p.level)?clamp(p.level,1,4):1;
 const now=Date.now();
 for(const k of ['hp','mp']){
   if(!Number.isFinite(p[k]))p[k]=E14_POTION_CAP;
   p[k]=clamp(Math.floor(p[k]),0,E14_POTION_CAP);
   const t=k+'At';
   if(!Number.isFinite(p[t])||p[t]>now)p[t]=now;
   if(p[k]>=E14_POTION_CAP){p[t]=now;continue;}
   const gained=Math.floor((now-p[t])/E14_POTION_COOLDOWN_MS);
   if(gained>0){p[k]=Math.min(E14_POTION_CAP,p[k]+gained);p[t]+=gained*E14_POTION_COOLDOWN_MS;if(p[k]===E14_POTION_CAP)p[t]=now;}
 }
 return p;
}
function e14Drink(k){
 if(!state||busy||managing||simulationLocked())return;
 const s=state,p=e14Potions(s),d=derive(s),hp=k==='hp';
 if(!p[k])return toast('Sin cargas. Se recargan automáticamente cada 1 minuto.');
 if(hp?s.hp>=d.hpMax:s.mp>=d.mpMax)return toast(hp?'Ya tenés toda la vida.':'Ya tenés todo el maná.');
 const amount=E14_UPGRADES[p.level-1][k],before=hp?s.hp:s.mp;
 if(hp)s.hp=Math.min(d.hpMax,s.hp+amount);else s.mp=Math.min(d.mpMax,s.mp+amount);
 if(p[k]===E14_POTION_CAP)p[k+'At']=Date.now();
 p[k]--;
 addLog((hp?'Poción de vida':'Poción de maná')+': recuperás '+Math.ceil((hp?s.hp:s.mp)-before)+' '+(hp?'HP':'MP')+'.');
 persist();updateLive();
}
function e14BuyUpgrade(){
 if(!state||busy||managing||simulationLocked())return;
 const p=e14Potions(state),next=E14_UPGRADES[p.level];
 if(!next)return toast('Tus frascos ya están al máximo.');
 if(state.gold<next.price)return toast('Necesitás '+next.price+' oro.');
 state.gold-=next.price;p.level++;
 addLog('Mejorás los frascos al nivel '+p.level+'.');
 persist();renderGame();
}
function e14AddDock(){
 const inner=document.querySelector('#combatDock .dockInner');
 if(!inner||document.getElementById('e14Potions'))return;
 const div=document.createElement('div');div.id='e14Potions';div.className='dockPotions';
 div.innerHTML='<button type="button" data-e14-drink="hp" id="e14HpPotion">🧪 Vida</button><button type="button" data-e14-drink="mp" id="e14MpPotion">🔷 Maná</button><span class="potionHint" id="e14PotionHint">2 cargas por frasco · +1 cada 1 min</span>';
 inner.append(div);measureDock();
}
function e14Update(){
 if(!state)return;
 const p=e14Potions(state),up=E14_UPGRADES[p.level-1];
 for(const k of ['hp','mp']){
   const b=document.getElementById(k==='hp'?'e14HpPotion':'e14MpPotion');if(!b)continue;
   const seconds=p[k]>=E14_POTION_CAP?0:Math.max(1,Math.ceil((E14_POTION_COOLDOWN_MS-(Date.now()-p[k+'At']))/1000));
   b.textContent=(k==='hp'?'🧪 Vida':'🔷 Maná')+' +'+up[k]+' ('+p[k]+'/'+E14_POTION_CAP+')'+(seconds?' · '+seconds+'s':'');
   b.disabled=busy||managing||p[k]===0||(k==='hp'?state.hp>=derive(state).hpMax:state.mp>=derive(state).mpMax);
 }
 const hint=document.getElementById('e14PotionHint');if(hint)hint.textContent='E1.4.3 · Frascos Nv '+p.level+' · 1 carga cada 60 s · mejorables en Tienda';
 const shop=document.getElementById('e14PotionShop');
 if(shop){const next=E14_UPGRADES[p.level];const b=shop.querySelector('button');
   shop.querySelector('.potionInfo').textContent='Nv '+p.level+' · Vida +'+up.hp+' HP · Maná +'+up.mp+' MP · 2 cargas por frasco.';
   if(b){b.textContent=next?'Mejorar a Nv '+next.level+' · '+next.price+' oro':'Mejora máxima';b.disabled=!next||busy||managing||state.gold<next.price;}
 }
}
function e14AddShop(){
 const shop=document.getElementById('page_shop');
 if(!shop||document.getElementById('e14PotionShop'))return;
 const panel=document.createElement('div');panel.id='e14PotionShop';panel.className='potionShop';
 panel.innerHTML='<b>🧪 Mejoras de frascos</b><p class="potionInfo muted"></p><p class="muted">Las pociones son permanentes, no consumen oro al usarlas y recuperan 1 carga cada 1 minuto.</p><button type="button" data-e14-upgrade>Mejorar frascos</button>';
 shop.append(panel);
}
const e14RenderDock=renderDock;
renderDock=function(){e14RenderDock();e14AddDock();e14Update();};
const e14RenderGame=renderGame;
renderGame=function(){e14RenderGame();e14AddDock();e14AddShop();e14Update();};
const e14UpdateLive=updateLive;
updateLive=function(){e14UpdateLive();e14Update();};
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
 const b=e.target.closest('[data-e14-drink],[data-e14-upgrade]');if(!b)return;
 e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
 if(b.hasAttribute('data-e14-upgrade'))e14BuyUpgrade();else e14Drink(b.dataset.e14Drink);
},true);



===== src/js/dev/008-dev-environment.js =====

"use strict";
/* Almacenamiento DEV separado. */
const DEV_KIND="argentum-dev";
const MODE_STORAGE={normal:{db:"argentum-idle",key:"argentum-idle-save-v1"},dev:{db:"argentum-idle-dev",key:"argentum-idle-dev-save-v1"}};
let modeTransition=false;
let normalContext=null;
let devContext=null;
const normalDecodeRoster=decodeRoster;
const normalNewState=newState;
const normalMakeRosterRecord=makeRosterRecord;
const normalRenderGame=renderGame;
const normalShowCharacters=showCharacters;
const normalEnemySwing=enemySwing;
const normalImportJSON=importJSON;
const normalChangeRoster=changeRoster;
const normalRunRosterTests=runRosterTests;
function taggedDev(raw){
  return !!(raw && (raw.kind===DEV_KIND || raw.environment==="dev" || raw.state?.devEnvironment===true || (Array.isArray(raw.characters)&&raw.characters.some(e=>e?.state?.devEnvironment===true))));
}
function decodeForMode(raw,mode){
  if(mode==="normal"){
    if(taggedDev(raw))return null;
    return normalDecodeRoster(raw);
  }
  // DEV no acepta archivos normales, ni siquiera como fuente de migración.
  if(!raw || raw.kind!==DEV_KIND || raw.version!==7 || !Array.isArray(raw.characters) || raw.characters.some(e=>e?.state?.devEnvironment!==true))return null;
  return normalDecodeRoster(raw);
}
decodeRoster=function(raw){return decodeForMode(raw,gameMode);};
newState=function(){
  const s=normalNewState();
  if(gameMode==="dev"){s.devEnvironment=true;s.dev={invulnerable:false};}
  return s;
};
makeRosterRecord=function(){
  const rec=normalMakeRosterRecord();
  if(gameMode==="dev")rec.kind=DEV_KIND;
  return rec;
};
enemySwing=function(d,en){
  const protect=gameMode==="dev"&&state?.devEnvironment===true&&state.dev?.invulnerable===true;
  const hp=state.hp;
  normalEnemySwing(d,en);
  if(protect)state.hp=hp;
};
function clampDevResources(s){
  const d=derive(s);
  s.hp=clamp(s.hp,0,d.hpMax);s.mp=clamp(s.mp,0,d.mpMax);
}
function requiredSkillXp(level){return 10*Math.pow(level-1,2);}
function devSetLevel(s,level){s.xp=xpFor(level);clampDevResources(s);}
function devSetSkill(s,key,level){
  if(key==="weapon")s.skill.xp=xpFor(level);
  else if(SKILLS.some(k=>k.key===key))s.skills[key].xp=requiredSkillXp(level);
  else throw Error("Habilidad inexistente.");
  clampDevResources(s);
}
function prepareDevDungeon(s,id){
  const dg=dungeonById(id);if(!dg)throw Error("Dungeon inexistente.");
  s.xp=Math.max(s.xp,xpFor(dg.minLevel));
  if(dg.navReq)s.skills.navegacion.xp=Math.max(s.skills.navegacion.xp,requiredSkillXp(dg.navReq));
  clampDevResources(s);
}
function stopContextActivities(s){if(s){if(s===state)e210StopFishing();freezeCharacter(s);}}
function devFixture(){
  const previous=draft;
  try{draft={name:"Prueba aislada",race:"humano",cls:"guerrero"};return normalNewState();}
  finally{draft=previous;}
}
function refreshModeChrome(){
  document.body.classList.toggle("devEnvironment",gameMode==="dev");
  let toggle=document.getElementById("devModeToggle");
  if(!toggle){toggle=document.createElement("button");toggle.id="devModeToggle";toggle.type="button";toggle.dataset.devAction="switchMode";document.querySelector("header").append(toggle);}
  toggle.textContent=gameMode==="dev"?"Salir de DEV → personajes normales":"Entrar al entorno DEV";
  toggle.disabled=modeTransition;
  let banner=document.getElementById("devModeBanner");
  if(gameMode==="dev"){
    if(!banner){banner=document.createElement("div");banner.id="devModeBanner";document.querySelector("header").after(banner);}
    banner.textContent="🛠 ENTORNO DEV · Personajes de prueba · Sin acceso a recursos normales"+(state?.dev?.invulnerable?" · INVULNERABILIDAD ACTIVA":"");
  }else banner?.remove();
}
function appendDevPanel(){
  document.getElementById("devAdminPanel")?.remove();
  document.getElementById("devIsolationTests")?.remove();
  refreshModeChrome();
  if(gameMode!=="dev")return;
  const tests=document.createElement("details");tests.id="devIsolationTests";tests.className="panel";

  const selection=document.getElementById("characterScreen");
  const host=state&&ready?document.getElementById("gameScreen"):selection.querySelector(".panel");
  if(state&&ready&&state.devEnvironment===true){
    const s=state,d=derive(s);
    const panel=document.createElement("details");panel.id="devAdminPanel";panel.className="panel";panel.open=true;
    panel.innerHTML=`<summary>🛠 Preparación DEV · ${esc(s.name)}</summary>
      <p class="muted">Herramienta local, no un sistema seguro de permisos online. Cada modificación pausa las actividades. No altera personajes normales.</p>
      <div class="devFields"><label>Nivel del personaje<select id="devLevelPreset">${[1,10,20,35,50,100].map(n=>`<option value="${n}" ${n===d.L?"selected":""}>${n}</option>`).join("")}<option value="custom" ${![1,10,20,35,50,100].includes(d.L)?"selected":""}>Personalizado</option></select></label><label>Personalizado (1–1000)<input id="devCustomLevel" type="number" min="1" max="1000" step="1" value="${Math.min(1000,d.L)}"></label><button data-dev-action="level">Establecer nivel</button></div>
      <div class="devFields"><label>Cantidad de oro<input id="devGold" type="number" min="0" max="1000000000000" step="1" value="10000"></label><button data-dev-action="goldSet">Establecer oro</button><button data-dev-action="goldAdd">Agregar oro</button></div>
      <div class="devFields"><label>Habilidad<select id="devSkill"><option value="weapon">Combate con Armas</option>${SKILLS.map(k=>`<option value="${k.key}">${esc(k.name)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label><label>Nivel (1–1000)<input id="devSkillLevel" type="number" min="1" max="1000" step="1" value="25"></label><button data-dev-action="skill">Establecer habilidad</button></div>
      <div class="actions"><button data-dev-action="restore">Recuperar HP y MP</button><button data-dev-action="spells">Aprender todos los hechizos</button><button data-dev-action="equipment">Obtener equipo de tienda</button></div>
      <p class="muted">El equipo se agrega sin equiparlo; los requisitos y bonificaciones reales se mantienen. Preparar un dungeon sube únicamente los requisitos que falten.</p>
      <div class="devFields"><label>Dungeon<select id="devDungeon">${DUNGEONS.map(dg=>`<option value="${dg.id}">${esc(dg.name)}</option>`).join("")}</select></label><button data-dev-action="dungeon">Preparar requisitos y entrar</button></div>
      <div class="actions"><button data-dev-action="invulnerable" aria-pressed="${!!s.dev?.invulnerable}">Invulnerabilidad: ${s.dev?.invulnerable?"ACTIVA":"desactivada"}</button><button class="danger" data-dev-action="reset">Reiniciar personaje DEV</button><button class="danger" data-act="reset">Eliminar personaje DEV</button><button data-act="export">Exportar JSON DEV</button></div>
      <p class="muted">Las asignaciones de nivel usan XP acumulada real. Al cambiar máximos se limita HP/MP sin curar gratis; usá Recuperar para llenarlos.</p>`;
    host.prepend(panel);
  }
  host.append(tests);
  if(!state){
    const title=selection.querySelector("h2");
    if(title?.firstChild)title.firstChild.textContent="Personajes DEV · ";
    const p=selection.querySelector("p.muted");if(p)p.textContent="Espacios de prueba separados de los cinco personajes normales. Solo el DEV activo progresa. Salir de DEV pausa todas sus actividades.";
  }
}
renderGame=function(){normalRenderGame();appendDevPanel();};
showCharacters=function(){normalShowCharacters();appendDevPanel();};
importJSON=async function(file){
  if(modeTransition||busy||managing)return;
  try{
    const raw=JSON.parse(await file.text());
    if(!decodeForMode(raw,gameMode))throw Error(gameMode==="dev"?"DEV solo admite respaldos argentum-dev válidos. No se aceptan personajes normales.":"El modo normal no admite partidas DEV ni formatos inválidos.");
    await normalImportJSON(file);
    refreshModeChrome();
  }catch(e){toast(e.message||"No se pudo validar el archivo.");}
};
changeRoster=async function(action,id){
  if(modeTransition)return;
  await normalChangeRoster(action,id);
  refreshModeChrome();
};
async function loadDevRecord(){
  const records=[];let corrupt=false;
  try{const r=await idbGet();if(r)records.push(r);}catch(e){/* localStorage puede conservar una copia válida */}
  try{const text=localStorage.getItem(MODE_STORAGE.dev.key);if(text)records.push(JSON.parse(text));}catch(e){corrupt=true;}
  records.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.savedAt)||0)-(Number(a.savedAt)||0)||(Number(b.revision)||0)-(Number(a.revision)||0));
  for(const raw of records){const decoded=decodeForMode(raw,"dev");if(decoded)return decoded;}
  if(records.length||corrupt)throw Error("Guardado DEV corrupto: no se sobrescribió ni se utilizó un guardado normal. Recuperá un respaldo DEV antes de reintentar.");
  return {version:7,kind:DEV_KIND,revision:0,savedAt:0,activeId:null,characters:[]};
}
async function switchEnvironment(){
  if(modeTransition||busy||managing||!storageReady)return;
  if(!confirm(gameMode==="normal"?"Entrar a DEV pausará tu personaje normal y lo guardará. No generará recompensas mientras estés en DEV. ¿Continuar?":"Salir de DEV pausará sus personajes y volverá a la selección normal. ¿Continuar?"))return;
  // Termina primero el tiempo real del contexto de origen.
  if(state&&ready)syncRuntime();
  if(busy)return toast("Esperá a que termine el cálculo offline y volvé a cambiar de modo.");
  modeTransition=true;managing=true;
  const originMode=gameMode;
  const originalDb=dbP;
  let originSnapshot=null;
  try{
    if(state)stopContextActivities(state);
    roster.characters.forEach(e=>stopContextActivities(e.state));
    roster.activeId=null;state=null;ready=false;runtimeAt=0;runtimeRemainder=0;
    if(!await saveRoster())throw Error("No se pudo guardar el contexto de origen; no se cambió de modo.");
    await writeQueue;
    originSnapshot=copyJSON(roster);
    const context={roster:originSnapshot,db:dbP};
    if(originMode==="normal")normalContext=context;else devContext=context;
    gameMode=originMode==="normal"?"dev":"normal";
    LS_KEY=MODE_STORAGE[gameMode].key;
    dbP=(gameMode==="normal"?normalContext:devContext)?.db||null;
    if(gameMode==="dev"){
      roster=await loadDevRecord();
      // Entrar nunca simula tiempo desde el último uso del laboratorio.
      roster.characters.forEach(e=>stopContextActivities(e.state));roster.activeId=null;
    }else{
      if(!normalContext)throw Error("No hay contexto normal válido; no se creó un reemplazo.");
      roster=copyJSON(normalContext.roster);
    }
    state=null;ready=false;runtimeAt=0;runtimeRemainder=0;
    if(!await saveRoster())throw Error("No se pudo guardar el contexto de destino.");
    showCharacters();refreshModeChrome();
    toast(gameMode==="dev"?"Entorno DEV abierto. Elegí o creá un personaje de prueba.":"Personajes normales conservados. Todas las actividades permanecen pausadas.");
  }catch(e){
    // Un error DEV nunca se resuelve copiando datos normales a la base DEV.
    gameMode=originMode;LS_KEY=MODE_STORAGE[originMode].key;
    const context=originMode==="normal"?normalContext:devContext;
    if(originSnapshot){roster=copyJSON(originSnapshot);dbP=context?.db||originalDb;}
    else {dbP=originalDb;roster.characters.forEach(e=>stopContextActivities(e.state));roster.activeId=null;}
    state=null;ready=false;runtimeAt=0;runtimeRemainder=0;
    showCharacters();refreshModeChrome();toast(e.message||"No se pudo cambiar de entorno.");
  }finally{managing=false;modeTransition=false;refreshModeChrome();}
}
function readDevInteger(id,min,max){
  const value=Number(document.getElementById(id)?.value);
  if(!Number.isSafeInteger(value)||value<min||value>max)throw Error(`Ingresá un entero entre ${min} y ${max}.`);
  return value;
}
async function devAdminAction(action){
  if(gameMode!=="dev"||!state?.devEnvironment||!ready||modeTransition||busy||managing||!storageReady)return;
  syncRuntime();if(busy)return;
  const snapshot=copyJSON(state);const activeId=roster.activeId;
  managing=true;
  try{
    stopContextActivities(state);runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;
    const s=state;
    if(action==="level"){
      const preset=document.getElementById("devLevelPreset").value;
      const n=preset==="custom"?readDevInteger("devCustomLevel",1,1000):Number(preset);
      if(!Number.isSafeInteger(n)||n<1||n>1000)throw Error("Nivel inválido.");
      devSetLevel(s,n);
    }else if(action==="goldSet"||action==="goldAdd"){
      const amount=readDevInteger("devGold",0,1000000000000);
      const result=action==="goldSet"?amount:s.gold+amount;
      if(!Number.isSafeInteger(result))throw Error("Cantidad fuera del rango seguro.");s.gold=result;
    }else if(action==="skill")devSetSkill(s,document.getElementById("devSkill").value,readDevInteger("devSkillLevel",1,1000));
    else if(action==="restore"){const d=derive(s);s.hp=d.hpMax;s.mp=d.mpMax;}
    else if(action==="spells")SPELLS.forEach(sp=>s.spells[sp.id]=true);
    else if(action==="equipment")EQUIP.forEach(it=>s.owned[it.id]=true);
    else if(action==="dungeon"){
      const id=document.getElementById("devDungeon").value;prepareDevDungeon(s,id);selectDungeon(id);
    }else if(action==="invulnerable"){
      s.dev=s.dev||{};s.dev.invulnerable=!s.dev.invulnerable;
    }else if(action==="reset"){
      if(!confirm(`¿Reiniciar a «${s.name}» en DEV? Perderá todo su progreso de prueba. Los normales no cambian.`))return;
      const previous=draft;
      try{draft={name:s.name,race:s.race,cls:s.cls};state=newState();roster.characters.find(e=>e.id===activeId).state=state;}
      finally{draft=previous;}
    }else return;
    addLog("DEV: preparación aplicada; actividades pausadas.");
    if(!await saveRoster())throw Error("No se pudo guardar la preparación DEV.");
  }catch(e){
    state=snapshot;roster.characters.find(e=>e.id===activeId).state=state;
    runtimeAt=Date.now();toast(e.message||"Error de preparación DEV.");
  }finally{managing=false;renderGame();refreshModeChrome();}
}
function runDevTests(){
  const lines=[];
  const test=(name,fn)=>{try{if(!fn())throw Error("resultado inesperado");lines.push("✓ "+name);}catch(e){lines.push("✗ "+name+": "+e.message);}};
  const fixtureRoster=()=>({version:7,revision:1,savedAt:1000,activeId:"test-a",characters:[{id:"test-a",state:devFixture()}]});
  const devRoster=()=>{const r=fixtureRoster();r.kind=DEV_KIND;r.characters[0].state.devEnvironment=true;return r;};
  test("Bases y claves normal/DEV distintas",()=>MODE_STORAGE.normal.db!==MODE_STORAGE.dev.db&&MODE_STORAGE.normal.key!==MODE_STORAGE.dev.key);
  test("JSON normal rechazado por DEV",()=>!decodeForMode(fixtureRoster(),"dev"));
  test("JSON DEV rechazado por normal",()=>!decodeForMode(devRoster(),"normal"));
  test("Quitar envoltorio DEV no oculta la marca de personaje",()=>{const r=devRoster();delete r.kind;return !decodeForMode(r,"normal");});
  test("Envoltorio DEV sin marca de personaje rechazado",()=>{const r=fixtureRoster();r.kind=DEV_KIND;return !decodeForMode(r,"dev");});
  test("DEV corrupto no es aceptado ni reemplazado por normal",()=>{const r=devRoster();r.characters[0].state.gold=-1;return !decodeForMode(r,"dev")&&!decodeForMode(fixtureRoster(),"dev");});
  test("XP y nivel reales: 1, 10, 20, 35, 50, 100, 173",()=>[1,10,20,35,50,100,173].every(n=>{const s=devFixture();devSetLevel(s,n);return s.xp===xpFor(n)&&derive(s).L===n;}));
  test("XP de habilidades reales",()=>SKILLS.every(k=>{const s=devFixture();devSetSkill(s,k.key,25);return s.skills[k.key].xp===5760&&skl(s,k.key)===25;}));
  test("Combate con Armas usa su propia curva",()=>{const s=devFixture();devSetSkill(s,"weapon",50);return s.skill.xp===xpFor(50)&&derive(s).sk===50;});
  test("Bajar nivel limita HP/MP al máximo derivado",()=>{const s=devFixture();s.xp=xpFor(100);s.hp=99999;s.mp=99999;devSetLevel(s,1);const d=derive(s);return s.hp===d.hpMax&&s.mp===d.mpMax;});
  test("Preparación Pirata respeta personaje 35 y Navegación 25",()=>{const s=devFixture();prepareDevDungeon(s,"dungeon_pirata");return derive(s).L===35&&skl(s,"navegacion")===25&&dungeonOk(dungeonById("dungeon_pirata"),s);});
  test("Snapshot normal no comparte referencias con DEV",()=>{const a=fixtureRoster(),before=JSON.stringify(a),b=copyJSON(a);b.characters[0].state.gold+=999;return JSON.stringify(a)===before;});
  test("Cambio de entorno detiene combate",()=>{const s=devFixture();s.combat.active=true;stopContextActivities(s);return !s.combat.active&&s.savedAt===0;});
  test("Cambio de entorno pausa navegación sin premios",()=>{const s=devFixture();s.nav={id:"costa",remainingMs:3600000,totalMs:3600000,elapsedMs:0,paused:false,legacy:false,earnedGold:0,earnedXp:0};const gold=s.gold,xp=s.skills.navegacion.xp;stopContextActivities(s);advanceNav(s,86400000);return s.nav.paused&&s.nav.remainingMs===3600000&&s.gold===gold&&s.skills.navegacion.xp===xp;});
  test("Serialización DEV conserva personajes y privilegio solo en DEV",()=>{const r=devRoster();r.characters[0].state.dev={invulnerable:true};const t=decodeForMode(copyJSON(r),"dev");return t.characters.length===1&&t.characters[0].state.dev.invulnerable&&!decodeForMode(r,"normal");});
  test("Volver a selección no reactiva actividad",()=>{const r=devRoster();r.characters.forEach(e=>stopContextActivities(e.state));r.activeId=null;const t=decodeForMode(r,"dev");return t.activeId===null&&!t.characters[0].state.combat.active;});
  const out=document.getElementById("devTestOutput");
  if(out)out.textContent=lines.join("\n")+"\n\n"+lines.filter(x=>x.startsWith("✓")).length+" / "+lines.length+" aprobadas.\nEstas pruebas puras solo se ejecutan al pulsar el botón. No prueban escrituras reales de IndexedDB, recargas, archivos, eventos ni múltiples pestañas.";
}
runRosterTests=function(){
  const previous=decodeRoster;
  try{decodeRoster=normalDecodeRoster;normalRunRosterTests();}
  finally{decodeRoster=previous;}
};
document.addEventListener("click",async e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest("[data-dev-action]");if(!b)return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  if(b.disabled||busy||managing||modeTransition||!storageReady)return;
  const action=b.dataset.devAction;
  if(action==="switchMode")return switchEnvironment();
  if(gameMode!=="dev")return;
  if(action==="tests")return runDevTests();
  return devAdminAction(action);
},true);
refreshModeChrome();


===== src/js/dev/010-dev-progression.js =====

"use strict";
/* DEV: checkpoints de estado, RNG y contadores; un step por segundo. */
const XP_OPTIONS=[1,2,5,10,50,100,365,1000];
const SIM_DURATIONS=[[3600,"1 hora"],[28800,"8 horas"],[86400,"24 horas"],[604800,"7 días"],[2592000,"30 días"],[31536000,"365 días"]];
let simTimer=null,simEpoch=0,simLastWall=0,simUIAt=0,simCheckpointAt=0,simSaving=false;
let simulationExecuting=false;
let simTestContext=false;
const knownSimulations=new WeakSet();
const baseActivityFactor=xpActivityFactor;
const baseSyncRuntime=syncRuntime;
const baseTick=tick;
const basePersist=persist;
const baseRecord=makeRosterRecord;
const baseAppendDev=appendDevPanel;
const baseDevAdmin=devAdminAction;
const baseSwitchEnvironment=switchEnvironment;
const baseChangeRoster=changeRoster;
const baseImportProgression=importJSON;
const baseExportProgression=exportJSON;
const baseSimDeath=onDeath;
const baseSaveProgression=saveRoster;
function devProgress(s=state){
  if(!s.dev)s.dev={invulnerable:false};
  if(!s.dev.progress)s.dev.progress={xpMultiplier:1,xpScope:"both",history:[],simulation:null};
  const p=s.dev.progress;
  if(!XP_OPTIONS.includes(p.xpMultiplier))p.xpMultiplier=1;
  if(!["both","character","skills"].includes(p.xpScope))p.xpScope="both";
  if(!Array.isArray(p.history))p.history=[];
  return p;
}
function currentSimulation(){return gameMode==="dev"&&state?.devEnvironment?state.dev?.progress?.simulation:null;}
function simulationLocked(){const r=currentSimulation();return !!r&&["running","paused","recovered"].includes(r.status);}
function multiplierFor(s,kind){
  if(gameMode!=="dev"||!s?.devEnvironment)return 1;
  const p=devProgress(s),r=p.simulation;
  if(simulationExecuting||(r&&["running","paused","recovered"].includes(r.status)))return 1;
  return p.xpScope==="both"||p.xpScope===kind?p.xpMultiplier:1;
}
xpActivityFactor=function(s,kind){return multiplierFor(s,kind);};
function seedHash(text){let h=2166136261;for(const c of String(text)){h^=c.charCodeAt(0);h=Math.imul(h,16777619);}return h>>>0;}
function seededNext(r){r.rngState=(r.rngState+0x6D2B79F5)>>>0;let t=r.rngState;t=Math.imul(t^(t>>>15),t|1);t^=t+Math.imul(t^(t>>>7),t|61);return ((t^(t>>>14))>>>0)/4294967296;}
function sumKills(s){return Object.values(s.defeated).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);}
function skillSnapshot(s){return {weapon:s.skill.xp,...Object.fromEntries(SKILLS.map(k=>[k.key,s.skills[k.key].xp]))};}
function valuesSnapshot(s){return {level:levelFromXp(s.xp),xp:s.xp,gold:s.gold,kills:sumKills(s),skills:skillSnapshot(s)};}
function activityReason(s){if(s.combat.active)return "Combate activo";if(navActive(s))return "Navegando";if(s.restLeft>0)return "Descanso manual en curso";return "Personaje detenido";}
function frameFlags(s){return {combatActive:!!s.combat.active,navPaused:!!s.nav?.paused};}
function restoreFrame(s,r){
  if(!r.frame)return;
  s.combat.active=!!r.frame.combatActive;
  if(navHas(s))s.nav.paused=!!r.frame.navPaused;
  // Nunca se permiten navegación y combate simultáneos.
  if(navActive(s))s.combat.active=false;
}
function validSimulation(r){return !!r&&typeof r==="object"&&["running","paused","recovered","finished","stopped"].includes(r.status)&&Number.isSafeInteger(r.processed)&&Number.isSafeInteger(r.target)&&r.target>0&&r.processed>=0&&r.processed<=r.target&&Number.isInteger(r.rngState)&&r.initial&&r.initial.skills&&Number.isFinite(r.realMs)&&Number.isFinite(r.combatSec)&&Number.isFinite(r.deaths)&&[10,100,365].includes(r.speed);}
function updateSimFinal(r){r.final=valuesSnapshot(state);if(r.status==="running"||!r.frame)r.frame=frameFlags(state);}
function addHistory(r){
  const p=devProgress();const record=copyJSON(r);record.final=valuesSnapshot(state);
  const i=p.history.findIndex(x=>x.id===record.id);
  if(i>=0)p.history[i]=record;else p.history.unshift(record);
  // Solo informes, nunca personajes ni objetos. Se pueden exportar en JSON.
  if(p.history.length>50)p.history.length=50;
}
function durationText(sec){sec=Math.max(0,Math.floor(sec));const days=Math.floor(sec/86400);sec%=86400;const h=Math.floor(sec/3600),m=Math.floor(sec%3600/60);return (days?days+" d ":"")+h+" h "+m+" min "+sec%60+" s";}
function progressionStep(s,r){
  const combat=s.combat.active&&!navActive(s)&&s.restLeft===0;
  simulationExecuting=true;
  try{step();}finally{simulationExecuting=false;}
  r.processed++;
  if(combat)r.combatSec++;
  if(combat&&!s.combat.active&&r.lastDeathAt!==r.processed-1)r.reason="Combate detenido";
}
onDeath=function(en){
  const r=currentSimulation();
  if(simulationExecuting&&r){r.deaths++;r.lastDeathAt=r.processed;r.reason="Derrota frente a "+en.name+"; sin reinicio automático";}
  baseSimDeath(en);
};
function withSimulationRng(r,fn){const previous=rng;rng=()=>seededNext(r);try{return fn();}finally{rng=previous;}}
function simulationBatch(r,count){
  const oldOff=OFF.active;OFF.active=true;
  try{withSimulationRng(r,()=>{for(let i=0;i<count;i++)progressionStep(state,r);});}
  finally{OFF.active=oldOff;}
}
syncRuntime=function(){if(simulationLocked()){runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;return;}return baseSyncRuntime();};
tick=function(){if(simulationLocked()){runtimeAt=Date.now();return;}return baseTick();};
makeRosterRecord=function(){
  const r=currentSimulation();
  if(r&&simulationLocked())updateSimFinal(r);
  return baseRecord();
};
saveRoster=function(){
  const r=currentSimulation();if(r&&simulationLocked())updateSimFinal(r);
  return baseSaveProgression();
};
persist=function(){if(fishingFinishDepth)return Promise.resolve(true);if(simulationLocked()){runtimeAt=Date.now();return saveRoster();}return basePersist();};
async function simulationCheckpoint(){
  if(simSaving)return false;
  simSaving=true;
  try{
    const ok=await saveRoster();
    const r=currentSimulation();
    if(!ok&&r&&r.status==="running"){r.status="paused";r.reason="Pausa: fallo de guardado; exportá DEV antes de cerrar";simEpoch++;clearTimeout(simTimer);}
    return ok;
  }finally{simSaving=false;simLastWall=performance.now();}
}
function beginPump(){clearTimeout(simTimer);const token=++simEpoch;simLastWall=performance.now();simCheckpointAt=simLastWall;simUIAt=0;simTimer=setTimeout(()=>pumpSimulation(token),0);}
async function pumpSimulation(token){
  const r=currentSimulation();
  if(token!==simEpoch||!r||r.status!=="running"||gameMode!=="dev")return;
  if(simSaving){simTimer=setTimeout(()=>pumpSimulation(token),20);return;}
  const now=performance.now(),elapsed=Math.max(0,now-simLastWall);simLastWall=now;
  // Tiempo real incluye cálculo y espera, no tiempo pausado ni cierre de pestaña.
  r.realMs+=elapsed;
  r.credit=(Number(r.credit)||0)+elapsed/1000*r.speed;
  if(r.maximum)r.credit=0;
  const budget=r.maximum?r.target-r.processed:Math.min(Math.floor(r.credit),r.target-r.processed);
  const start=performance.now();let count=0;
  try{
    const oldOff=OFF.active;OFF.active=true;
    try{withSimulationRng(r,()=>{
      while(count<budget&&count<1000&&performance.now()-start<8){progressionStep(state,r);count++;}
    });}finally{OFF.active=oldOff;}
    if(!r.maximum)r.credit=Math.max(0,r.credit-count);
    updateSimFinal(r);
    if(r.processed>=r.target){await finishSimulation("finished","Duración objetivo completada");return;}
    if(now-simUIAt>=200){simUIAt=now;updateLive();updateInv();updateSimulationUI();}
    if(now-simCheckpointAt>=5000){simCheckpointAt=now;await simulationCheckpoint();}
  }catch(e){r.status="paused";r.reason="Error del motor: "+e.message;simEpoch++;await simulationCheckpoint();updateSimulationUI();return;}
  if(token===simEpoch&&r.status==="running")simTimer=setTimeout(()=>pumpSimulation(token),r.maximum?0:10);
}
async function finishSimulation(status,reason){
  const r=currentSimulation();if(!r)return;
  simEpoch++;clearTimeout(simTimer);r.status=status;r.endReason=reason;
  updateSimFinal(r);addHistory(r);
  freezeCharacter(state);runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;
  await simulationCheckpoint();renderGame();
}
async function pauseSimulation(reason="Pausa solicitada"){
  const r=currentSimulation();if(!r||!simulationLocked())return;
  if(r.status==="running"){r.realMs+=Math.max(0,performance.now()-simLastWall);r.frame=frameFlags(state);}
  simEpoch++;clearTimeout(simTimer);r.status="paused";r.pauseReason=reason;
  r.credit=0;runtimeAt=Date.now();await simulationCheckpoint();updateSimulationUI();
}
async function startSimulation(){
  if(gameMode!=="dev"||!state?.devEnvironment||simulationLocked()||busy||managing||simSaving)return;
  syncRuntime();if(busy)return;
  const speed=Number(document.getElementById("simSpeed").value),choice=document.getElementById("simDuration").value;
  const custom=Number(document.getElementById("simCustom").value);
  const target=choice==="custom"?Math.round(custom*3600):Number(choice);
  if(![10,100,365].includes(speed)||!Number.isSafeInteger(target)||target<1||target>315360000)return toast("Duración inválida: entre 1 segundo y 3650 días.");
  const seed=document.getElementById("simSeed").value||"argentum-1",p=devProgress();
  const r={id:newCharacterId(),status:"paused",name:state.name,cls:state.cls,race:state.race,seed,rngState:seedHash(seed),target,processed:0,combatSec:0,realMs:0,deaths:0,speed,maximum:document.getElementById("simMaximum").checked,initial:valuesSnapshot(state),frame:frameFlags(state),reason:activityReason(state),lastDeathAt:-1,credit:0,invulnerableAtStart:!!state.dev.invulnerable,createdAt:Date.now()};
    p.simulation=r;knownSimulations.add(r);runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;updateSimFinal(r);
  if(!await simulationCheckpoint())return toast("No se inició: no pudo guardarse el punto inicial DEV.");
  r.status="running";renderGame();beginPump();
}
async function resumeSimulation(){
  const r=currentSimulation();if(!r||!["paused","recovered"].includes(r.status)||simSaving)return;
  restoreFrame(state,r);r.credit=0;r.status="paused";
  if(!await simulationCheckpoint())return;
  r.status="running";r.pauseReason=null;runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;renderGame();beginPump();
}
function recoverSimulation(){
  if(gameMode!=="dev"||!state?.devEnvironment)return;
  const p=devProgress(),r=p.simulation;if(!r)return;
  if(!validSimulation(r)){p.simulation=null;freezeCharacter(state);toast("Checkpoint de simulación inválido: no se continuó la prueba.");return;}
  if(!knownSimulations.has(r)){
    if(r.status==="running"){r.status="recovered";r.credit=0;r.pauseReason="Recuperada desde el checkpoint; reanudación manual";}
    knownSimulations.add(r);
  }
}
function skillChanges(r,final){
  if(!r?.initial?.skills||!final?.skills)return "—";
  return Object.keys(r.initial.skills).map(k=>{
    const a=r.initial.skills[k],b=final.skills[k]??a,weapon=k==="weapon";
    const name=weapon?"Combate con Armas":SKILLS.find(x=>x.key===k)?.name||k;
    const level=weapon?levelFromXp:skillLvl;
    return `${name}: ${level(a)} → ${level(b)} (+${fmt(b-a)} XP)`;
  }).join(" · ");
}
function simulationSummary(r){
  const f=r.final||r.initial,sec=r.processed||0,rate=n=>sec?fmt(n*3600/sec):"0";
  return `<b>${esc(r.name||"Prueba")} · ${esc(classById(r.cls)?.name||r.cls||"")}</b><br>Estado: ${esc(r.status)} · Semilla: ${esc(r.seed)}${r.invulnerableAtStart?" · Invulnerabilidad DEV":""}<br>Tiempo total: ${durationText(sec)} / ${durationText(r.target)} · Combate: ${durationText(r.combatSec)}<br>Nivel ${r.initial.level} → ${f.level} · XP ${fmt(r.initial.xp)} → ${fmt(f.xp)} (+${fmt(f.xp-r.initial.xp)}) · Oro ${fmt(r.initial.gold)} → ${fmt(f.gold)} (+${fmt(f.gold-r.initial.gold)})<br>${rate(f.xp-r.initial.xp)} XP/h · ${rate(f.gold-r.initial.gold)} oro/h de tiempo total simulado · Bajas ${f.kills-r.initial.kills} · Muertes ${r.deaths}<br>Tiempo real activo: ${durationText(r.realMs/1000)} · ${esc(r.endReason||r.pauseReason||"En curso")}<br>${esc(r.reason||"")}<br><small>${esc(skillChanges(r,f))}</small>`;
}
function updateSimulationUI(){
  const panel=document.getElementById("devProgressionPanel");if(!panel||!state)return;
  const p=devProgress(),r=p.simulation,locked=simulationLocked();
  const multi=document.getElementById("devXpMultiplier"),scope=document.getElementById("devXpScope");
  if(multi)multi.disabled=locked;if(scope)scope.disabled=locked;
  const note=document.getElementById("simClockNote");
  if(note)note.textContent=locked?"RELOJ DEV EXCLUSIVO · XP ×1 obligatoria · reloj normal suspendido. La pausa también congela todos los temporizadores.":`Velocidad normal ×1 / XP ×${p.xpMultiplier} · ${p.xpScope==="both"?"personaje y habilidades":p.xpScope==="skills"?"habilidades":"personaje"}. Aplicación inmediata; oro y drops originales.`;
  const output=document.getElementById("simLive");
  if(output)output.innerHTML=r?`<div class="row"><b>${esc(r.status)}</b><span>${(r.processed/r.target*100).toFixed(2)}%</span></div><div class="bar xp"><div style="width:${r.processed/r.target*100}%"></div></div><div class="simMetrics"><div><small>Tiempo total procesado</small>${durationText(r.processed)}</div><div><small>Tiempo en combate</small>${durationText(r.combatSec)}</div><div><small>Tiempo fuera de combate</small>${durationText(r.processed-r.combatSec)}</div><div><small>Tiempo real activo</small>${durationText(r.realMs/1000)}</div></div><div>${simulationSummary({...r,final:valuesSnapshot(state)})}</div><p class="muted">Estado actual: ${esc(activityReason(state))}. Las tasas se dividen por TODO el tiempo simulado, incluido el tiempo detenido. ${r.pauseReason?esc(r.pauseReason):""}</p>`:"Todavía no hay una simulación.";
  for(const [id,enabled] of [["simStart",!locked],["simPause",r?.status==="running"],["simResume",r&&["paused","recovered"].includes(r.status)],["simStop",locked]]){
    const b=document.getElementById(id);if(b)b.disabled=!enabled||simSaving;
  }
  panel.querySelectorAll("[data-sim-config]").forEach(el=>el.disabled=locked);
}
function appendProgressionPanel(){
  document.getElementById("devProgressionPanel")?.remove();
  if(gameMode!=="dev"||!state?.devEnvironment||!ready)return;
  recoverSimulation();const p=devProgress();
  const panel=document.createElement("details");panel.id="devProgressionPanel";panel.open=true;

No lanza hechizos automáticamente. Mientras esté en curso o pausada, la XP queda en ×1 y el reloj normal se congela.</p><div class="devFields"><label>Velocidad objetivo<select id="simSpeed" data-sim-config><option value="10">×10</option><option value="100">×100</option><option value="365" selected>×365</option></select></label><label>Duración objetivo<select id="simDuration" data-sim-config>${SIM_DURATIONS.map(([s,n])=>`<option value="${s}">${n}</option>`).join("")}<option value="custom">Personalizada</option></select></label><label>Horas personalizadas<input id="simCustom" data-sim-config type="number" min="0.000278" max="87600" step="any" value="1"></label><label>Semilla<input id="simSeed" data-sim-config type="text" maxlength="120" value="argentum-1"></label></div><label class="muted"><input id="simMaximum" data-sim-config type="checkbox"> Máximo rendimiento por lotes, sin limitar la velocidad al reloj real</label><p class="muted">×365 estricto procesa 365 segundos por segundo real: un año requiere aproximadamente un día. Para ensayos largos más rápidos, activá máximo rendimiento; también ejecuta TODOS los pasos reales, sin estimaciones. La semilla solo es comparable con el mismo estado inicial y las mismas acciones manuales.</p><p class="muted">Prepará primero el personaje e iniciá combate o navegación antes de simular.   document.getElementById("devAdminPanel")?.after(panel);
  updateSimulationUI();

document.addEventListener("change",async e=>{
  if(!["devXpMultiplier","devXpScope"].includes(e.target.id))return;
  if(gameMode!=="dev"||!state?.devEnvironment||simulationLocked()||busy||managing)return;
