document.addEventListener("click",async e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest("[data-sim-action]");
  if(b){
    e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    if(b.disabled||gameMode!=="dev"||!state?.devEnvironment||busy||managing||simSaving)return;
    const a=b.dataset.simAction;
    if(a==="start")return startSimulation();
    if(a==="pause")return pauseSimulation();
    if(a==="resume")return resumeSimulation();
    if(a==="stop")return finishSimulation("stopped","Detención solicitada; resultados conservados");
    if(a==="tests")return runProgressionTests();
    if(a==="exportReports")return exportSimulationReports();
  }
  if(!simulationLocked())return;
  const control=e.target.closest("[data-act]");if(!control)return;
  const act=control.dataset.act;
  if(["export","downloadHtml"].includes(act))return;
  if(!["cast","start","stop","rest"].includes(act)){
    e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();return toast("Terminá la prueba antes de cambiar inventario, actividad, personaje o guardado.");
  }
  e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  const r=currentSimulation();
  if(r.status!=="running")return toast("La simulación está pausada; reanudala para actuar.");
  if(control.disabled||simSaving)return;
  withSimulationRng(r,()=>{
    if(act==="cast")castSpell(control.dataset.id);
    if(act==="start"){startCombat();r.reason="Combate iniciado manualmente";}
    if(act==="stop"){stopCombat();r.reason="Combate detenido manualmente";}
    if(act==="rest"){rest();r.reason="Descanso solicitado manualmente";}
  });
  updateSimFinal(r);await simulationCheckpoint();renderGame();
},true);
function exportSimulationReports(){
  const records=roster.characters.map(e=>({id:e.id,name:e.state.name,cls:e.state.cls,reports:e.state.dev?.progress?.history||[],current:e.state.dev?.progress?.simulation||null}));
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify({kind:"argentum-dev-reports",version:1,characters:records},null,2)],{type:"application/json"});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement("a");a.href=url;a.download="argentum-dev-informes.json";a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
}
/* Pruebas puras del motor real: se aíslan globals y RNG; no hay I/O. */
function runProgressionTests(){
  if(simulationLocked())return toast("Detené la simulación para ejecutar pruebas aisladas.");
  const saved={state,mode:gameMode,rng,draft,off:{...OFF},need:needRender,runtimeAt,runtimeRemainder};
  const lines=[];
  const test=(name,fn)=>{try{if(!fn())throw Error("resultado inesperado");lines.push("✓ "+name);}catch(e){lines.push("✗ "+name+": "+e.message);}};
  const fixture=()=>{const s=devFixture();s.devEnvironment=true;s.dev={invulnerable:false};return s;};
  const run=(initial,total,batch,seed="test")=>{
    state=copyJSON(initial);gameMode="dev";
    const r={id:"test",status:"paused",processed:0,target:total,combatSec:0,realMs:0,deaths:0,rngState:seedHash(seed),initial:valuesSnapshot(state),reason:"",speed:365,lastDeathAt:-1};
    devProgress().simulation=r;
    while(r.processed<total)simulationBatch(r,Math.min(batch,total-r.processed));
    const out={state:copyJSON(state),rng:r.rngState,processed:r.processed,combat:r.combatSec,deaths:r.deaths};return out;
  };
  try{
    OFF.active=true;simTestContext=true;
    test("Normal: XP ×1 incluso con opciones DEV",()=>{state=fixture();gameMode="normal";devProgress().xpMultiplier=1000;return multiplierFor(state,"character")===1&&multiplierFor(state,"skills")===1;});
    test("100 XP con ×365 = 36500, sin modificar oro",()=>{gameMode="dev";state=fixture();devProgress().xpMultiplier=365;const g=state.gold;gainCharacterXP(state,100);return state.xp===36500&&state.gold===g;});
    test("Selección XP personaje/habilidades/ambas",()=>["character","skills","both"].every(scope=>{state=fixture();const p=devProgress();p.xpMultiplier=10;p.xpScope=scope;gainCharacterXP(state,10);sklXp(state,"magia",10);return state.xp===(scope==="skills"?10:100)&&state.skills.magia.xp===(scope==="character"?10:100);}));
    test("Establecer niveles ignora multiplicador",()=>{state=fixture();devProgress().xpMultiplier=1000;devSetLevel(state,50);devSetSkill(state,"magia",25);return state.xp===xpFor(50)&&state.skills.magia.xp===5760;});
    test("Navegación ×10: oro 60, XP 4000 por hora",()=>{state=fixture();devProgress().xpMultiplier=10;state.nav={id:"costa",totalMs:3600000,remainingMs:3600000,elapsedMs:0,paused:false,legacy:false,earnedGold:0,earnedXp:0};advanceNav(state,3600000);return state.gold===160&&state.skills.navegacion.xp===4000;});
    test("Motor individual = lotes de 173 (estado, RNG, muertes)",()=>{const s=fixture();s.combat.active=true;return JSON.stringify(run(s,7200,1))===JSON.stringify(run(s,7200,173));});
    test("Invulnerabilidad y lotes con mismo RNG",()=>{const s=fixture();s.dev.invulnerable=true;s.combat.active=true;return JSON.stringify(run(s,3600,1))===JSON.stringify(run(s,3600,250));});
    test("Navegación por step: XP ×1 durante simulación",()=>{const s=fixture();s.dev.progress={xpMultiplier:365,xpScope:"both",history:[],simulation:null};s.nav={id:"costa",totalMs:3600000,remainingMs:3600000,elapsedMs:0,paused:false,legacy:false,earnedGold:0,earnedXp:0};const a=run(s,3600,113);return a.state.gold===160&&a.state.skills.navegacion.xp===400;});
    test("Checkpoint JSON + reanudación = ejecución continua",()=>{
      const s=fixture();s.combat.active=true;s.dev.invulnerable=true;
      const expected=run(s,3600,137);
      run(s,1800,73);const checkpoint=copyJSON(state),r=checkpoint.dev.progress.simulation;
      r.target=3600;r.frame=frameFlags(checkpoint);freezeCharacter(checkpoint);
      state=copyJSON(checkpoint);const recovered=state.dev.progress.simulation;restoreFrame(state,recovered);
      while(recovered.processed<3600)simulationBatch(recovered,Math.min(91,3600-recovered.processed));
      // Comparar exclusivamente estado del juego, RNG y contadores (no metadatos de UI).
      const actual=copyJSON(state),reference=expected.state;delete actual.dev.progress;delete reference.dev.progress;
      return JSON.stringify(actual)===JSON.stringify(reference)&&recovered.rngState===expected.rng&&recovered.combatSec===expected.combat;
    });
    test("Pausa y reloj normal no avanzan personaje",()=>{state=fixture();devProgress().simulation={status:"paused"};const before=JSON.stringify(state);runtimeAt=Date.now()-100000;syncRuntime();tick();return JSON.stringify(state)===before;});
    test("Muerte: sin reinicio ni recompensas posteriores",()=>{const s=fixture();s.hp=1;s.combat.enemyId="rey_pirata";s.combat.dungeonId="dungeon_pirata";s.combat.enemyHp=3308;s.combat.active=true;const out=run(s,86400,250);return out.deaths===1&&!out.state.combat.active&&sumKills(out.state)===0;});
    test("Cambio de contexto congela navegación sin premios",()=>{state=fixture();state.nav={id:"costa",remainingMs:3600000,paused:false};const g=state.gold;stopContextActivities(state);advanceNav(state,3600000);return state.gold===g&&state.nav.paused&&!state.combat.active;});
    test("RNG aislado: se restaura después de pasos",()=>{const original=rng;run(fixture(),50,7);return rng===original;});
    test("JSON DEV sigue rechazado en normal",()=>{const s=fixture();return !decodeForMode({version:7,kind:DEV_KIND,activeId:"a",characters:[{id:"a",state:s}]},"normal");});
    test("Checkpoint corrupto rechazado",()=>!validSimulation({status:"running",processed:NaN,target:10}));
  }finally{
    state=saved.state;gameMode=saved.mode;rng=saved.rng;draft=saved.draft;Object.assign(OFF,saved.off);needRender=saved.need;runtimeAt=saved.runtimeAt;runtimeRemainder=saved.runtimeRemainder;simulationExecuting=false;simTestContext=false;
  }
  const out=document.getElementById("simTests");if(out)out.textContent=lines.join("\n")+"\n\n"+lines.filter(x=>x.startsWith("✓")).length+" / "+lines.length+" aprobadas.\nPruebas del motor y serialización; no verifican IndexedDB real, recarga del navegador ni rendimiento de un año.";
}
window.addEventListener("pagehide",()=>{
  if(gameMode!=="dev"||!simulationLocked()||!storageReady)return;
  try{localStorage.setItem(MODE_STORAGE.dev.key,JSON.stringify(makeRosterRecord()));}catch(_){}
});


===== src/js/dev/011-dev-diagnostics.js =====

"use strict";
/* Diagnóstico aislado. */
let diagnosticJob=null;
let diagnosticFrame=null;
let diagnosticReports=[];
const diagnosticsEnemySwing=enemySwing;
enemySwing=function(d,en){
  const before=state.hp;
  diagnosticsEnemySwing(d,en);
  if(diagnosticFrame)diagnosticFrame.damage+=Math.max(0,before-state.hp);
};
const diagnosticsDeath=onDeath;
onDeath=function(en){if(diagnosticFrame)diagnosticFrame.deaths++;diagnosticsDeath(en);};
const diagnosticsCast=castSpell;
castSpell=function(id){
  const before=state?.hp||0;
  diagnosticsCast(id);
  if(diagnosticFrame){diagnosticFrame.healing+=Math.max(0,state.hp-before);diagnosticFrame.casts++;}
};
const diagnosticsAppend=appendDevPanel;
appendDevPanel=function(){diagnosticsAppend();appendDiagnostics();};
function appendDiagnostics(){
  document.getElementById('devBalancePanel')?.remove();
  if(gameMode!=='dev'||!state?.devEnvironment||!ready)return;
  const panel=document.createElement('details');panel.id='devBalancePanel';panel.className='panel';panel.style.marginTop='12px';
  panel.innerHTML=`<summary>📊 Diagnóstico reproducible de balance · motor original</summary>
  <p class="muted">Compara las ocho clases con Humano, el mismo nivel inicial, equipo, habilidad, NPC y semilla. XP ×1, sin invulnerabilidad. No busca una estrategia óptima ni cambia de enemigo. El nivel puede aumentar durante el ensayo. Se detiene el farmeo al morir, sin resurrección ni reinicio. Las bajas usan las tablas originales.</p>
  <div class="devFields"><label>Nivel inicial<input id="diagLevel" type="number" min="1" max="1000" value="1"></label><label>Nivel de habilidades<input id="diagSkills" type="number" min="1" max="1000" value="1"></label><label>Horas simuladas por clase<input id="diagHours" type="number" min="0.01" max="168" step="0.01" value="1"></label></div>
  <div class="devFields"><label>Objetivo<select id="diagEnemy">${ENEMIES.map(en=>`<option value="${en.id}">${esc(en.name)} · Nv ${en.level}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Equipo<select id="diagEquipment"><option value="none">Sin equipo</option><option value="basic">Daga, cuero y madera</option><option value="advanced">Hacha, placas y torre (si cumple requisitos)</option></select></label></div>
  <div class="devFields"><label>Política de intervención<select id="diagPolicy"><option value="none">Sin curación ni descanso</option><option value="heal">Guion: Curar Heridas Leves con HP &lt; 50%</option><option value="rest">Guion: descansar con HP &lt; 50%, reanudar al terminar</option></select></label><label>Semilla<input id="diagSeed" type="text" value="balance-1" maxlength="100"></label></div>
  <p class="muted">Curación: una comprobación antes de cada segundo, solo si hay maná y cooldown disponible; usa el hechizo real. Descanso: solo si es legal y pagable; reanuda únicamente el combate pausado por ese descanso, nunca tras una muerte. Ningún guion se aplica fuera del diagnóstico. Equipo avanzado incompatible queda sin equipar y se informa. Todos comienzan con 100 oro y recursos completos.</p>
  <div class="actions"><button data-diagnostic="start">Comparar ocho clases</button><button data-diagnostic="stop">Detener y conservar resultados parciales</button><button data-diagnostic="export">Exportar informe JSON</button></div><p class="muted">Iniciar pausa y guarda el personaje DEV actual. Durante el diagnóstico no avanzan personajes reales. No se reanuda automáticamente al finalizar. Una recarga cancela el ensayo; solo conserva los informes ya persistidos. No es un checkpoint de simulación.</p><div id="diagProgress" role="status">Sin ejecutar.</div><div id="diagResults" style="overflow:auto"></div>`;
  document.getElementById('devProgressionPanel')?.after(panel);
  diagnosticReports=Array.isArray(state.dev.balanceReports)?state.dev.balanceReports:[];
  displayDiagnosticReports();
}
function displayDiagnosticReports(){
  const box=document.getElementById('diagResults');if(!box)return;
  box.innerHTML=diagnosticReports.map(r=>{
    if(r.error)return `<p>${esc(r.className)}: ${esc(r.error)}</p>`;
    const rate=(n,t)=>t>0?(n*3600/t).toFixed(1):'—';
    const estimate=[10,20,35,50].map(n=>{
      if(r.final.xp>=xpFor(n))return `Nv ${n}: alcanzado${r.reached[n]!==undefined?' a '+durationText(r.reached[n]):' antes del ensayo'}`;
      const remaining=Math.max(0,xpFor(n)-r.final.xp),xp=r.final.xp-r.initial.xp;
      return `Nv ${n}: ${xp>0?(remaining/(xp/r.seconds)/3600).toFixed(1)+' h adicionales':'sin tasa positiva'} (extrapolación constante, NO ruta simulada)`;
    }).join('<br>');
    return `<article class="panel" style="margin:8px 0"><b>${esc(r.className)} · ${r.cancelled?'parcial':'completo'}</b><div class="muted">${esc(r.enemy)} · ${esc(r.policy)} · Semilla ${esc(r.seed)}<br>Equipo: ${esc(r.equipment.join(', ')||'ninguno')} ${esc(r.warnings.join('; '))}</div><p>Tiempo total ${durationText(r.seconds)} · Combate ${durationText(r.combatSeconds)} · Descanso ${durationText(r.restSeconds)}<br>Nivel ${r.initial.level} → ${r.final.level} · XP ganada ${fmt(r.final.xp-r.initial.xp)} · Oro neto ${fmt(r.final.gold-r.initial.gold)}<br>Por hora TOTAL: ${rate(r.final.xp-r.initial.xp,r.seconds)} XP · ${rate(r.final.gold-r.initial.gold,r.seconds)} oro neto · ${rate(r.kills,r.seconds)} bajas<br>Por hora de COMBATE: ${rate(r.final.xp-r.initial.xp,r.combatSeconds)} XP · ${rate(r.kills,r.combatSeconds)} bajas<br>Tiempo de combate / baja: ${r.kills?(r.combatSeconds/r.kills).toFixed(1)+' s (incluye combate incompleto)':'sin bajas'}<br>Daño recibido ${fmt(r.damage)} · Muertes ${r.deaths} · Curaciones ${r.casts} (+${fmt(r.healing)} HP) · Descansos ${r.rests} (${fmt(r.restGold)} oro)<br>Segundos combatiendo con HP &lt; 50%: ${r.lowHpSeconds} · Curación solicitada sin poder lanzarla: ${r.blockedHealSeconds} s<br>Estado final: ${esc(r.reason)}</p><p class="muted">${estimate}<br>Las tasas observadas no garantizan supervivencia futura. Si murió, extrapolar supondría reinicios que NO se ejecutaron; no es una predicción válida de farmeo continuo.</p><small>${esc(skillChanges({initial:r.initial},r.final))}</small></article>`;
  }).join('')||'<p class="muted">No hay mediciones. No se presentan cifras estimadas como pruebas ejecutadas.</p>';
}
function makeDiagnosticState(cls,config){
  const previousDraft=draft;
  let s;
  try{draft={name:'Diagnóstico '+cls.name,race:'humano',cls:cls.id};s=normalNewState();}finally{draft=previousDraft;}
  s.devEnvironment=true;s.dev={invulnerable:false};devSetLevel(s,config.level);
  devSetSkill(s,'weapon',config.skills);SKILLS.forEach(k=>devSetSkill(s,k.key,config.skills));
  const en=enemyById(config.enemy),dg=dungeonById(en.dungeonId);
  if(!dungeonOk(dg,s)||!enemyUnlocked(en,derive(s).L))throw Error('Objetivo bloqueado por requisitos reales de nivel o Navegación.');
  const warnings=[];
  const items=config.equipment==='advanced'?['hacha','placas','torre']:config.equipment==='basic'?['daga','cuero','madera']:[];
  items.forEach(id=>{const item=itemById(id);if(reqOk(s,item.req)){s.owned[id]=true;s.equip[item.slot]=id;}else warnings.push(item.name+': requisito no cumplido');});
  const d=derive(s);s.hp=d.hpMax;s.mp=d.mpMax;
  s.combat={active:true,dungeonId:en.dungeonId,enemyId:en.id,enemyHp:en.hp,pT:0,eT:0,enemyPara:0};
  return {s,warnings};
}
async function runDiagnosticComparison(){
  if(gameMode!=='dev'||!state?.devEnvironment||busy||managing||simulationLocked()||diagnosticJob)return toast('Terminá la simulación o tarea actual antes del diagnóstico.');
  const config={level:Number(document.getElementById('diagLevel').value),skills:Number(document.getElementById('diagSkills').value),hours:Number(document.getElementById('diagHours').value),enemy:document.getElementById('diagEnemy').value,equipment:document.getElementById('diagEquipment').value,policy:document.getElementById('diagPolicy').value,seed:document.getElementById('diagSeed').value||'balance-1'};
  if(![config.level,config.skills].every(n=>Number.isInteger(n)&&n>=1&&n<=1000)||!Number.isFinite(config.hours)||config.hours<0.01||config.hours>168)return toast('Nivel/habilidades: 1–1000. Duración: 0,01–168 horas.');
  syncRuntime();if(busy)return;
  freezeCharacter(state);runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;
  if(!await saveRoster())return toast('No se inició: falló el guardado DEV previo.');
  const realState=state,job={cancel:false};diagnosticJob=job;busy=true;
  const total=Math.round(config.hours*3600);diagnosticReports=[];
  try{
    for(const cls of CLASSES){
      if(job.cancel)break;
      let setup;
      try{setup=makeDiagnosticState(cls,config);}catch(e){diagnosticReports.push({className:cls.name,error:e.message});continue;}
      const s=setup.s,r={className:cls.name,enemy:enemyById(config.enemy).name,policy:config.policy,seed:config.seed,config:{...config},equipment:Object.values(s.equip).filter(Boolean).map(id=>itemById(id).name),warnings:setup.warnings,initial:valuesSnapshot(s),seconds:0,combatSeconds:0,restSeconds:0,kills:0,damage:0,deaths:0,casts:0,healing:0,rests:0,restGold:0,lowHpSeconds:0,blockedHealSeconds:0,reached:{},reason:'Combate activo',rngState:seedHash(config.seed)};
      let resumeAfterRest=false;
      while(r.seconds<total&&!job.cancel){
        const previous={state,rng,off:{...OFF},need:needRender};
        const start=performance.now();let count=0;
        try{
          state=s;rng=()=>seededNext(r);OFF.active=true;diagnosticFrame=r;
          while(r.seconds<total&&count<250&&performance.now()-start<8){
            if(resumeAfterRest&&s.restLeft===0&&!r.deaths){beginPhysicalCombat(s);resumeAfterRest=false;}
            const low=s.hp<derive(s).hpMax*0.5;
            if(s.combat.active&&low){
              r.lowHpSeconds++;
              if(config.policy==='heal'){
                if(canCast('curar_leve'))castSpell('curar_leve');else r.blockedHealSeconds++;
              }else if(config.policy==='rest'&&s.restCd===0&&s.gold>=restCost(derive(s).L)){
                const gold=s.gold;rest();if(s.restLeft>0){r.rests++;r.restGold+=gold-s.gold;resumeAfterRest=true;}
              }
            }
            if(s.combat.active&&!s.restLeft)r.combatSeconds++;
            if(s.restLeft>0)r.restSeconds++;
            step();r.seconds++;count++;
            const level=levelFromXp(s.xp);for(const n of [10,20,35,50])if(level>=n&&r.initial.level<n&&r.reached[n]===undefined)r.reached[n]=r.seconds;
            if(r.deaths){resumeAfterRest=false;r.reason='Muerte: farmeo detenido, sin reinicio';}
          }
        }finally{diagnosticFrame=null;state=previous.state;rng=previous.rng;Object.assign(OFF,previous.off);needRender=previous.need;}
        const progress=document.getElementById('diagProgress');if(progress)progress.textContent=`${cls.name}: ${durationText(r.seconds)} / ${durationText(total)} · ${Math.floor(r.seconds/total*100)}% · ${r.deaths} muertes`;
        await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
      }
      r.cancelled=job.cancel;r.kills=sumKills(s)-r.initial.kills;r.final=valuesSnapshot(s);
      if(!r.deaths)r.reason=job.cancel?'Cancelación solicitada; '+activityReason(s):activityReason(s)+' al completar la duración';
      diagnosticReports.push(r);
      realState.dev.balanceReports=copyJSON(diagnosticReports);
      displayDiagnosticReports();
            await saveRoster();
    }
  }catch(e){toast('Diagnóstico detenido: '+e.message);}
  finally{
    state=realState;diagnosticFrame=null;diagnosticJob=null;busy=false;runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;
    state.dev.balanceReports=copyJSON(diagnosticReports);await saveRoster();
    const progress=document.getElementById('diagProgress');if(progress)progress.textContent=job.cancel?'Cancelado: resultados parciales conservados.':'Diagnóstico terminado. Consultá los resultados; no son una estrategia óptima.';
    displayDiagnosticReports();updateLive();
  }
}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const button=e.target.closest('[data-diagnostic]');if(!button)return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  if(gameMode!=='dev'||!state?.devEnvironment)return;
  const action=button.dataset.diagnostic;
  if(action==='stop'){if(diagnosticJob)diagnosticJob.cancel=true;return;}
  if(action==='start')return runDiagnosticComparison();
  if(action==='export'){
    const text=JSON.stringify({kind:'argentum-dev-balance-report',version:1,generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),reports:diagnosticReports},null,2);
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text],{type:'application/json'}));const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='argentum-dev-diagnostico.json';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
  }
},true);


===== src/js/dev/013-progression-lab.js =====

'use strict';
/* Laboratorio aislado y progreso visible; sin nuevo límite normal. */
const PROGRESS_MILESTONES={
 weapon:[[5,'Golpe firme',false],[10,'Categoría de armas de hierro',false],[20,'Categoría de armas de acero',false],[35,'Armas especializadas',false],[50,'Especialización ofensiva',false],[75,'Armas maestras',false],[100,'Maestría',false]],
 magia:[[3,'Comprar Flecha Mágica',true],[5,'Comprar Remover Parálisis y Túnica arcana',true],[6,'Comprar Flecha Eléctrica',true],[10,'Comprar Misil Mágico e Inmovilizar',true],[12,'Comprar Curar Heridas Graves',true],[16,'Comprar Descarga Eléctrica',true],[23,'Comprar Tormenta de Fuego',true],[35,'Comprar Apocalipsis',true],[50,'Especialización mágica',false],[75,'Hechizo maestro',false],[100,'Maestría',false]],
 resMagica:[[5,'Información ampliada de mitigación',false],[10,'Preparación rúnica',false],[20,'Talismanes protectores',false],[35,'Desafío de sellos',false],[50,'Protección manual',false],[75,'Protección especializada',false],[100,'Maestría',false]],
 escudos:[[5,'Minijuego de escudos',false],[8,'Comprar Escudo de hierro',true],[18,'Comprar Escudo torre',true],[20,'Técnica de guardia',false],[35,'Escudos especializados',false],[50,'Reacción defensiva',false],[75,'Escudo maestro',false],[100,'Maestría',false]],
 armaduras:[[5,'Comprar Cota de malla',true],[15,'Comprar Armadura de placas',true],[25,'Armaduras especializadas',false],[35,'Ajuste de equipo',false],[50,'Armaduras avanzadas',false],[75,'Armadura maestra',false],[100,'Maestría',false]],
 navegacion:[[8,'Ruta de altamar',true],[16,'Cruce de tormenta',true],[25,'Dungeon Pirata: requiere también personaje 35',true],[40,'Exploración marítima',false],[60,'Rutas de investigación',false],[80,'Expediciones de colección',false],[100,'Maestría marítima',false]]
};
const progressSkillName=k=>k==='character'?'Personaje':k==='weapon'?'Combate con Armas':SKILLS.find(x=>x.key===k)?.name||k;
function progressSkillLevel(s,k){return k==='character'?levelFromXp(s.xp):k==='weapon'?levelFromXp(s.skill.xp):skl(s,k);}
function progressThreshold(k,n){return k==='character'||k==='weapon'?xpFor(n):10*(n-1)**2;}
function progressStatsText(a,b){return [['hpMax','HP'],['mpMax','MP'],['attack','ATK'],['defense','DEF'],['magicRes','RM'],['spellMult','Poder mágico']].map(([k,name])=>`${name}: ${Number(a[k].toFixed(3))} → ${Number(b[k].toFixed(3))}`).join(' · ');}
function progressNotice(s,before,after,label){
 if(OFF.active||labFrame||s!==state)return;
 if(!s.ui)s.ui={};
 const m=label.match(/(\d+) → (\d+)/),a=m?+m[1]:0,b=m?+m[2]:0;
 const k=Object.keys(PROGRESS_MILESTONES).find(k=>label.startsWith(progressSkillName(k)));
 const rows=k?PROGRESS_MILESTONES[k]:DUNGEONS.map(d=>[d.minLevel,d.name+(d.navReq?' (además Navegación '+d.navReq+')':''),true]);
 const u=rows.filter(r=>r[2]&&r[0]>a&&r[0]<=b).map(r=>r[1]);
 s.ui.lastProgressNotice={label,text:progressStatsText(before,after)+(u.length?' · Requisitos alcanzados: '+u.join('; ')+'. Revisá compra y requisitos adicionales.':'')};
 if(u.length||!e217HasLevelFeedback(s))toast('¡'+label+'!'+(u.length?' · '+u.join(' · '):''));
}
const visibleGainXP=gainCharacterXP;
gainCharacterXP=function(s,n){const a=derive(s),value=visibleGainXP(s,n),b=derive(s);e217XPEvent(s,'character',value,a.L,b.L);if(b.L>a.L)progressNotice(s,a,b,`Nivel ${a.L} → ${b.L}`);return value;};
const visibleSkillXP=sklXp;
sklXp=function(s,key,n){const level=skl(s,key),a=derive(s),value=visibleSkillXP(s,key,n),end=skl(s,key);e217XPEvent(s,key,value,level,end);if(end>level)progressNotice(s,a,derive(s),`${progressSkillName(key)} ${level} → ${end}`);return value;};
const visiblePlayerSwing=playerSwing;
playerSwing=function(d,en){const a=derive(state),x=state.skill.xp;visiblePlayerSwing(d,en);const b=derive(state);e217XPEvent(state,'weapon',state.skill.xp-x,a.sk,b.sk);if(b.sk>a.sk)progressNotice(state,a,b,`Combate con Armas ${a.sk} → ${b.sk}`);};
function visibleSkillCard(name,xp,weapon=false){
 const level=weapon?levelFromXp(xp):skillLvl(xp),lo=weapon?xpFor(level):10*(level-1)**2,hi=weapon?xpFor(level+1):10*level**2;
 const pct=clamp(100*(xp-lo)/(hi-lo),0,100);
 return `<div class="skillCard"><div class="row"><span>${esc(name)}</span><b>Nv ${level} → ${level+1}</b></div><div class="bar xp" role="progressbar" aria-label="${esc(name)}" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100" aria-valuenow="${pct.toFixed(1)}"><div style="width:${pct}%"></div></div><small>${fmt(xp-lo)} / ${fmt(hi-lo)} XP · ${pct.toFixed(1)}% · faltan ${fmt(hi-xp)} XP</small></div>`;
}
hybridSkillCard=visibleSkillCard;

function progressionVisibleRefresh(){
 if(!state||!ready)return;
 const s=state,d=derive(s),a=xpFor(d.L),b=xpFor(d.L+1);
 document.getElementById('xpText').textContent=`${fmt(s.xp-a)} / ${fmt(b-a)} XP · ${(100*(s.xp-a)/(b-a)).toFixed(1)}% · faltan ${fmt(b-s.xp)} para Nv ${d.L+1}`;
 for(const k in PROGRESS_MILESTONES){const card=document.querySelector(`[data-skill-key="${k}"]`);if(!card)continue;let hint=card.querySelector('.nextUnlock');if(!hint){hint=document.createElement('small');hint.className='nextUnlock';hint.style.minHeight='48px';card.append(hint);}Array.from(card.querySelectorAll('small')).slice(1).forEach(el=>{if(el!==hint)el.remove();});const l=progressSkillLevel(s,k),n=PROGRESS_MILESTONES[k].find(r=>r[0]>l&&r[2])||PROGRESS_MILESTONES[k].find(r=>r[0]>l);hint.textContent=n?`${n[2]?'Próximo requisito':'FUTURO, no implementado'}: ${n[1]} · ${l}/${n[0]}`:'Sin más hitos definidos.';}
 document.getElementById('visibleProgressBox')?.remove(); return; // E1.4: objetivos pausados hasta implementar misiones reales.
}

const progressLive=updateLive;
updateLive=function(){progressLive();progressionVisibleRefresh();};
/* Laboratorio: una referencia de contexto de vida estrictamente síncrona. */
let labFrame=null,labJob=null;
const labOriginalXP=xpFor,labOriginalDerive=derive,labOriginalHit=hitChance;
const LAB_DEFAULT_XP20=17000;
function labCurve(n,c){
 if(n<=1)return 0;
 if(n<=10)return Math.floor(c.xp10*((n-1)/9)**1.15);
 if(n<=20)return Math.floor(c.xp10+(c.xp20-c.xp10)*((n-10)/10)**1.15);
 // Solo para que el motor pueda consultar el siguiente umbral. No se mide >20.
 return Math.floor(c.xp20+(c.xp20-c.xp10)*(n-20));
}
xpFor=function(n){return labFrame?.candidate?labCurve(n,labFrame.config):labOriginalXP(n);};
derive=function(s){
 const d=labOriginalDerive(s),f=labFrame;
 if(!f?.candidate||s!==f.fixture)return d;
 const c=classById(s.cls),r=raceById(s.race),eq=equipBonus(s),p=f.config;
 // Combate con Armas conserva su curva original en ambos perfiles.
 const sk=levelFromOriginalWeaponXP(s.skill.xp);d.sk=sk;
 d.attack=Math.max(1,Math.round((CFG.BASE.attack+c.attack+r.attack+p.physLevel*(d.L-1)+eq.attack)*(1+p.weaponScale*(sk-1))));
 d.spellMult=(1+(c.magic+r.magic)/50)*(1+p.magicLevel*(d.L-1))*(1+p.magicSkill*(skl(s,'magia')-1));return d;
};
function levelFromOriginalWeaponXP(x){let n=1;while(labOriginalXP(n+1)<=x)n++;return n;}
const labDeriveCandidate=derive;
derive=function(s){const d=labDeriveCandidate(s);if(labFrame?.candidate&&s===labFrame.fixture)d.sk=levelFromOriginalWeaponXP(s.skill.xp);return d;};
hitChance=function(a,b){
 if(labFrame?.candidate&&labFrame.playerAccuracy){const p=labFrame.config;return clamp(CFG.HIT_BASE+p.levelAccuracy*(a-b)+p.weaponAccuracy*(derive(state).sk-1),.15,.98);}
 return labOriginalHit(a,b);
};
const labPlayerSwing=playerSwing;
playerSwing=function(d,en){if(!labFrame)return labPlayerSwing(d,en);labFrame.playerAccuracy=true;labFrame.attempts++;const before=state.combat.enemyHp;try{return labPlayerSwing(d,en);}finally{labFrame.playerAccuracy=false;const loss=Math.max(0,before-state.combat.enemyHp);labFrame.physicalDamage+=loss;if(loss>0)labFrame.hits++;}};
const labEnemySwing=enemySwing;
enemySwing=function(d,en){const hp=state.hp;labEnemySwing(d,en);if(labFrame)labFrame.received+=Math.max(0,hp-state.hp);};
const labDeath=onDeath;
onDeath=function(en){if(labFrame)labFrame.deaths++;return labDeath(en);};
const labCast=castSpell;
castSpell=function(id){
 if(!labFrame)return labCast(id);
 const oldMp=state.mp,hp=state.hp,enemyHp=state.combat.enemyHp,kills=sumKills(state);labCast(id);
 if(state.mp<oldMp){labFrame.casts++;labFrame.mana+=oldMp-state.mp;labFrame.healing+=Math.max(0,state.hp-hp);if(spellById(id)?.kind==='dmg')labFrame.magicDamage+=sumKills(state)>kills?enemyHp:Math.max(0,enemyHp-state.combat.enemyHp);}
};
const labAppend=appendDevPanel;
appendDevPanel=function(){labAppend();appendBalanceLab();};
function appendBalanceLab(){
 document.getElementById('balanceLabPanel')?.remove();if(gameMode!=='dev'||!ready||!state?.devEnvironment)return;
 const panel=document.createElement('details');panel.className='panel';panel.id='balanceLabPanel';
 panel.innerHTML=`<summary>🔬 Laboratorio 1–20 · actual vs candidato</summary><p class="muted">Ensayos aislados, XP ×1, Humano, nivel 1, habilidades 1, 100 oro. Compara ocho clases y dos perfiles con las mismas semillas. Sin compras automáticas, cambio de NPC ni resurrección. El equipo se concede inicialmente: esto NO mide su adquisición.</p><div class="labFields"><label>Repeticiones por clase/perfil<input id="labRepeats" type="number" min="1" max="30" value="3"></label><label>Máximo de horas por ensayo<input id="labHours" type="number" min="0.1" max="168" value="4" step="0.1"></label><label>Semilla base<input id="labSeed" value="progresion-1"></label><label>NPC fijo<select id="labEnemy">${ENEMIES.filter(e=>e.dungeonId==='newbie'&&e.level<=4).map(e=>`<option value="${e.id}">${esc(e.name)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Equipo concedido<select id="labEquipment"><option value="none">Sin equipo</option><option value="basic">Daga, cuero, madera</option></select></label><label>Política explícita<select id="labPolicy"><option value="idle">Físico idle; sin intervención</option><option value="heal">Curar Leve si HP &lt; 50%</option><option value="active">Curar Leve &lt; 50%; si no, Dardo disponible</option><option value="rest">Descanso legal &lt; 50%; reiniciar tras descanso</option></select></label></div><p class="muted">Políticas activas: comprobar antes de cada segundo; una acción como máximo. Solo Dardo y Curar Leve iniciales, con costes/cooldowns reales. Descanso nunca reinicia tras muerte. Objetivo fijo elegido por vos, no estrategia óptima. El ensayo termina al alcanzar 20, morir, cancelar o agotar el tiempo.</p><h2>Parámetros candidatos (no afectan el juego)</h2><div class="labFields">${[['xp10','XP acumulada nivel 10',3000],['xp20','XP acumulada nivel 20',17000],['physLevel','ATK por nivel',1.2],['weaponScale','Escala Armas por nivel',.01],['magicLevel','Escala magia por personaje',.012],['magicSkill','Escala por Magia',.01],['levelAccuracy','Precisión por diferencia de nivel',.008],['weaponAccuracy','Precisión por Armas',.001]].map(([id,name,v])=>`<label>${name}<input id="lab_${id}" type="number" min="0" step="any" value="${v}"></label>`).join('')}</div><p class="progressHint">Curva experimental solo de personaje: anclas 1=0, 10 y 20 editables, interpolación con exponente 1,15. Armas usa su curva original. HP/MP, defensas, enemigos, recompensas, curaciones y descanso no cambian. Por encima de 20 no se evalúa. Ninguna fórmula está aprobada para normal.</p><div class="actions"><button data-lab="run">Comparar perfiles y ocho clases</button><button data-lab="stop">Cancelar</button><button data-lab="export">Exportar resultados</button><button data-lab="tests">Pruebas aisladas</button></div><div id="labStatus" role="status">Sin ejecutar.</div><pre id="labTests" style="white-space:pre-wrap"></pre><div id="labResults" style="overflow:auto"></div>`;
 document.getElementById('gameScreen').append(panel);renderLabResults();
}
function labConfig(){
 const c={};for(const id of ['xp10','xp20','physLevel','weaponScale','magicLevel','magicSkill','levelAccuracy','weaponAccuracy']){c[id]=Number(document.getElementById('lab_'+id).value);if(!Number.isFinite(c[id])||c[id]<0)throw Error('Parámetro inválido: '+id);}
 if(c.xp10<9||c.xp20<=c.xp10+10||c.xp20>1e9||c.physLevel>10||c.weaponScale>1||c.magicLevel>1||c.magicSkill>1||c.levelAccuracy>1||c.weaponAccuracy>1)throw Error('Parámetros fuera de los límites de laboratorio.');return c;
}
function labFixture(cls,equipment){const previous=draft;try{draft={name:'Laboratorio',race:'humano',cls};const s=normalNewState();s.devEnvironment=true;s.dev={invulnerable:false};if(equipment==='basic')for(const id of ['daga','cuero','madera']){s.owned[id]=true;s.equip[itemById(id).slot]=id;}const d=labOriginalDerive(s);s.hp=d.hpMax;s.mp=d.mpMax;return s;}finally{draft=previous;}}
function newLabFrame(s,config,candidate,seed,policy){return {fixture:s,config,candidate,policy,rngState:seedHash(seed),playerAccuracy:false,seconds:0,combatSeconds:0,restSeconds:0,attempts:0,hits:0,physicalDamage:0,magicDamage:0,received:0,deaths:0,casts:0,mana:0,healing:0,rests:0,restGold:0,lowHpSeconds:0,blockedHealing:0,reached10:null,reached20:null,resumeRest:false,killDurations:[],sinceKill:0};}
function labOneSecond(f){
 const s=state,killsBeforeActions=sumKills(s);
 if(f.resumeRest&&s.restLeft===0&&!f.deaths){beginPhysicalCombat(s);f.resumeRest=false;}
 const d=derive(s),low=s.hp<d.hpMax*.5;
 if(s.combat.active&&low)f.lowHpSeconds++;
 if(s.combat.active){
 if(['active','heal'].includes(f.policy)&&low){if(canCast('curar_leve'))castSpell('curar_leve');else f.blockedHealing++;}
 else if(f.policy==='active'&&canCast('dardo'))castSpell('dardo');
 if(f.policy==='rest'&&low&&s.restCd===0&&s.gold>=restCost(d.L)){const g=s.gold;rest();if(s.restLeft){f.rests++;f.restGold+=g-s.gold;f.resumeRest=true;}}
 }
 const was=s.combat.active&&!navActive(s)&&!s.restLeft,kills=sumKills(s);
 if(was){f.combatSeconds++;f.sinceKill++;}if(s.restLeft)f.restSeconds++;
 step();f.seconds++;
 if(sumKills(s)>killsBeforeActions){f.killDurations.push(f.sinceKill);f.sinceKill=0;}
 const l=derive(s).L;if(l>=10&&f.reached10===null)f.reached10=f.seconds;if(l>=20&&f.reached20===null)f.reached20=f.seconds;
}
function labSynchronous(f,fn){
 const previous={state,rng,off:{...OFF},need:needRender,at:runtimeAt,remainder:runtimeRemainder,frame:labFrame};
 try{labFrame=f;state=f.fixture;rng=()=>seededNext(f);OFF.active=true;return fn();}
 finally{labFrame=previous.frame;state=previous.state;rng=previous.rng;Object.assign(OFF,previous.off);needRender=previous.need;runtimeAt=previous.at;runtimeRemainder=previous.remainder;}
}
async function runBalanceLab(){
 if(gameMode!=='dev'||!state?.devEnvironment||busy||managing||simulationLocked()||labJob)return;
 let config,repeats,hours;try{config=labConfig();repeats=Number(document.getElementById('labRepeats').value);hours=Number(document.getElementById('labHours').value);if(!Number.isInteger(repeats)||repeats<1||repeats>30||!Number.isFinite(hours)||hours<.1||hours>168)throw Error('Repeticiones 1–30; horas 0,1–168.');}catch(e){return toast(e.message);}
 const seed=document.getElementById('labSeed').value||'progresion-1',policy=document.getElementById('labPolicy').value,equipment=document.getElementById('labEquipment').value,enemy=document.getElementById('labEnemy').value;
 syncRuntime();if(busy)return;freezeCharacter(state);runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;if(!await saveRoster())return toast('No se inició: guardado DEV fallido.');
 const real=state,job={cancel:false};labJob=job;busy=true;const reports=[];real.dev.labReports=reports;
 try{
 for(const cls of CLASSES)for(const candidate of [false,true])for(let rep=0;rep<repeats;rep++){
 if(job.cancel)break;
 const s=labFixture(cls.id,equipment),en=enemyById(enemy);s.combat.enemyId=en.id;s.combat.enemyHp=en.hp;
 if(!enemyUnlocked(en,1)){reports.push({error:'NPC bloqueado a nivel 1',cls:cls.id});continue;}
 s.combat.active=true;const f=newLabFrame(s,config,candidate,seed+':'+rep,policy),initial=labSynchronous(f,()=>valuesSnapshot(s)),limit=Math.round(hours*3600);
 while(f.seconds<limit&&!job.cancel&&!f.deaths&&f.reached20===null){
 labSynchronous(f,()=>{const start=performance.now();let count=0;while(count++<250&&performance.now()-start<8&&f.seconds<limit&&!f.deaths&&f.reached20===null)labOneSecond(f);});
 const out=document.getElementById('labStatus');if(out)out.textContent=`${cls.name} · ${candidate?'candidato':'actual'} · repetición ${rep+1}/${repeats} · ${durationText(f.seconds)}/${durationText(limit)} · nivel ${labSynchronous(f,()=>derive(s).L)}. Cancelable.`;
 await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
 }
 const final=labSynchronous(f,()=>valuesSnapshot(s));
 reports.push({cls:cls.id,profile:candidate?'candidato':'actual',seed:seed+':'+rep,policy,equipment,enemy,config:{...config},initial,final,seconds:f.seconds,combatSeconds:f.combatSeconds,restSeconds:f.restSeconds,reached10:f.reached10,reached20:f.reached20,kills:sumKills(s),deaths:f.deaths,attempts:f.attempts,hits:f.hits,physicalDamage:f.physicalDamage,magicDamage:f.magicDamage,received:f.received,casts:f.casts,mana:f.mana,healing:f.healing,rests:f.rests,restGold:f.restGold,lowHpSeconds:f.lowHpSeconds,blockedHealing:f.blockedHealing,killDurations:f.killDurations,reason:job.cancel?'Cancelación':f.deaths?'Derrota; sin reinicio':f.reached20!==null?'Nivel 20 alcanzado':'Tiempo agotado',cancelled:job.cancel});
  await saveRoster();renderLabResults();
 }
 }catch(e){toast('Laboratorio detenido: '+e.message);}finally{state=real;labFrame=null;busy=false;labJob=null;runtimeAt=Date.now();runtimeRemainder=0;await saveRoster();const out=document.getElementById('labStatus');if(out)out.textContent=job.cancel?'Cancelado: resultados parciales conservados.':'Ensayos terminados. Las metas no alcanzadas no se extrapolan.';renderLabResults();updateLive();}
}
function renderLabResults(){
 const box=document.getElementById('labResults');if(!box||!state)return;const reports=state.dev?.labReports||[];
 const rate=(n,s)=>s>0?(n*3600/s).toFixed(1):'—';
 const rows=reports.filter(r=>!r.error);
 const groups=new Map();for(const r of rows){const key=r.cls+' / '+r.profile;if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,[]);groups.get(key).push(r);}
 const spread=a=>{if(!a.length)return 'no alcanzado';a.sort((x,y)=>x-y);const m=a.length%2?a[(a.length-1)/2]:(a[a.length/2-1]+a[a.length/2])/2;return `${durationText(m)} [${durationText(a[0])}–${durationText(a[a.length-1])}]`;};
 box.innerHTML=`<p class="muted">Mediana y rango de tiempos SOLO entre ensayos que alcanzan la meta; se muestra cuántos. Sin extrapolar muertes ni objetivos fallidos. XP/h y oro/h se dividen por tiempo total realmente procesado; una muerte finaliza el ensayo. No equivalen a 24 h de ausencia.</p><table class="labTable"><tr><th>Clase/perfil</th><th>Hasta 10</th><th>Hasta 20</th><th>Muertes/ensayos</th></tr>${Array.from(groups,([key,list])=>`<tr><td>${esc(key)}</td><td>${spread(list.filter(r=>r.reached10!==null).map(r=>r.reached10))} · ${list.filter(r=>r.reached10!==null).length}/${list.length}</td><td>${spread(list.filter(r=>r.reached20!==null).map(r=>r.reached20))} · ${list.filter(r=>r.reached20!==null).length}/${list.length}</td><td>${list.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.deaths,0)}/${list.length}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table>${rows.map(r=>`<details><summary>${esc(classById(r.cls).name)} · ${r.profile} · ${esc(r.seed)} · ${esc(r.reason)}</summary><p>Tiempo ${durationText(r.seconds)} · combate ${durationText(r.combatSeconds)} · descanso ${durationText(r.restSeconds)}<br>Nivel ${r.initial.level} → ${r.final.level} · XP ${fmt(r.final.xp-r.initial.xp)} · oro neto ${fmt(r.final.gold-r.initial.gold)}<br>${rate(r.final.xp-r.initial.xp,r.seconds)} XP/h · ${rate(r.final.gold-r.initial.gold,r.seconds)} oro/h · ${r.kills} bajas · ${r.deaths} muertes<br>Precisión observada ${r.attempts?(100*r.hits/r.attempts).toFixed(1):'—'}% (${r.hits}/${r.attempts}) · daño físico total ${r.physicalDamage} · mágico total ${r.magicDamage}<br>Tiempo combate/baja ${r.kills?(r.combatSeconds/r.kills).toFixed(1):'—'} s (incluye pelea incompleta).<br>Daño recibido ${r.received} · curaciones HP ${fmt(r.healing)} · lanzamientos ${r.casts} · MP ${r.mana} · descansos ${r.rests} (${r.restGold} oro).<br>HP bajo ${r.lowHpSeconds} s · intentos de curación bloqueados ${r.blockedHealing} s.<br>${esc(skillChanges(r,r.final))}</p></details>`).join('')||'<p>Sin resultados ejecutados.</p>'}`;
}
function runBalanceLabTests(){
 if(busy||simulationLocked())return;const out=[];const check=(name,fn)=>{try{if(!fn())throw Error('Resultado inesperado');out.push('✓ '+name);}catch(e){out.push('✗ '+name+': '+e.message);}};
 const c={xp10:3000,xp20:17000,physLevel:1.2,weaponScale:.01,magicLevel:.012,magicSkill:.01,levelAccuracy:.008,weaponAccuracy:.001};
 check('Fuera del laboratorio: curva normal intacta',()=>xpFor(20)===labOriginalXP(20));
 check('Anclas experimentales 1/10/20',()=>labCurve(1,c)===0&&labCurve(10,c)===3000&&labCurve(20,c)===17000);
 check('Umbrales estrictamente crecientes 1–20',()=>Array.from({length:19},(_,i)=>labCurve(i+2,c)>labCurve(i+1,c)).every(Boolean));
 const execute=batch=>{const s=labFixture('guerrero','basic');s.combat.active=true;const f=newLabFrame(s,c,true,'igualdad','active');while(f.seconds<600&&!f.deaths&&f.reached20===null)labSynchronous(f,()=>{for(let i=0;i<batch&&f.seconds<600&&!f.deaths&&f.reached20===null;i++)labOneSecond(f);});return JSON.stringify({s,seconds:f.seconds,rng:f.rngState,damage:f.physicalDamage,magic:f.magicDamage,deaths:f.deaths});};
 check('Mismos pasos y semilla: individual = lotes de 73',()=>execute(1)===execute(73));
 check('Estado real y RNG restaurados después de fixture',()=>{const saved=JSON.stringify(state),old=rng;execute(17);return JSON.stringify(state)===saved&&rng===old&&labFrame===null;});
 check('Armas conserva curva original en perfil candidato',()=>{const s=labFixture('guerrero','none');s.skill.xp=labOriginalXP(10);const f=newLabFrame(s,c,true,'arma','idle');return labSynchronous(f,()=>derive(s).sk===10);});
 check('Hitos previstos no incorporan equipo ni hechizos',()=>!itemById('espada_acero')&&!spellById('golpe_firme')&&PROGRESS_MILESTONES.weapon.some(r=>r[0]===20&&!r[2]));
 check('Normal rechaza JSON marcado DEV',()=>!decodeForMode({kind:DEV_KIND,version:7,activeId:null,characters:[]},'normal'));
 const box=document.getElementById('labTests');if(box)box.textContent=out.join('\n')+'\nSolo ejecutadas al pulsar este botón. No comprueban IndexedDB real, recarga, cancelación de UI ni guardados entre pestañas.';
}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
 const b=e.target.closest('[data-lab]');if(!b)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(gameMode!=='dev'||!state?.devEnvironment)return;
 const a=b.dataset.lab;if(a==='stop'){if(labJob)labJob.cancel=true;return;}if(busy||managing||simulationLocked())return;
 if(a==='run')return runBalanceLab();if(a==='tests')return runBalanceLabTests();
 if(a==='export'){const text=JSON.stringify({kind:'argentum-dev-lab-results',version:1,createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),reports:state.dev.labReports||[]},null,2),url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text],{type:'application/json'})),link=document.createElement('a');link.href=url;link.download='argentum-laboratorio-1-20.json';link.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}
},true);


===== src/js/fishing/136-polish29.js =====
'use strict';
// Small presentation changes on the existing lifecycle; no save migration.
function e229CollectionBadge(zone,css=''){
 return '<span class="e229CollectionSeal '+css+'" role="img" aria-label="Insignia de colección de '+esc(zone.name)+'">'+(e227Illustration('collection',zone.id)||e227ZoneArt(zone))+'</span>';
}
function e229Reward(q){return q.rodId?e194RodArt(q.rodId):e227Illustration('ui',q.goals.some(g=>/Entregá/.test(g.label))?'quest-delivery':'quest-exploration');}
function e229Polish(){
 if(!state||fishingFinishDepth)return;const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');if(!page)return;
 const zone=e165Zone(),banner=page.querySelector('.e1123ZoneBanner'),book=page.querySelector('.e186Book'),info=page.querySelector('.e165ZoneInfo');

'use strict';
// Read-only presentation. No persistence, simulation or reward calculations here.
let e231ArtSerial=0;
function e230HudArt(kind,id,width,height,category='normal'){
 const painted=kind==='fish'?E227_ART['specimen-'+category+':'+id]:null;const f=painted||E227_ART['hud-'+kind+':'+id],water=E227_ART['ui:water'];if(!f||!water)return '';
 const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(width)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(height)),key='e231-art-'+(++e231ArtSerial);
 const label=e231ArtNames.get(kind+':'+id);
 const glints=!painted&&['large','huge','legendary'].includes(category),color=category==='legendary'?'#ffe19a':category==='huge'?'#aee9ff':'#d5ecc3';
 const dots=glints?'<g class="e231ScaleGlints" mask="url(#'+key+'-body)" fill="'+color+'">'+[[.25,.40],[.38,.55],[.51,.38],[.58,.62],[.67,.45],[.75,.56]].map(([x,y],i)=>'<path opacity="'+(category==='legendary'?.82:.55)+'" d="M'+(w*x)+' '+(h*y-3)+'l1.2 2 3 .8-3 .8-1.2 2-1.2-2-3-.8 3-.8Z"/>').join('')+'</g>':'';
 return `<svg class="${kind==='rod'?'rodIllustration':'fishIllustration'} e229HudComposition e230HudComposition" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" role="img" aria-label="${esc(label||id)}" data-foreground="alpha" data-specimen-category="${category}"><defs><pattern id="${key}-water" width="${w}" height="${h}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse" viewBox="0 0 ${water.width} ${water.height}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice"><use href="#${water.symbol}" width="${water.width}" height="${water.height}"/></pattern>${glints?`<mask id="${key}-body" mask-type="alpha" maskUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}"><use href="#${f.symbol}" width="${w}" height="${h}"/></mask>`:''}</defs><rect width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="url(#${key}-water)"/><use class="e231Subject" href="#${f.symbol}" width="${w}" height="${h}"/>${dots}</svg>`;
}

function e230RenderQuests(body,defs,zone){
 const buttons=new Map();for(const row of body.querySelectorAll('[data-quest-key]')){const b=row.querySelector('[data-expansion-claim],[data-catalog-claim],[data-e187-claim],[data-e171-deliver],[data-e17-deliver],[data-e18-claim]');if(b)buttons.set(row.dataset.questKey,b);}
 const ready=q=>!q.done&&q.goals.every(g=>g.have>=g.need);
 const sorted=defs.map((q,i)=>({q,i})).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.q.done)-Number(b.q.done)||Number(ready(b.q))-Number(ready(a.q))||a.i-b.i);
 const fragment=document.createDocumentFragment();for(const {q} of sorted){const row=document.createElement('article');row.className='e216Quest e230Quest'+(q.done?' e229Completed':'')+(ready(q)?' e230Claimable':'');row.dataset.questKey=q.id;row.dataset.questId=q.id;row.title=q.note;
 const award=e227Illustration('quest',q.id)||e229Reward(q),reward=q.rodId?e194RodArt(q.rodId):award;
 const goals=q.goals.map(g=>{const ratio=q.done?1:Math.min(1,Math.max(0,g.have/g.need||0)),fish=(g.fishIds||[]).map(id=>{const f=E16_FISH.find(f=>f.id===id);return f?'<span class="e230GoalFish '+(e227Discovered(state,id)?'':'unknown')+'" title="'+esc(e227Discovered(state,id)?e228FishName(f):'Especie requerida aún no descubierta')+'">'+e189FishSVG(id)+'</span>':'';}).join('');return '<div class="e230Goal'+(q.done||g.have>=g.need?' done':'')+'">'+(fish?'<div class="e234GoalFishGroup">'+fish+'</div>':'')+'<span>'+esc(g.label)+'</span><b>'+(q.done?'✓':fmt(Math.min(g.have,g.need))+'/'+fmt(g.need))+'</b><progress max="1" value="'+ratio+'" aria-label="'+esc(g.label)+'"></progress></div>';}).join('');
 row.innerHTML='<div class="e230QuestSeal" aria-hidden="true">'+award+'</div><div class="e230QuestGoals"><strong>'+esc(q.title)+'</strong>'+goals+(q.goals.some(g=>g.kind==='delivery')?'<small class="e230Delivery">Entrega: consume peces del inventario.</small>':'')+'</div><div class="e230QuestReward"><span class="e230RewardArt">'+reward+'</span><b>'+esc(q.reward)+'</b><small>'+(q.done?'Completado':ready(q)?'Disponible para reclamar':'Pendiente')+'</small></div>';
 const b=buttons.get(q.id);if(b&&!q.done)row.querySelector('.e230QuestReward').append(b);fragment.append(row);
 }body.replaceChildren(fragment);
}

const e232BaseStats=e1101Stats;

function e232CaptureProfile(fish,rod,level,weight){
 const st=e1101Stats(rod),demand=clamp((fish.req-1)/99,0,1),handling=.65*st.control/64+.35*st.resilience/60;
 const shortfall=Math.max(0,.20+.90*demand-handling),load=Number.isFinite(weight)?clamp(weight/e190Capacity(rod),0,1):0;
 return{demand,shortfall,load,pressure:(1+.65*demand+.9*shortfall+.12*load)*(1-Math.min(.24,handling*.25))-Math.min(.03,Math.max(0,level-fish.req)*.0006),endurance:1+.85*shortfall+.35*load,loss:1+.65*shortfall+.25*load};
}

 const count=Number(r.categoryCounts[category]),countKnown=Object.hasOwn(r.categoryCounts,category)&&Number.isSafeInteger(count)&&count>=0;
 return{best:r.categories[category]||0,count:countKnown?count:0,countKnown,partial:r.countsPartial};
}

// Static surface accents: the alpha mask confines every reflection to the animal.
// No raster variants, full-image color filters, timers or extra animation loops.
function e232SpecimenLayer(symbol,key,category,width='100%',height='100%'){
 if(!['large','huge','legendary'].includes(category))return '';
 const rank={large:1,huge:2,legendary:3}[category],id=symbol.split('hud-fish-').pop(),color=['','#dcebcf','#a8e7ff','#ffd46e'][rank];
 let paths,points,anatomy;
 if(/calamar/.test(id)){anatomy='mantle-tentacles';paths=['M104 44Q138 17 170 33','M91 49Q61 43 35 63T18 69','M88 57Q65 80 43 71T23 75'];points=[[128,34],[151,32],[77,55],[54,70],[30,62]];}
 else if(/bagre/.test(id)){anatomy='back-whiskers';paths=['M38 41Q85 23 146 44','M40 52Q25 58 17 69','M43 55Q29 67 24 78','M113 38L129 23'];points=[[58,39],[84,34],[115,39],[35,58],[26,68]];}
 else if(/cangrejo|isopodo/.test(id)){anatomy='shell-claws';paths=['M72 43Q101 23 127 44','M45 45Q32 26 48 20','M147 43Q168 24 152 17','M78 59Q101 67 123 56'];points=[[87,42],[112,39],[42,29],[155,28],[104,58]];}
 else if(/mantarraya/.test(id)){anatomy='wings';paths=['M38 61Q64 37 96 42','M104 42Q145 34 169 61','M99 48Q109 66 116 82'];points=[[68,48],[136,48],[96,44],[113,66]];}
 else if(/anguila|morena|congrio/.test(id)){anatomy='sinuous-back';paths=['M25 62Q68 28 98 50T173 43','M36 65Q76 43 108 64'];points=[[49,48],[83,44],[111,56],[155,49]];}
 else{anatomy='scales-fins';const shift=[...id].reduce((n,c)=>n+c.charCodeAt(0),0)%9-4;paths=['M40 43Q90 '+(23+shift)+' 152 43','M75 47q6 9 12 0m5 4q6 9 12 0m5-9q6 9 12 0','M92 30L105 20M104 67L120 76'];points=[[65,40],[88,35+shift],[115,38],[139,47],[101,64]];}
 const strokes=paths.slice(0,rank===1?1:paths.length).map(d=>'<path d="'+d+'" fill="none" stroke="'+color+'" stroke-width="'+[0,1.1,2.6,3.3][rank]+'" stroke-linecap="round" opacity="'+[0,.48,.86,.95][rank]+'"/>').join('');
 const spots=rank>1?points.map(([x,y],i)=>'<ellipse cx="'+x+'" cy="'+y+'" rx="'+(rank===3?6:4)+'" ry="2.4" fill="'+color+'" opacity=".32"/>'+(rank===3&&i<4?'<path d="M'+x+' '+(y-3)+'l1 2 3 1-3 1-1 2-1-2-3-1 3-1Z" fill="#fff2ba"/>':'')).join(''):'';
 // Shared alpha threshold removes semi-transparent fringe; no blur/color filter
 // is applied to the picture. Anatomy strokes remain inside the opaque animal.
 return '<defs><mask id="'+key+'-body" mask-type="alpha" maskUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x="0" y="0" width="'+width+'" height="'+height+'"><use href="#'+symbol+'" width="'+width+'" height="'+height+'" filter="url(#argentum-specimen-edge)"/></mask></defs><g class="e231ScaleGlints e232SpecimenSurface" mask="url(#'+key+'-body)" data-category="'+category+'" data-anatomy="'+anatomy+'"><svg width="'+width+'" height="'+height+'" viewBox="0 0 200 100" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true">'+strokes+spots+'</svg></g>';
}

"use strict";
/* Colección visual vectorial autónoma: funciona offline y no modifica partidas. */
function e1113Hash(s){let h=2166136261;for(const c of String(s)){h=Math.imul(h^c.charCodeAt(0),16777619)}return h>>>0}
function e1113RodTheme(id){
 const k=String(id||'').toLowerCase();
 if(/tiempo|time/.test(k))return ['#2aaea9','#e1bb70','#77dce5','time'];
 if(/solar|sol/.test(k))return ['#b98a37','#ffe4a1','#f2bb52','sun'];
 if(/bruma|mist/.test(k))return ['#567c9d','#c4e5f5','#79c9ec','mist'];
 if(/cost|mar|ocean/.test(k))return ['#2f8caa','#e2d9a5','#6dd7d5','sea'];
 if(/guard|rio|river/.test(k))return ['#51745a','#e0b66f','#7bc4a3','river'];
 if(/bamb|bamboo/.test(k))return ['#718846','#e0c477','#a8ca64','bamboo'];
 if(/reforz|steel|acero|fibra/.test(k))return ['#536d85','#d8e3ec','#7cbdde','steel'];
 if(/esmer|laguna/.test(k))return ['#387b5b','#c5e3a1','#80e8aa','emerald'];
 if(/coral|nacar|arrecife/.test(k))return ['#ad6677','#ffe1c7','#f4a6a6','coral'];
 if(/abis|abyss|sombr/.test(k))return ['#594577','#d8b7ff','#b488ed','abyss'];
 if(/astral|celest/.test(k))return ['#5275af','#f4e2ac','#b6cfff','astral'];
 if(/colec|trofeo/.test(k))return ['#9a7844','#f2d78d','#e9bf6a','trophy'];
 if(/viejo|pescador/.test(k))return ['#825e3d','#d6ba83','#b99962','old'];
 return ['#687e81','#e2d2a7','#91b9b7','classic'];
}
function e194RodArt(id){
 const [body,metal,accent,kind]=e1113RodTheme(id),uid='ar'+e1113Hash(id).toString(36);

'use strict';
(function(){
 const ids=()=>typeof E165_ZONES!=='undefined'?E165_ZONES:[];
 function decorate(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.e1120Menu[data-type="zone"]').forEach(menu=>{
   const choices=menu.querySelectorAll('.e1120Choice');
   choices.forEach((btn,i)=>{
    if(btn.classList.contains('e1114ScenicChoice')||btn.classList.contains('e228ZoneChoice'))return;
    const zone=ids()[i];if(!zone)return;
    const art=window.e1114ZoneImages||{};
    if(typeof e227ZoneArt==='function'&&e227ZoneArt(zone)){btn.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',e227ZoneArt(zone,'e1114ZoneThumb'));btn.classList.add('e1114ScenicChoice');return;}
    const img=document.createElement('img');img.className='e1114ZoneThumb';
    img.alt='Paisaje de '+zone.name;img.loading='eager';img.src=e221ZoneImage(zone)||art.muelle||'';
    btn.insertBefore(img,btn.firstChild);btn.classList.add('e1114ScenicChoice');
   });
  });
 }
 const observer=new MutationObserver(decorate);
 function start(){observer.observe(document.body,{childList:true});decorate();}
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start,{once:true});else start();
})();

'use strict';
function e223FishBackdrop(art){
 if(typeof e230HudArt==='function')return;
 const source=art.querySelector(':scope>img,:scope>svg');if(!source)return;
 const backdrop=document.createElement('span');backdrop.className='e223FishBackdrop';backdrop.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
 if(source.tagName==='IMG')backdrop.style.backgroundImage='url("'+source.getAttribute('src')+'")';
 else if(source.querySelector('use')){const copy=source.cloneNode(true);copy.removeAttribute('role');copy.removeAttribute('aria-label');copy.removeAttribute('class');copy.setAttribute('preserveAspectRatio','xMidYMid slice');copy.querySelector('title')?.remove();copy.style.cssText='display:block;width:100%;height:100%';backdrop.append(copy);}
 // Transparent standalone vectors already integrate with the card's base color.
 else return;
 art.prepend(backdrop);
}

function e216QuestDefinitions(){
 const delivery=(id,q,reward,done,rodId)=>({id,rodId,zone:q.zone,title:q.title,reward,done,goals:[{kind:'level',fishIds:[],label:`Alcanzá habilidad de Pesca ${q.level??q.req}`,have:e16FishLevel(),need:q.level??q.req},...q.needs.map(n=>{const f=E16_FISH.find(f=>f.id===n.id);return{kind:'delivery',fishIds:[n.id],label:`Entregá ${n.qty} ${f?.name||n.id}`,have:e188FishStock(n.id),need:n.qty};})],note:`Habilidad de Pesca ${q.level??q.req}. Entrega de inventario: acepta cualquier tamaño y consume los peces.${q.kind==='xp'?' Mejora permanente activa: se aplica automáticamente a la XP de cada captura.':q.kind==='control'?' Mejora permanente activa: se aplica automáticamente a la barra de captura.':''}`});
 const result=[delivery('e17_viejo',E17_QUEST,E17_ROD.name,!!state.owned?.[E17_ROD.id],E17_ROD.id),...E171_QUESTS.map(q=>delivery(q.id,q,q.rod.name,!!state.owned?.[q.rod.id],q.rod.id)),...E187_QUESTS.map(q=>delivery(q.id,q,q.reward,!!state.ui?.e187Rewards?.[q.id]||(q.kind==='rod'&&!!state.owned?.[E187_ROD.id]),q.kind==='rod'?E187_ROD.id:null))];
 const w=state.ui?.fishingWeights?.byZone?.rio||{};
 result.push({id:'coleccionista',rodId:E18_ROD.id,zone:'rio',title:'El trofeo de las corrientes',reward:E18_ROD.name,done:!!state.owned?.[E18_ROD.id],note:'Capturas históricas del Río del Bosque; no consume peces. Se conserva el crédito de trofeo de las partidas antiguas.',goals:[{label:'Conseguí 5 capturas',have:w.catches||0,need:5},{label:'Descubrí 3 especies distintas',have:e234SpeciesCount(w,'rio'),need:3},{label:'Capturá un pez de al menos 8 kg',have:w.bestKg||0,need:8}]});
 for(const rod of E208_CATALOG.filter(r=>r.unlock)){const q=rod.unlock,p=e208QuestProgress(rod);result.push({id:rod.id,rodId:rod.id,zone:q.zone,title:q.title,reward:rod.name,done:!!state.owned?.[rod.id],note:'Capturas históricas de esta zona; los tres objetivos son necesarios. No consume peces.',goals:[{kind:'level',label:`Alcanzá habilidad de Pesca ${rod.req}`,have:e16FishLevel(),need:rod.req},{label:`Conseguí ${q.catches} capturas`,have:p.catches,need:q.catches},{label:`Descubrí ${q.species} especies distintas`,have:p.species,need:q.species},{label:`Capturá un pez de al menos ${e190Kg(q.bestKg)}`,have:p.bestKg,need:q.bestKg}]});}
 return result.concat(typeof e227QuestDefinitions==='function'?e227QuestDefinitions():[]).map(e232QuestDefinition);
}
function e216ZoneProgress(zone,definitions=e216QuestDefinitions()){
 const species=[...new Set(zone.fish)],record=state.ui?.e18Fishing?.bySpecies||{},weights=state.ui?.fishingWeights?.bySpecies||{};
 const discovered=species.filter(id=>e227Discovered(state,id));
 const accredited=!!state.ui?.e191Achievements?.[zone.id],quests=definitions.filter(q=>q.zone===zone.id),done=quests.filter(q=>q.done).length;
 const mainQuests=quests.filter(q=>q.masteryRole==='main'||E227_OLD_QUESTS.some(old=>old.id===q.id)),mainDone=mainQuests.filter(q=>q.done).length;
 const threshold=Math.ceil(species.length/2),raw=[accredited||discovered.length>=threshold,mainQuests.length?mainDone===mainQuests.length:accredited||discovered.length>0,accredited||discovered.length===species.length],earned=Number(state.ui?.fishingMasteryEarned?.[zone.id])||0,stars=raw.map((yes,i)=>yes||!!(earned&(1<<i)));
 return{zone,species,discovered,threshold,quests,done,mainQuests,mainDone,accredited,stars,count:stars.filter(Boolean).length,complete:stars.every(Boolean)};
}
function e216Stars(p){return `<span class="atlasStars" role="img" aria-label="${p.count} de 3 estrellas">${p.stars.map(done=>`<span class="${done?'earned':''}" aria-hidden="true">${done?'★':'☆'}</span>`).join('')}</span>`;}
function e216BadgeDetails(q){return `<h4>📜 ${esc(q.title)}</h4><ul>${q.goals.map(g=>`<li>✓ ${esc(g.label)}</li>`).join('')}</ul><p>🎁 ${esc(q.reward)}</p><small>${esc(q.note)} · Recompensa ya obtenida; no se vuelve a entregar.</small>`;}
function e216ZoneDetails(p,v){
 const z=p.zone,remaining=p.species.filter(id=>!p.discovered.includes(id)),image=e221ZoneImage(z);
 const goals=[`Descubrir ${p.threshold} especies · ${Math.min(p.discovered.length,p.threshold)}/${p.threshold}`,p.mainQuests.length?`Completar encargos principales · ${p.mainDone}/${p.mainQuests.length}`:'Primera especie · sin encargos principales',`Colección completa · ${p.discovered.length}/${p.species.length}`];
 const badges=p.quests.filter(q=>q.done),selected=badges.find(q=>q.id===v.badge);
 return `<section class="atlasZoneDetail" aria-label="Ficha de ${esc(z.name)}">${typeof e227ZoneArt==='function'&&e227ZoneArt(z)?e227ZoneArt(z,'atlasLandscape'):image?`<img class="atlasLandscape" src="${image}" alt="Paisaje de ${esc(z.name)}">`:''}<div class="atlasDetailHead"><h3>${esc(z.name)}</h3>${e216Stars(p)}${p.complete?'<span class="atlasMedal" title="Tres hitos completados">✺ Maestría de zona</span>':''}</div><div class="atlasCounts"><span title="Los descubrimientos se comparten con las colecciones existentes">Especies <b>${p.discovered.length}/${p.species.length}</b></span><span>Encargos <b>${p.done}/${p.quests.length}</b></span><span>${p.accredited?'Colección histórica acreditada':remaining.length?'Faltan '+remaining.length+' especies':'Colección completa'}</span></div><ol class="atlasMilestones">${goals.map((label,i)=>`<li class="${p.stars[i]?'done':''}"><b>${p.stars[i]?'★':'☆'}</b> ${esc(label)}</li>`).join('')}</ol><div class="atlasBadges" aria-label="Recompensas obtenidas">${badges.map(q=>`<button type="button" class="atlasBadge" data-atlas-badge="${q.id}" aria-expanded="${selected?.id===q.id}" title="${esc(q.title+' — '+q.goals.map(g=>g.label).join('; ')+' — Premio: '+q.reward)}">✦ ${esc(q.title)}</button>`).join('')||'<small>Sin insignias de encargos obtenidas.</small>'}${state.ui?.e191Achievements?.[z.id]?'<span class="atlasBadge collectionBadge" title="Premio de colección ya reclamado">◈ Explorador de zona</span>':''}</div>${e222QuestPreview(p,v)}${selected?`<div class="atlasBadgeDetail" tabindex="-1">${e216BadgeDetails(selected)}</div>`:''}</section>`;
}
function e216FilterAtlas(root){
 const v=e216View(),query=v.search.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase();let visible=0;
 root.querySelectorAll('[data-atlas-zone]').forEach(row=>{row.hidden=!(row.dataset.name.includes(query)&&(v.filter==='all'||(v.filter==='complete')===(row.dataset.complete==='true')));if(!row.hidden)visible++;});
 if(v.zone&&root.querySelector('[data-atlas-zone="'+v.zone+'"]').hidden){const row=root.querySelector('[data-atlas-zone="'+v.zone+'"]');row.classList.remove('selected');row.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');row.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');root.querySelector('.atlasSelection')?.remove();v.zone=null;v.badge=null;}
 root.querySelector('.atlasResults').textContent=visible+' zonas';
}
function e216RenderAtlas(root){
 if(root.querySelector('.atlasRows')){
  const v=e216View(),rows=root.querySelector('.atlasRows'),top=rows.scrollTop;
  rows.querySelector('.atlasSelection')?.remove();
  rows.querySelectorAll('[data-atlas-zone]').forEach(row=>{const selected=row.dataset.atlasZone===v.zone;row.classList.toggle('selected',selected);row.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(selected));row.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(selected));if(selected){const detail=document.createElement('div');detail.className='atlasSelection';detail.innerHTML=e216ZoneDetails(e216ZoneProgress(E165_ZONES.find(z=>z.id===v.zone)),v);row.after(detail);}});
  e216FilterAtlas(root);rows.scrollTop=top;return;
 }
 const v=e216View(),defs=e216QuestDefinitions(),zones=E165_ZONES.map(z=>e216ZoneProgress(z,defs));
 root.innerHTML=`<summary>✧ Atlas de Pesca <small>${zones.filter(p=>p.complete).length}/${zones.length} zonas dominadas · ${defs.filter(q=>q.done).length}/${defs.length} encargos</small></summary><div class="atlasTools"><input type="search" data-atlas-search placeholder="Buscar zona…" aria-label="Buscar zona del Atlas"><select data-atlas-filter aria-label="Filtrar Atlas"><option value="all">Todas</option><option value="pending">Pendientes</option><option value="complete">Completas</option></select><small class="atlasResults" aria-live="polite"></small></div><div class="atlasRows" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label="Zonas del Atlas">${zones.map(p=>`<button type="button" class="atlasRow${v.zone===p.zone.id?' selected':''}" data-atlas-zone="${p.zone.id}" data-name="${esc(p.zone.name.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase())}" data-complete="${p.complete}" aria-pressed="${v.zone===p.zone.id}" aria-expanded="${v.zone===p.zone.id}"><b>${esc(p.zone.name)}</b>${e216Stars(p)}<small>${p.complete?'✺ Maestría':p.discovered.length+'/'+p.species.length+' especies · '+p.done+'/'+p.quests.length+' encargos'}</small></button>${v.zone===p.zone.id?`<div class="atlasSelection">${e216ZoneDetails(p,v)}</div>`:''}`).join('')}</div>`;
 root.querySelector('[data-atlas-search]').value=v.search;root.querySelector('[data-atlas-filter]').value=v.filter;e216FilterAtlas(root);e221Header(root,'atlas','Atlas de Pesca',root.querySelector(':scope>summary small')?.textContent||'');
}
function e216AtlasNode(){
 const root=document.createElement('details');root.id='fishingAtlas';root.className='fishingAtlas';root.open=e216View().open;root.__characterState=state;e216RenderAtlas(root);
 root.addEventListener('toggle',()=>{if(root.isConnected&&root.__characterState===state)e216View().open=root.open;});
 root.addEventListener('input',event=>{if(event.target.matches('[data-atlas-search]')){e216View().search=event.target.value;e216FilterAtlas(root);}});
 root.addEventListener('change',event=>{if(event.target.matches('[data-atlas-filter]')){e216View().filter=event.target.value;e216FilterAtlas(root);}});
 root.addEventListener('click',event=>{const zone=event.target.closest('[data-atlas-zone]'),badge=event.target.closest('[data-atlas-badge]');if(!zone&&!badge)return;const v=e216View();if(zone){v.zone=v.zone===zone.dataset.atlasZone?null:zone.dataset.atlasZone;v.badge=null;v.focusQuest=null;}else{v.badge=v.badge===badge.dataset.atlasBadge?null:badge.dataset.atlasBadge;}e216RenderAtlas(root);if(zone)root.querySelector(`[data-atlas-zone="${zone.dataset.atlasZone}"]`)?.focus({preventScroll:true});else root.querySelector(`[data-atlas-badge="${badge.dataset.atlasBadge}"]`)?.focus({preventScroll:true});});
 return root;
}
function e216Remember(){
 const root=document.getElementById('fishingAtlas');if(!root||root.__characterState!==state)return;e216View().scrollTop=root.querySelector('.atlasRows')?.scrollTop||0;
 const v=e216View();v.open=root.open;v.quests=new Set([...document.querySelectorAll('.e216Quest[open]')].map(n=>n.dataset.questKey));

function e216CompactQuests(){
 const body=document.querySelector('.e194QuestBody');if(!body)return;
 const definitions=e216QuestDefinitions(),zone=e165Zone().id,v=e216View();
 for(const section of [...body.children]){
  if(section.classList.contains('e216Quest'))continue;
  const claim=section.querySelector('[data-catalog-claim],[data-e187-claim],[data-e171-deliver],[data-e17-deliver]');
  const id=section.dataset.questId||claim?.dataset.catalogClaim||claim?.dataset.e187Claim||claim?.dataset.e171Deliver||(section.matches('.e17Quest')?'e17_viejo':section.querySelector('[data-e18-claim]')?'coleccionista':null);
  const q=definitions.find(q=>q.zone===zone&&(q.id===id||q.title===section.querySelector('h3,b')?.textContent?.replace(/^[📜🏆\s]+/u,'').trim()));if(!q)continue;
  const goals=q.goals,done=goals.filter(g=>g.have>=g.need).length,progress=Math.round(100*goals.reduce((sum,g)=>sum+clamp(g.have/g.need,0,1),0)/goals.length);
  const detail=document.createElement('details');detail.className='e216Quest';detail.dataset.questKey=q.id;detail.open=v.quests.has(q.id);
  const summary=document.createElement('summary');summary.innerHTML=`<span><b>${esc(q.title)}</b><small>🎁 ${esc(q.reward)}</small></span><span class="questRowProgress"><small>${done}/${goals.length} objetivos</small><span class="questMiniTrack" role="progressbar" aria-label="Progreso de ${esc(q.title)}" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100" aria-valuenow="${progress}"><span style="width:${progress}%"></span></span></span>`;
  section.before(detail);detail.append(summary,section);detail.addEventListener('toggle',()=>{if(!detail.isConnected)return;const set=e216View().quests;detail.open?set.add(q.id):set.delete(q.id);});
 }
}
function e216RodComparison(rod){
 const current=e161Rod(),a=e1101Stats(rod),b=e1101Stats(current),fields=[['Atracción','speed','%'],['Suerte','luck','%'],['Control','control','%'],['Resiliencia','resilience','%'],['Trofeos','trophy','%'],['Capacidad','maxKg',' kg']];
 return fields.map(([label,key,unit])=>{const value=key==='control'?e190ControlPercent(a[key]):a[key],base=key==='control'?e190ControlPercent(b[key]):b[key],delta=Math.round((value-base)*100)/100,format=n=>e225StatFormat.format(Number(n)),diff=delta===0?'=':(delta>0?'▲':'▼')+format(Math.abs(delta))+(key==='maxKg'?' kg':' pp');return `<span class="rodCompare ${delta>0?'better':delta<0?'worse':'same'}" data-stat="${key}" data-delta="${delta}"><span>${label} ${format(value)}${unit}</span><b title="Diferencia respecto a la caña equipada">${diff}</b></span>`;}).join('');
}

 let parts='';

===== src/js/fishing/collection/122-e218-journal.js =====

'use strict';
const E218_CATEGORIES={normal:'Común',large:'Grande',huge:'Enorme',legendary:'Legendario'};
const e218Selected=new WeakMap();
function e220QualityKey(label){
 if(typeof label!=='string')return null;
 return ({'':'normal',normal:'normal',comun:'normal',large:'large',grande:'large',huge:'huge',enorme:'huge',legendary:'legendary',legendario:'legendary'})[label.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f!¡]/g,'').trim().toLowerCase()]||null;
}
function e218Record(id){
 const w=state.ui?.fishingWeights?.bySpecies?.[id]||{},h=state.ui?.e18Fishing?.bySpecies?.[id]||{},categories={};
 for(const key of Object.keys(E218_CATEGORIES)){const n=Number(w.categoryBestKg?.[key]);if(Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0)categories[key]=n;}
 const last=state.ui?.lastFishingCatch,weight=last?.id===id?Number(last.weightKg):0,realLast=Number.isFinite(weight)&&weight>0;
 const lastCategory=realLast&&Object.hasOwn(last,'quality')?e220QualityKey(last.quality):null;
 if(lastCategory)categories[lastCategory]=Math.max(categories[lastCategory]||0,weight);
 const historical=Number(w.bestKg),best=Math.max(Number.isFinite(historical)&&historical>0?historical:0,...Object.values(categories),realLast?weight:0);
 const matches=Object.keys(categories).filter(k=>categories[k]===best);
 let category=Object.hasOwn(E218_CATEGORIES,w.bestCategory)&&historical===best?w.bestCategory:null;
 if(!category&&lastCategory&&weight===best)category=lastCategory;
 if(!category&&matches.length===1)category=matches[0];
 if(category&&best>0)categories[category]=Math.max(categories[category]||0,best);
 return {count:Math.max(Number(h.total)||0,Number(w.catches)||0),best,category,categories,quality:h.best||'normal',categoryCounts:w.categoryCounts||h.categoryCounts||{},countsPartial:Number(w.categoryCountBaseline)>0||Object.values(w.categoryCounts||h.categoryCounts||{}).reduce((a,b)=>a+(Number(b)||0),0)<Math.max(Number(h.total)||0,Number(w.catches)||0)};
}
function e218Detail(f){
 const r=e218Record(f.id),panel=document.createElement('section');panel.className='e218Detail';panel.id='fishingSpeciesDetail';
 const zones=E165_ZONES.filter(z=>z.fish.includes(f.id)&&e16FishLevel()>=(z.req||1));
 panel.innerHTML='<div class="e218Art">'+e189FishSVG(f.id)+'</div><div><h3>'+(typeof e228FishName==='function'?e228FishName(f):f.name)+'</h3><p>'+fmt(r.count)+' capturas · '+zones.map(z=>z.name).join(' · ')+'</p><strong>Récord absoluto: '+(r.best?e190Kg(r.best):'Sin peso registrado')+(r.best?' · '+(E218_CATEGORIES[r.category]||'Categoría histórica no registrada'):'')+'</strong>'+(!r.category&&r.best?'<p class="journalHistorical">Peso histórico conservado. Su categoría no quedó registrada; las nuevas capturas completan las marcas por categoría.</p>':'')+'<div class="e218Records">'+Object.entries(E218_CATEGORIES).map(([key,label])=>'<div data-category="'+key+'"><b>'+label+'</b><span>'+(r.categories[key]?e190Kg(r.categories[key]):'Sin récord')+'</span></div>').join('')+'</div></div>';
 if(typeof e230StyleJournalCard==='function')e230StyleJournalCard(panel,f.id);
 return panel;
}
function e218PlaceDetail(grid,id){
 grid.querySelector('.e218Detail')?.remove();grid.querySelectorAll('[data-journal-fish]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(b.dataset.journalFish===id)));
 const selected=grid.querySelector('[data-journal-fish="'+id+'"]');if(!selected||selected.hidden)return;
 const cards=[...grid.querySelectorAll('[data-journal-fish]')].filter(c=>!c.hidden),top=selected.offsetTop;
 const last=cards.filter(c=>Math.abs(c.offsetTop-top)<3).at(-1)||selected;
 const fish=E16_FISH.find(f=>f.id===id);if(fish)last.after(e218Detail(fish));
}
const e220JournalViews=new WeakMap();
function e220JournalView(){if(!e220JournalViews.has(state))e220JournalViews.set(state,{search:'',zone:'all',sort:'difficulty',category:null});return e220JournalViews.get(state);}
function e220Normalize(text){return text.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().trim();}
function e220FilterJournal(list){
 const v=e220JournalView(),query=e220Normalize(v.search),cards=[...list.querySelectorAll('[data-journal-fish]')];
 list.querySelector('.e218Detail')?.remove();
 const records=new Map(cards.map(c=>{const r=e218Record(c.dataset.journalFish);return[c.dataset.journalFish,{...r,raw:r,totalCount:r.count,...e232JournalStats(r,v.category)}];}));
 const fish=new Map(E16_FISH.map(f=>[f.id,f])),difficulty=e222DifficultyReference();
 cards.sort((a,b)=>{const x=fish.get(a.dataset.journalFish),y=fish.get(b.dataset.journalFish),ra=records.get(x.id),rb=records.get(y.id),tie=()=>x.name.localeCompare(y.name,'es');
  if(v.sort==='difficulty'||v.sort==='difficultyHigh'){const d=(difficulty.get(y.id)?.chance??1)-(difficulty.get(x.id)?.chance??1)||(difficulty.get(x.id)?.level??0)-(difficulty.get(y.id)?.level??0)||tie()||x.id.localeCompare(y.id);return v.sort==='difficultyHigh'?-d:d;}
  if(v.sort==='name')return tie();
  if(v.sort==='valueHigh'||v.sort==='valueLow')return (v.sort==='valueHigh'?y.price-x.price:x.price-y.price)||tie();
  if(v.sort==='weightHigh'||v.sort==='weightLow'){if(!ra.best||!rb.best)return ra.best===rb.best?tie():!ra.best?1:-1;return(v.sort==='weightHigh'?rb.best-ra.best:ra.best-rb.best)||tie();}
  if(!ra.countKnown||!rb.countKnown)return ra.countKnown===rb.countKnown?tie():ra.countKnown?-1:1;
  return(v.sort==='least'?ra.count-rb.count:rb.count-ra.count)||tie();
 });
 let visible=0;
 for(const card of cards){const f=fish.get(card.dataset.journalFish),r=records.get(f.id),category=v.category,matchCategory=!category||category==='species'||!!r.categories[category]||r.category===category||(r.totalCount>0&&r.quality===category);e232JournalCaption(card,f,r.raw,category);card.hidden=!(matchCategory&&e220Normalize(f.name).includes(query)&&(v.zone==='all'||E165_ZONES.find(z=>z.id===v.zone)?.fish.includes(f.id)));if(!card.hidden)visible++;list.append(card);}
 const selected=e218Selected.get(state);if(selected&&!cards.some(c=>c.dataset.journalFish===selected&&!c.hidden))e218Selected.delete(state);
 list.querySelectorAll('[data-journal-fish]').forEach(c=>c.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false'));
 const empty=list.parentElement.querySelector('.journalEmpty');empty.hidden=visible>0;empty.textContent=cards.length?'No hay especies descubiertas con estos filtros.':'Tus especies aparecerán acá cuando captures peces.';
 list.parentElement.querySelector('[data-journal-results]').textContent=visible+' especies';
 for(const b of list.closest('details').querySelectorAll('[data-journal-category]'))b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(b.dataset.journalCategory===v.category));
 if(typeof e230StyleJournal==='function')e230StyleJournal(list,records);
 if(list.closest('details').open)e218PlaceDetail(list,e218Selected.get(state));
}

function e227Discovered(s,id){
 const legacy=E234_BY_ID.get(id);if(legacy&&(s.ui?.fishingRegionalLegacy34?.discovered?.[id]||Number(s.ui?.fishingWeights?.byZone?.[legacy.zone]?.species?.[legacy.from])>0))return true;

}
function e227HistoricalDone(s,q){return !!(q.rodId?s.owned?.[q.rodId]:s.ui?.e187Rewards?.[q.id]);}
function e227MigrateMastery(s){
 if(!s||s.ui?.fishingMasteryVersion>=27)return s;
 s.ui=s.ui||{};
 // New optional fields only. Never replace unknown UI data or existing earned bits.
 const earned=s.ui.fishingMasteryEarned&&typeof s.ui.fishingMasteryEarned==='object'&&!Array.isArray(s.ui.fishingMasteryEarned)?s.ui.fishingMasteryEarned:{};
 for(const zone of E227_MASTERY_BASELINE){
  const found=zone.speciesIds.filter(id=>e227Discovered(s,id)).length,accredited=!!s.ui.e191Achievements?.[zone.zoneId];
  const qs=E227_OLD_QUESTS.filter(q=>zone.mainQuestIds.includes(q.id));
  const stars=[accredited||found>=Math.ceil(zone.speciesIds.length/2),qs.length?qs.every(q=>e227HistoricalDone(s,q)):accredited||found>0,accredited||found===zone.speciesIds.length];
  const mask=stars.reduce((a,b,i)=>a|(b?1<<i:0),0);earned[zone.zoneId]=(Number(earned[zone.zoneId])||0)|mask;
 }
 s.ui.fishingMasteryEarned=earned;s.ui.fishingMasteryVersion=27;return s;
}
function e227EarnMastery(){
 if(!state)return;e227MigrateMastery(state);
 const defs=e216QuestDefinitions();for(const zone of E165_ZONES){const p=e216ZoneProgress(zone,defs),mask=p.stars.reduce((a,b,i)=>a|(b?1<<i:0),0);state.ui.fishingMasteryEarned[zone.id]=(Number(state.ui.fishingMasteryEarned[zone.id])||0)|mask;}
}
// Decode runs for every character, including inactive ones; no storage writes during decode.

function e227QuestGoal(q,goal){
 const zone=E165_ZONES.find(z=>z.id===q.zone),record=state.ui?.fishingWeights?.byZone?.[q.zone]||{};
 if(goal.kind==='speciesCount')return{label:`Capturá ${goal.need} ${E16_FISH.find(f=>f.id===goal.id).name} en ${zone.name}`,have:e234LocalCount(record,goal.id),need:goal.need};
 if(goal.kind==='captures')return{label:`Conseguí ${goal.need} capturas en ${zone.name}`,have:Number(record.catches)||0,need:goal.need};

 return pool[pool.length-1];
