===== src/js/fishing/feedback/120-e217-xp-effects.js =====

'use strict';
// Presentation events only: never poll saved XP to infer gains.
const e217PendingXP=new Map(),e217ActiveFX=new Map();let e217FlushFrame=0,e217EffectFrame=0;
function e217LiveXP(s){return s===state&&ready&&storageReady&&!busy&&!managing&&!OFF.active&&!simulationLocked()&&!labFrame&&!document.hidden;}
function e217XPEvent(s,key,amount,before,after){
 if(!Number.isFinite(amount)||!(amount>0)||!e217LiveXP(s))return;
 if(key==='pesca'){before=Math.min(100,before);after=Math.min(100,after);}
 const event=e217PendingXP.get(key);
 if(event&&event.s===s){event.amount+=amount;event.after=Math.max(event.after,after);}
 else e217PendingXP.set(key,{s,key,amount,before,after});
 if(!e217FlushFrame)e217FlushFrame=requestAnimationFrame(e217FlushXP);
}
function e217HasLevelFeedback(s){return e217LiveXP(s)&&[...e217PendingXP.values()].some(e=>e.s===s&&e.after>e.before&&e217XPTarget(e.key));}
function e217Visible(node){
 if(!node||!node.isConnected||!node.getClientRects().length||getComputedStyle(node).visibility==='hidden')return false;
 const r=node.getBoundingClientRect();return r.width>0&&r.height>0&&r.bottom>0&&r.top<innerHeight&&r.right>0&&r.left<innerWidth;
}
function e217XPTarget(key){
 const selectors=key==='pesca'?['#e188FishingHud #dockPlayerXpText','#page_fishing .e187XpLabel','.e197FishingSkills .e197Line b']:
 key==='character'?['#combatDock:not(.e188FishingMode) #dockPlayerXpText','#xpText']:[`#skillsBox [data-skill-key="${key}"] small`];
 for(const selector of selectors){const node=document.querySelector(selector);if(!e217Visible(node))continue;
  const bar=key==='pesca'?(node.closest('.e163XpTrack')||document.querySelector(node.closest('#combatDock')?'#combatDock .dockProgress .bar':'.e197Track')):
   key==='character'?(node.closest('.dockProgress')?.querySelector('.bar')||document.getElementById('xpBar')?.parentElement):node.closest('.skillCard')?.querySelector('.bar');
  if(bar&&e217Visible(bar))return{node,bar};
 }
 return null;
}
function e217EffectRoot(){let root=document.getElementById('e217XpEffects');if(!root){root=document.createElement('div');root.id='e217XpEffects';root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');document.body.append(root);}return root;}
function e217FormatXP(n){return Number(n.toFixed(2)).toLocaleString('es-AR');}
function e217SkillLabel(key){return key==='pesca'?'Pesca':key==='character'?'Personaje':key==='weapon'?'Armas':SKILLS.find(s=>s.key===key)?.name||key;}
function e217RemoveFX(id){const f=e217ActiveFX.get(id);if(!f)return;f.node.remove();f.arrow?.remove();f.bar?.classList.remove('e217LevelGlow');e217ActiveFX.delete(id);}
function e217StartFX(event,kind){
 const id=event.key+':'+kind,now=performance.now(),previous=e217ActiveFX.get(id);
 const amount=event.amount;
 if(previous&&kind==='drop'&&previous.s===event.s){
  if(now-previous.started<160){previous.amount+=amount;previous.node.textContent='+'+e217FormatXP(previous.amount)+' XP';}
  else if(previous.next)previous.next.amount+=amount;
  else previous.next={...event};
  return;
 }
 if(previous)e217RemoveFX(id);
 const node=document.createElement('span');node.className=kind==='drop'?'e217XpDrop':'e217LevelNotice';node.dataset.xpSkill=event.key;
 node.textContent=kind==='drop'?'+'+e217FormatXP(amount)+' XP':'¡'+e217SkillLabel(event.key).toLocaleUpperCase('es-AR')+' NIVEL '+event.after+'!';
 let arrow=null;if(kind==='level'){arrow=document.createElement('span');arrow.className='e221LevelArrow';arrow.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 16 20" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 1 1 9h4v10h6V9h4Z" fill="#f0cc70" stroke="#8e6028" stroke-width="1"/></svg>';e217EffectRoot().append(arrow);}
 e217EffectRoot().append(node);e217ActiveFX.set(id,{...event,node,arrow,amount,kind,started:now,updated:now,until:now+(kind==='drop'?1700:2000),bar:null});
}
function e217FlushXP(){
 e217FlushFrame=0;
 for(const event of e217PendingXP.values())if(e217LiveXP(event.s)&&e217XPTarget(event.key)){e217StartFX(event,'drop');if(event.after>event.before)e217StartFX(event,'level');}
 e217PendingXP.clear();if(e217ActiveFX.size&&!e217EffectFrame)e217EffectFrame=requestAnimationFrame(e217PositionFX);
}
function e221TextRect(node){const range=document.createRange();range.selectNodeContents(node);return range.getBoundingClientRect();}
function e221LevelPosition(f,target){
 const b=target.bar.getBoundingClientRect(),hud=target.node.closest('#e188FishingHud');
 const mainCandidate=f.key==='pesca'?document.querySelector('#page_fishing .e163XpTrack'):null,mainRect=mainCandidate?.getBoundingClientRect();
 const layoutKey=[b.x,b.y,b.width,b.height,innerWidth,innerHeight,mainRect?.x,mainRect?.y,mainRect?.width,mainRect?.height,hud?.querySelector('#e188HudResult')?.textContent].join('|');
 if(f.layout?.key===layoutKey)return f.layout.position;
 const blockers=hud?[...hud.querySelectorAll('#e188HudTitle,#e188HudResult,#e188HudPercent,.e214CaptureStatus')].map(e221TextRect):[];
 if(hud)blockers.push(...[...document.querySelectorAll('#combatDock .e189Art,#combatDock .e194DockRod,#e188FishingHud .e188Track')].map(n=>n.getBoundingClientRect()));
 let anchor=target.bar;
 const main=f.key==='pesca'?document.querySelector('#page_fishing .e163XpTrack'):null;
 if(hud&&innerWidth<650&&e217Visible(main))anchor=main;
 let rect=anchor.getBoundingClientRect();f.node.style.fontSize=(innerWidth<650?11:13)+'px';
 let w=f.node.getBoundingClientRect().width,h=f.node.getBoundingClientRect().height,left=rect.left+(rect.width-w)/2,top=rect.bottom+2;
 if(anchor===target.bar&&hud){
  const boundary=hud.getBoundingClientRect(),overlaps=(x,y,width,height)=>blockers.some(r=>x<r.right&&x+width>r.left&&y<r.bottom&&y+height>r.top);
  let placed=false;
  for(const size of [13,11,10,9,8]){f.node.style.fontSize=size+'px';const box=f.node.getBoundingClientRect();w=box.width;h=box.height;left=rect.left+(rect.width-w)/2;
   for(let y=rect.bottom+2;y+h<=boundary.bottom;y+=2)if(!overlaps(left,y,w,h)){top=y;placed=true;break;}if(placed)break;
  }
  if(!placed){top=rect.bottom+2;f.node.style.fontSize='8px';}
 }
 f.node.dataset.anchor=anchor===main?'main':'hud';
 if(f.arrow){const level=hud?document.getElementById('dockPlayerLevel'):f.key==='character'?document.getElementById('dockPlayerLevel'):target.node.closest('.skillCard')?.querySelector('.row b');
  if(level){const r=e221TextRect(level);f.arrow.style.left=Math.min(innerWidth-15,r.right+4)+'px';f.arrow.style.top=Math.max(1,r.top-11)+'px';}
 }
 const position={left,top};f.layout={key:layoutKey,position};return position;
}
function e217PositionFX(){
 e217EffectFrame=0;const now=performance.now();
 for(const [id,f] of e217ActiveFX){
  const target=e217LiveXP(f.s)?e217XPTarget(f.key):null;
  if(now>=f.until||!target){const next=f.next;e217RemoveFX(id);if(target&&next&&e217LiveXP(next.s))e217StartFX(next,'drop');continue;}
  if(f.kind==='drop'&&f.anchor!==target.node){const style=getComputedStyle(target.node);f.node.style.fontFamily=style.fontFamily;f.node.style.fontSize=(parseFloat(style.fontSize)*.96)+'px';f.node.style.fontWeight=style.fontWeight;f.node.style.lineHeight=style.lineHeight;f.anchor=target.node;}
  const range=document.createRange();range.selectNodeContents(target.node);const r=range.getBoundingClientRect(),b=target.bar.getBoundingClientRect(),w=f.node.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  const celebration=f.kind==='level'?e221LevelPosition(f,target):null;
  const left=f.kind==='drop'?r.left-w-6:celebration.left,top=f.kind==='drop'?r.top:celebration.top;
  f.node.style.left=Math.max(6,Math.min(innerWidth-w-6,left))+'px';f.node.style.top=Math.max(6,top)+'px';
  if(f.kind==='level'&&f.bar!==target.bar){f.bar?.classList.remove('e217LevelGlow');f.bar=target.bar;f.bar.classList.add('e217LevelGlow');}
 }
 if(e217ActiveFX.size)e217EffectFrame=requestAnimationFrame(e217PositionFX);else document.getElementById('e217XpEffects')?.remove();
}

function e219ClearXPEffects(){
 cancelAnimationFrame(e217FlushFrame);cancelAnimationFrame(e217EffectFrame);e217FlushFrame=0;e217EffectFrame=0;
 e217PendingXP.clear();for(const id of [...e217ActiveFX.keys()])e217RemoveFX(id);
 document.getElementById('e217XpEffects')?.remove();
}
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)e219ClearXPEffects();});

function e217CleanClone(page){page.querySelectorAll('#e217XpEffects,.e1120Menu').forEach(n=>n.remove());page.querySelectorAll('.e217LevelGlow').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('e217LevelGlow'));}

/* Decorative vector sprites are built locally: no network, no asset dependencies. */
function e189FishSVG(id){
 const key=(id||'').toLowerCase();
 const long=/anguila|morena|serpiente/.test(key),flat=/mantarraya|raya/.test(key),spiky=/pez_espada|lucio|tiburon|leviatan/.test(key);
 const palette=/salmon|trucha/.test(key)?['#f2a16f','#e05d58','#ffe3b2']:/atun|pez_espada|tiburon/.test(key)?['#8cb4cb','#3d7199','#d2e9f2']:/koi|carpa_dorada|pez_luna/.test(key)?['#f7ca65','#e9853b','#fff1b2']:/fantasma|abisal|cristal|luminoso|leviatan/.test(key)?['#b0d9ed','#6a8db7','#e9ffff']:/perca|lucio|esturion/.test(key)?['#91bc80','#407d67','#d7e9a4']:['#8ec9bd','#388e9d','#d7f6e8'];
 const [base,dark,light]=palette;
 const body=flat?'<path d="M13 37 Q38 7 70 29 L83 48 Q53 49 46 59 L35 49 Q24 54 13 37Z"/>':long?'<path d="M10 35 Q26 11 50 27 T80 30 Q86 45 65 49 T27 45 Q15 48 10 35Z"/>':'<path d="M12 35 Q32 11 64 23 L82 13 L78 35 L83 55 L64 47 Q35 63 12 35Z"/>';
 const fins=flat?'':long?'':'<path d="M34 23 L42 9 L56 22Z" fill="'+dark+'"/><path d="M41 49 L54 61 L62 46Z" fill="'+dark+'"/>';
 const nose=spiky?'<path d="M12 35 L1 31 L13 38" fill="'+dark+'"/>':'';
 return '<svg viewBox="0 0 94 70" role="img" aria-label="Ilustración de pez" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><defs><linearGradient id="e189fishgrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0.7" y2="1"><stop stop-color="'+light+'"/><stop offset=".48" stop-color="'+base+'"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="'+dark+'"/></linearGradient></defs>'+fins+'<g fill="url(#e189fishgrad)" stroke="'+dark+'" stroke-width="1.8">'+body+'</g>'+nose+'<path d="M24 33 Q45 42 67 33" stroke="'+dark+'" stroke-width="1" opacity=".45" fill="none"/><circle cx="24" cy="31" r="3.2" fill="#172f3a"/><circle cx="23.2" cy="30.2" r="1" fill="#fff"/><path d="M11 41 L19 42" stroke="'+dark+'" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>';
}

(function(){
 function updateFishingXp(){

  if(bar){bar.style.width=p.percent.toFixed(2)+'%';bar.parentElement.title=hint;bar.parentElement.setAttribute('aria-label','Experiencia de pesca');}
 }

===== src/js/fishing/minigame/016-e16-fishing.js =====

"use strict";
/* E1.6 pesca activa. Extensión aditiva compatible con guardados v7. */
UI_TABS.push(["fishing","Pesca"]);
const E16_ROD_PRICE=35;
/* @include src/catalogs/016-e16-fishing-e16_fish.js */
let e16Session=null,e16Holding=false,e16Frame=0,e16Last=0,e16Result="";
function e16FishSkill(){if(!state)return {xp:0};state.skills=state.skills||{};return state.skills.pesca||(state.skills.pesca={xp:0});}
function e16FishLevel(){return Math.min(100,skillLvl(e16FishSkill().xp));}
function e16FishProgress(){
 const xp=e16FishSkill().xp,level=e16FishLevel(),max=level===100;
 const low=10*(level-1)**2,high=10*level**2;
 return {xp,level,max,earned:Math.max(0,xp-low),need:high-low,left:max?0:Math.max(0,high-xp),percent:max?100:clamp(100*(xp-low)/(high-low),0,100)};
}
// Pesos propios de Argentum: kg físicos, separados de rareza y calidad de venta.
/* @include src/catalogs/016-e16-fishing-e190_weight_ranges.js */
function e190WeightRange(fish){return E190_WEIGHT_RANGES[fish.id];}
function e190Capacity(rod=e161Rod()){return E1103_SPECIAL[rod?.id]?.maxKg||25;}
function e190CanLift(fish,rod=e161Rod()){return e190WeightRange(fish)[0]<=e190Capacity(rod);}
const e225StatFormat=new Intl.NumberFormat('es-AR',{maximumFractionDigits:2});
function e190Kg(kg){return e225StatFormat.format(Number(kg))+' kg';}
function e190SampleWeight(fish,rod=e161Rod(),random=Math.random){
 const [low,maximum]=e190WeightRange(fish),high=Math.min(maximum,e190Capacity(rod));
 if(high<low)return null;
 return Math.round((low+random()*(high-low))*100)/100;
}
function e190TrackWidth(){return Math.max(60,(document.getElementById('e16Tank')?.clientWidth||520)-6);}
function e190ControlPercent(control){return control/5;}
function e190CaptureWidth(fish,rod=e161Rod(),level=e16FishLevel(),width=e190TrackWidth(),abismo=!!e187Rewards().abismo){
 const st=e1101Stats(rod),demand=Math.max(0,(fish.req-1)/99),difficulty=E161_DIFFICULTY[fish.id]||1;
 const fraction=clamp(.15+st.control/500+Math.min(.012,(level-1)/8250)-(difficulty-1)*.018-demand*.045,.075,.30);
 // The historical +8px reward is 8px at the 520px reference, proportional on mobile.
 return Math.max(16,width*(fraction+(abismo?8/520:0)));
}
function e190MotionProfile(fish,rod=e161Rod()){
 const resilience=clamp(e1101Stats(rod).resilience,0,60)/100;
 return {distance:1-resilience*.5,interval:1+resilience*2};
}

function e190Weights(){
 state.ui=state.ui||{};
 return state.ui.fishingWeights||(state.ui.fishingWeights={bySpecies:{},byZone:{}});
}
function e190RecordWeight(catchInfo){
 if(!Number.isFinite(catchInfo.weightKg))return;
 const fresh=!state.ui?.fishingWeights,weights=e190Weights(),id=catchInfo.fish.id;
 if(fresh)weights.legacyRiverTrophy=!!catchInfo.legacyRiverTrophy;
 const record=(target)=>{target.catches=(target.catches||0)+1;target.totalKg=Number(((target.totalKg||0)+catchInfo.weightKg).toFixed(2));target.bestKg=Math.max(target.bestKg||0,catchInfo.weightKg);};
 weights.bySpecies=weights.bySpecies||{};weights.byZone=weights.byZone||{};
 const speciesRecord=weights.bySpecies[id]||(weights.bySpecies[id]={});
 const known=e218Record(id),previousBest=known.best;
 catchInfo.newAbsoluteRecord=previousBest>0&&catchInfo.weightKg>previousBest;
 speciesRecord.bestKg=previousBest;speciesRecord.categoryBestKg={...speciesRecord.categoryBestKg,...known.categories};if(known.category)speciesRecord.bestCategory=known.category;
 const priorCount=Math.max(Number(speciesRecord.catches)||0,(Number(state.ui?.e18Fishing?.bySpecies?.[id]?.total)||0)-1);
 if(!speciesRecord.categoryCounts){
  const historical=state.ui?.e18Fishing?.bySpecies?.[id]?.categoryCounts||{};
  speciesRecord.categoryCounts=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(historical).filter(([key,n])=>Object.hasOwn(E218_CATEGORIES,key)&&Number.isSafeInteger(n)&&n>=0));
  speciesRecord.categoryCountBaseline=Math.max(0,priorCount-Object.values(speciesRecord.categoryCounts).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0));
 }
 record(speciesRecord);
 const label=e183LastQuality?.fish===catchInfo.fish.name?e183LastQuality.label:'';
 const category=label.includes('LEGENDARIO')?'legendary':label.includes('ENORME')?'huge':label.includes('Grande')?'large':'normal';
 speciesRecord.categoryCounts[category]=(Number(speciesRecord.categoryCounts[category])||0)+1;
 speciesRecord.categoryBestKg=speciesRecord.categoryBestKg||{};
 speciesRecord.categoryBestKg[category]=Math.max(Number(speciesRecord.categoryBestKg[category])||0,catchInfo.weightKg);
 if(catchInfo.weightKg>previousBest)speciesRecord.bestCategory=category;
 const zone=weights.byZone[catchInfo.zone]||(weights.byZone[catchInfo.zone]={species:{}});
 zone.species=zone.species||{};zone.species[id]=(zone.species[id]||0)+1;record(zone);
 weights.last={id,weightKg:catchInfo.weightKg,category,zone:catchInfo.zone,rodId:catchInfo.rodId};
 if(e182LastCatch)e182LastCatch.weightKg=catchInfo.weightKg;
}

function e16Paint(){if(!e16Session)return;const g=e16Session;const zone=document.getElementById('e16Zone'),target=document.getElementById('e16Target'),meter=document.getElementById('e16Progress');if(zone){zone.style.bottom=g.pos+'px';zone.style.height=g.fish.zone+'px';}if(target)target.style.bottom=g.target+'px';if(meter)meter.style.height=Math.round(g.progress*100)+'%';}

function e16Animate(now){if(!e16Session)return;const g=e16Session,dt=Math.min(.05,Math.max(0,(now-e16Last)/1000));e16Last=now;
 // Movimiento de la barra con inercia y gravedad; objetivo con cambios de dirección.

 e16Frame=requestAnimationFrame(e16Animate);
}

}

function e161RandomFish(){

 const eligible=E16_FISH.filter(f=>lvl>=f.req&&rod>=Math.max(0,Math.floor((f.req-1)/10)));
 const weights=eligible.map(f=>Math.max(.12,12/Math.pow(1+(lvl-f.req)/4,0.4)/Math.pow(E161_DIFFICULTY[f.id],2.3)));
 let n=Math.random()*weights.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
 for(let i=0;i<eligible.length;i++){n-=weights[i];if(n<=0)return eligible[i];}
 return eligible[eligible.length-1]||E16_FISH[0];
}

e16Animate=function(now){

 const diff=E161_DIFFICULTY[g.fish.id]||1;
 if(g.time>=g.burstAt){g.burst=(Math.random()*.42+.25)*diff*(Math.random()<.5?-1:1);g.burstAt=g.time+.45+Math.random()*.75/diff;}
 g.target+=(g.dir*(58+g.fish.speed*120)*diff*.53+g.burst*55+Math.sin(g.time*6+g.seed)*g.fish.wiggle*48)*dt;
 if(g.target<3){g.target=3;g.dir=1;g.burst=Math.abs(g.burst);}if(g.target>width-30){g.target=width-30;g.dir=-1;g.burst=-Math.abs(g.burst);}
 if(g.time>=g.turnAt){g.dir=Math.random()<.5?-1:1;g.turnAt=g.time+.55+Math.random()*.85/diff;}
 const inside=g.target+12>=g.pos&&g.target+12<=g.pos+g.fish.zone;
 g.progress=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,g.progress+(inside?.19:-.085*diff)*dt));
 e16Paint();if(g.progress>=1)return e16Finish(true);if(g.time>4&&g.progress<=0)return e16Finish(false);
 e16Frame=requestAnimationFrame(e16Animate);
};
e16Finish=function(won){
 const g=e16Session;if(!g)return;e16Session=null;e16Holding=false;cancelAnimationFrame(e16Frame);
 if(won){
  const id='pez_'+g.fish.id;state.inventory=state.inventory||{};state.inventory[id]=(state.inventory[id]||0)+1;
  const gained=sklXp(state,'pesca',Math.max(1,Math.round(g.fish.xp * e181XpFactor())));
  e16Result=`🎉 ¡Capturaste ${g.fish.name}! +${fmt(gained)} XP de Pesca · Valor de venta: ${fmt(g.fish.price)} oro (vendelo al mercader).`;
  addLog('Pesca: '+e16Result);persist();updateInv();/* Resultado visible dentro del panel de pesca; sin toast duplicado. */
 }else{e16Result='💨 ¡El pez escapó! Pronto habrá otra picada.';/* Escape informado dentro del panel de pesca. */}
 e16Page();if(e161Fishing&&e161OnPage())e161NextBite();
};
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
 const buy=e.target.closest('[data-e161-buy]'),toggle=e.target.closest('[data-e161-toggle]');if(!buy&&!toggle)return;
 e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(!state||!ready||busy||managing||!storageReady||simulationLocked())return;
 if(buy){const i=Number(buy.dataset.e161Buy),r=E161_RODS[i];if(!r||i!==e161RodIndex()+1||e16FishLevel()<r.req||state.gold<r.price)return;state.gold-=r.price;state.owned=state.owned||{};state.owned[r.id]=true;persist();renderGame();toast('¡Compraste '+r.name+'!');}
 if(toggle){if(e161Fishing){e195Reset();e161Fishing=false;e161StopWait();if(e16Session){cancelAnimationFrame(e16Frame);e16Session=null;e16Holding=false;}e16Result='Guardaste la caña.';e16Page();}else{if(!e161Rod()||!e161CanFish())return;e161Fishing=true;e182LastCatch=null;e16Result='';e161NextBite();}}
},true);


===== src/js/fishing/minigame/021-e163-fishing.js =====

"use strict";
/* E1.6.4: corrige geometría, añade peces especiales y simplifica inventario. */
/* @include src/catalogs/021-e163-fishing-e16_fish-additions-1.js */
Object.assign(E161_DIFFICULTY,{carpa_dorada:1.26,trucha_arcoiris:1.55,pez_fantasma:1.95});
// Pesca aleatoria: los especiales pueden picar con caña básica cuando cumplen nivel y caña.
e161RandomFish=function(){
 const lvl=e16FishLevel(),rod=e161RodIndex();
 const eligible=E16_FISH.filter(f=>lvl>=f.req&&rod>=Math.max(0,Math.floor((f.req-1)/10)));
 const weights=eligible.map(f=>f.rarity?f.rarity:Math.max(.12,1/Math.pow(1+(lvl-f.req)/4,.4)/Math.pow(E161_DIFFICULTY[f.id]||1,2.3)));
 let n=Math.random()*weights.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
 for(let i=0;i<eligible.length;i++){n-=weights[i];if(n<=0)return eligible[i];}
 return eligible[0]||E16_FISH[0];
};
// Evita que la barra y el pez queden fuera del recorrido visible.
const e163Paint=e16Paint;
e16Paint=function(){
 if(fishingRenderDepth)return; // Track geometry is valid after the full page is assembled.
 if(e16Session){const track=document.getElementById('e16Tank');if(track){const width=e190TrackWidth();e16Session.pos=Math.min(Math.max(0,e16Session.pos),Math.max(0,width-e16Session.fish.zone-4));e16Session.target=Math.min(Math.max(3,e16Session.target),width-30);}}
 e163Paint();
};
// El nivel se actualiza en la barra junto al minijuego, y no solo en el panel inferior.
const e163Finish=e16Finish;
e16Finish=function(won){e163Finish(won);if(won&&state){const el=document.getElementById('e163XpText');if(el)el.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');}};
// Reordena inventario: primero oro y valor vendible, después los listados con sus botones.
function e163Inventory(){
 const page=document.getElementById('page_inventory');if(!page||!state)return;
 const inv=state.inventory||{};
 let total=0,units=0;
 for(const [id,qty] of Object.entries(inv)){
  if(!Number.isSafeInteger(qty)||qty<=0)continue;
  const fish=E16_FISH.find(f=>'pez_'+f.id===id);
  const price=fish?.price??E15_PRICES[id];
  if(Number.isFinite(price)){total+=qty*price;units+=qty;}
 }
 let summary=document.getElementById('e163InvSummary');
 if(!summary){summary=document.createElement('div');summary.id='e163InvSummary';summary.className='e163InvSummary';}
 summary.innerHTML='<b>🎒 Inventario y ventas</b><div style="margin-top:5px">Oro disponible: <strong>'+fmt(state.gold)+'</strong> · Valor total vendible: <strong>'+fmt(total)+'</strong> oro · '+fmt(units)+' unidades</div><small class="muted">El valor total es estimado: recibís el oro cuando vendés los objetos.</small>';
 page.prepend(summary);
 const mats=document.getElementById('e15Merchant'),fish=document.getElementById('e16FishMerchant');
 if(mats)summary.after(mats);
 if(fish)(mats||summary).after(fish);
}
const e163Merchant=e15MerchantPanel;

const e163FishMerchant=e16FishMerchant;

===== src/js/fishing/minigame/023-e164-update.js =====

"use strict";
/* Conserva el formato de guardados v7: se agrega solo una preferencia opcional en ui. */
const E164_ROD_STATS=[
 {control:0,wait:0,rare:0,label:'Equilibrada para aprender'},
 {control:12,wait:0,rare:0,label:'Más control en cada captura'},
 {control:22,wait:12,rare:0,label:'Más control y picadas más rápidas'},
 {control:32,wait:18,rare:12,label:'Mayor control y oportunidad de peces raros'}
];
function e164SelectedRodIndex(){
 const best=e161RodIndex();if(best<0)return -1;
 const id=state?.ui?.fishingRodId;
 const idx=E161_RODS.findIndex(r=>r.id===id&&state.owned?.[r.id]);
 return idx>=0?idx:best;
}
e161Rod=function(){return E161_RODS[e164SelectedRodIndex()]||null;};
// Las cañas superiores tienen una identidad propia, no solamente una compra automática.
const e164NextBite=e161NextBite;
e161NextBite=function(){
 e164NextBite();
 if(e161Waiting!==null&&e161Fishing){
  const idx=e164SelectedRodIndex();const reduction=(E164_ROD_STATS[idx]?.wait||0)/100;
  if(reduction>0){const remaining=Math.max(1200,e161CatchAt-Date.now());
   clearTimeout(e161Waiting);e161CatchAt=Date.now()+Math.max(1200,Math.round(remaining*(1-reduction)));
   e161Waiting=setTimeout(()=>{e161Waiting=null;e161StopWait();if(!e161Fishing||!e161OnPage()||!e161CanFish())return;const f=e161RandomFish();e16Start(f.id);},Math.max(1200,e161CatchAt-Date.now()));
  }
 }
};
const e164RandomFish=e161RandomFish;
e161RandomFish=function(){
 const idx=e164SelectedRodIndex(),rare=(E164_ROD_STATS[idx]?.rare||0)/100;
 if(rare>0&&Math.random()<rare){
  const eligible=E16_FISH.filter(f=>f.rarity&&e16FishLevel()>=f.req&&e161RodIndex()>=Math.max(0,Math.floor((f.req-1)/10)));
  if(eligible.length)return eligible[Math.floor(Math.random()*eligible.length)];
 }
 return e164RandomFish();
};
function e164RenderRods(){
 const details=document.querySelector('#page_fishing details:has(.fishRod)');if(!details||!state)return;
 const selected=e164SelectedRodIndex(),best=e161RodIndex(),lvl=e16FishLevel();
 details.querySelectorAll('.fishRod').forEach((row,i)=>{
  const r=E161_RODS[i],st=E164_ROD_STATS[i];if(!r)return;
  row.classList.toggle('e164RodActive',i===selected);
  const info=row.querySelector('div');
  if(info&&!info.querySelector('.e164RodStat')){const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='e164RodStat';el.textContent='Control +'+e190ControlPercent(st.control)+'% de barra · Picada '+(st.wait?'−'+st.wait+'%':'normal')+' · Raros '+(st.rare?'+'+st.rare+'% oportunidad extra':'sin bonificación')+' · '+st.label;info.append(el);}
  if(state.owned?.[r.id]){
   const old=row.querySelector('span.fishMuted');if(old)old.remove();
   let button=row.querySelector('[data-e164-equip]');if(!button){button=document.createElement('button');button.className='small';button.dataset.e164Equip=String(i);row.append(button);}
   button.textContent=i===selected?'✓ Equipada':'Equipar';button.disabled=i===selected;
  }
 });
 const summary=details.querySelector('summary');if(summary)summary.innerHTML='<b>🎣 Cañas y estadísticas</b> · '+(e161Rod()?.name||'Sin caña');
}
function e164Inventory(){
 const page=document.getElementById('page_inventory');if(!page||!state)return;
 let panel=document.getElementById('e164Inventory');
 if(!panel){panel=document.createElement('section');panel.id='e164Inventory';panel.className='panel';}
 const inv=state.inventory||{};
 const rows=Object.entries(inv).filter(([id,n])=>Number.isSafeInteger(n)&&n>0&&(Object.hasOwn(E15_PRICES,id)||E16_FISH.some(f=>'pez_'+f.id===id)));

 panel.innerHTML='<h3>🎒 Tus objetos</h3><div class="muted" style="margin-bottom:6px">Oro: <b>'+fmt(state.gold)+'</b> · Valor total vendible: <b>'+fmt(total)+'</b> oro</div>'+
 (rows.length?rows.map(([id,n])=>{

  return '<div class="e164Item"><div><b>'+(f?f.icon+' ':'')+esc(itemNameOf(id))+'</b> <span class="muted">×'+fmt(n)+'</span><div class="muted" style="font-size:.8rem">'+fmt(price)+' oro/unidad · Total '+fmt(price*n)+'</div></div><div class="e164Buttons"><button class="small" '+attrs+' '+(f?'data-e16-qty':'data-e15-qty')+'="1">Vender 1</button><button class="small" '+attrs+' '+(f?'data-e16-qty':'data-e15-qty')+'="all">Vender todos</button></div></div>';
 }).join(''):'<p class="muted">Todavía no tenés materiales ni peces para vender.</p>');
 const summary=document.getElementById('e163InvSummary');if(summary)summary.replaceWith(panel);else page.prepend(panel);
}

 if(!state||!ready||busy||managing||!storageReady||simulationLocked()||e16Session)return;

===== src/js/fishing/minigame/025-e165-zones.js =====

"use strict";
/* Zonas de pesca: datos y selección opcional en ui, sin alterar el formato v7. */
/* @include src/catalogs/025-e165-zones-e165_zones.js */
/* @include src/catalogs/025-e165-zones-e16_fish-additions-1.js */
Object.assign(E161_DIFFICULTY,{lucio:1.37,anguila:1.65,lubina:1.52,atun:1.85,mantarraya:2.15});
function e165Zone(){
 const id=state?.ui?.fishingZoneId;
 const z=E165_ZONES.find(z=>z.id===id);
 return z&&e16FishLevel()>=z.req?z:E165_ZONES[0];
}
/* Conserva la rareza y bonificaciones de cañas de E1.6.4, pero limita la selección a la zona. */
e161RandomFish=function(){
 const z=e165Zone(),lvl=e16FishLevel(),rod=e161RodIndex();
 const pool=E16_FISH.filter(f=>z.fish.includes(f.id)&&lvl>=f.req&&rod>=Math.max(0,Math.floor((f.req-1)/10)));
 if(!pool.length)return E16_FISH[0];
 const idx=e164SelectedRodIndex(),bonus=(E164_ROD_STATS[idx]?.rare||0)/100;
 const weights=pool.map(f=>{
  if(f.rarity)return f.rarity*(1+bonus*5);
  return Math.max(.12,1/Math.pow(1+(lvl-f.req)/4,.4)/Math.pow(E161_DIFFICULTY[f.id]||1,2.3));
 });
 let roll=Math.random()*weights.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
 for(let i=0;i<pool.length;i++){roll-=weights[i];if(roll<=0)return pool[i];}
 return pool[pool.length-1];
};
const e165PreviousPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){
 e165PreviousPage();
 const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');if(!page||!state)return;
 const z=e165Zone(),lvl=e16FishLevel();
 const header=page.querySelector('.e162DockHead');
 if(header){
  const title=header.querySelector('h3');if(title)title.textContent=z.icon+' '+z.name;
  const zones=document.createElement('div');zones.className='e165Zones';
  zones.innerHTML=E165_ZONES.map(a=>`<button class="e165ZoneBtn ${a.id===z.id?'active':''}" data-e165-zone="${a.id}" ${lvl<a.req||!!e16Session?'disabled':''}>${a.icon} ${a.name}<small>${lvl<a.req?'🔒 Pesca nivel '+a.req:a.id===z.id?'✓ Zona actual':'Entrar a la zona'}</small></button>`).join('');
  header.after(zones);
  const info=document.createElement('div');info.className='e165ZoneInfo';
  const eligible=E16_FISH.filter(f=>z.fish.includes(f.id));
  info.textContent=z.desc+' · '+eligible.filter(f=>lvl>=f.req).length+' especies desbloqueadas de '+eligible.length+'.';
  zones.after(info);
 }
 /* Una sola barra de progreso visible, junto a la acción principal. */
 const panels=page.querySelectorAll('.fishPanel');
 for(const p of panels){if(p!==page.querySelector('.e162Dock')&&p.textContent.includes('Cada 3 niveles ganás +2 de zona'))p.remove();}
 const species=page.querySelector('.fishSpecies');
 if(species){species.innerHTML=E16_FISH.filter(f=>z.fish.includes(f.id)).map(f=>`<div class="${lvl<f.req?'fishLocked':''}">${f.icon} <b>${f.name}</b><div class="fishMuted">${lvl>=f.req?'Disponible':'🔒 Pesca '+f.req} · ${fmt(f.price)} oro al vender · ${fmt(f.xp)} XP</div></div>`).join('');}
};
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
 const btn=e.target.closest('[data-e165-zone]');if(!btn)return;
 e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
 if(!state||!ready||busy||managing||!storageReady||simulationLocked()||e16Session)return;
 const zone=E165_ZONES.find(z=>z.id===btn.dataset.e165Zone);
 if(!zone||e16FishLevel()<zone.req)return;
 if(e161Fishing){e161Fishing=false;e161StopWait();}
 e195Reset();
 state.ui=state.ui||{};state.ui.fishingZoneId=zone.id;
 persist();e16Page();toast('Nueva zona: '+zone.name);
},true);


===== src/js/fishing/minigame/027-e17-quests.js =====

"use strict";
/* E1.7: primer encargo del pescador. Usa state.inventory, state.owned y state.ui opcional (guardado v7). */
/* @include src/catalogs/027-e17-quests-e17_quest.js */
/* @include src/catalogs/027-e17-quests-e17_rod.js */
function e17Done(){return !!state?.owned?.[E17_ROD.id];}
function e17HasFish(){return E17_QUEST.needs.every(x=>Number.isSafeInteger(state?.inventory?.['pez_'+x.id])&&state.inventory['pez_'+x.id]>=x.qty);}
function e17Selected(){return e17Done()&&state?.ui?.fishingRodId===E17_ROD.id;}
/* Mantiene intacta la progresión de cañas comprables y agrega una alternativa de quest. */
const e17PreviousRod=e161Rod;
e161Rod=function(){return e17Selected()?E17_ROD:e17PreviousRod();};
const e17PreviousBite=e161NextBite;
e161NextBite=function(){
 e17PreviousBite();
 if(!e17Selected()||!e161Fishing||e161Waiting===null)return;
 const remaining=Math.max(1200,e161CatchAt-Date.now());
 clearTimeout(e161Waiting);
 const wait=Math.max(1200,Math.round(remaining*.90));
 e161CatchAt=Date.now()+wait;
 e161Waiting=setTimeout(()=>{e161Waiting=null;e161StopWait();if(!e161Fishing||!e161OnPage()||!e161CanFish())return;const f=e161RandomFish();e16Start(f.id);},wait);
};
function e17QuestHtml(){
 const lvl=e16FishLevel(),done=e17Done(),unlocked=lvl>=E17_QUEST.level;
 const fish=E17_QUEST.needs.map(x=>{
  const f=E16_FISH.find(y=>y.id===x.id),n=Math.max(0,Number(state.inventory?.['pez_'+x.id])||0);
  return `<div class="e17QuestFish"><b>${f?.icon||'🐟'} ${f?.name||x.id}</b>${fmt(Math.min(n,x.qty))} / ${x.qty} en inventario<div class="e17QuestBar"><div style="width:${100*Math.min(1,n/x.qty)}%"></div></div></div>`;
 }).join('');
 return `<section class="e17Quest" aria-label="Encargo del pescador"><h3>📜 ${E17_QUEST.title}</h3>
 <div class="fishMuted">🏡 Muelle del pueblo · Recomendado: Pesca ${E17_QUEST.level} · Una sola vez por personaje</div>
 ${done?`<p>✅ ¡Encargo completado! Ya conseguiste la <b>${E17_ROD.name}</b>.</p><div class="e17QuestReward"><span class="fishMuted">Control +7,5% de barra · Picadas 10% más rápidas · Recompensa permanente</span><button class="small" data-e17-equip ${e17Selected()?'disabled':''}>${e17Selected()?'✓ Caña equipada':'Equipar caña de misión'}</button></div>`:
 `<p>El viejo pescador necesita provisiones. Guardá estas capturas en tu inventario y entregalas juntas para conseguir una caña especial.</p>
 <div class="e17QuestGrid">${fish}</div>
 <div class="e17QuestReward"><div><b>🎁 ${E17_ROD.name}</b><div class="fishMuted">Recompensa de este encargo. Requiere habilidad de Pesca 6 para reclamarla; no se compra con oro.</div></div>
 <button class="primary" data-e17-deliver ${!unlocked||!e17HasFish()?'disabled':''}>${!unlocked?'🔒 Requiere Pesca 6':!e17HasFish()?'Faltan peces':'Entregar peces y reclamar caña'}</button></div>
 <p class="fishMuted">Los peces entregados se consumen. Podés venderlos en Inventario, pero entonces no contarán para el encargo.</p>`}
 </section>`;
}
const e17PreviousPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){
 e17PreviousPage();
 const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');if(!page||!state)return;
 const zone=e165Zone();
 if(zone.id==='muelle'){
  const section=document.createElement('div');section.innerHTML=e17QuestHtml();
  const quest=section.firstElementChild;
  const anchor=page.querySelector('.e165ZoneInfo')||page.querySelector('.e162Dock');
  if(anchor)anchor.after(quest);else page.append(quest);
 }
 const details=page.querySelector('details:has(.fishRod)');
 if(details&&e17Done()){
  const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='fishRod e17SpecialRod';
  row.innerHTML=`<div><b>🏆 ${E17_ROD.name}</b><div class="fishMuted">Misión del Muelle · Pesca 6 · ${E17_ROD.desc}</div></div><button class="small" data-e17-equip ${e17Selected()?'disabled':''}>${e17Selected()?'✓ Equipada':'Equipar'}</button>`;
  details.append(row);
  if(e17Selected()){const summary=details.querySelector('summary');if(summary)summary.textContent='🎣 Cañas y estadísticas · '+E17_ROD.name;}
 }
};
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
 const deliver=event.target.closest('[data-e17-deliver]');
 const equip=event.target.closest('[data-e17-equip]');
 if(!deliver&&!equip)return;
 event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();
 if(!state||!ready||busy||managing||!storageReady||simulationLocked()||e16Session)return;
 if(deliver){
  if(e17Done()||e165Zone().id!=='muelle'||e16FishLevel()<E17_QUEST.level||!e17HasFish())return;
  // Validar antes de modificar para evitar entregas parciales o pérdidas accidentales.
  for(const x of E17_QUEST.needs){const id='pez_'+x.id;state.inventory[id]-=x.qty;if(state.inventory[id]===0)delete state.inventory[id];}
  state.owned=state.owned||{};state.owned[E17_ROD.id]=true;
  state.ui=state.ui||{};state.ui.fishingRodId=E17_ROD.id;
  if(e161Fishing){e161Fishing=false;e161StopWait();}
  addLog('Completaste '+E17_QUEST.title+' y recibiste '+E17_ROD.name+'.');
  persist();renderGame();toast('🏆 ¡Misión completada! '+E17_ROD.name+' equipada.');
 }else if(equip){
  if(!e17Done()||e16FishLevel()<E17_ROD.req)return;
  if(e161Fishing){e161Fishing=false;e161StopWait();}
  state.ui=state.ui||{};state.ui.fishingRodId=E17_ROD.id;
  persist();e16Page();toast('Caña equipada: '+E17_ROD.name);
 }
},true);


===== src/js/fishing/minigame/029-e171-improvements.js =====

"use strict";
/* E1.7.1: cambios incrementales; no se altera el formato de guardado v7. */
/* @include src/catalogs/029-e171-improvements-e171_quests.js */
function e171Done(q){return !!state?.owned?.[q.rod.id];}
function e171CanDeliver(q){return e16FishLevel()>=q.level&&q.needs.every(n=>(state?.inventory?.['pez_'+n.id]||0)>=n.qty);}
function e171RodSelected(q){return e171Done(q)&&state?.ui?.fishingRodId===q.rod.id;}
/* Resolver una única caña efectiva. Las compradas siguen en propiedad pero no se equipan simultáneamente. */
const e171PriorRod=e161Rod;
e161Rod=function(){
 const q=E171_QUESTS.find(e171RodSelected);
 return q?q.rod:e171PriorRod();
};
/* Arranque justo: la zona verde comienza centrada sobre el pez, con margen en ambos extremos. */
const e171PreviousStart=e16Start;
e16Start=function(id){
 e171PreviousStart(id);
 if(!e16Session)return;
 const g=e16Session;
 const track=document.getElementById('e16Tank');
 const width=e190TrackWidth();
 const maxPos=Math.max(0,width-g.fish.zone-4);
 g.target=Math.max(8,Math.min(width-30,g.target));
 g.pos=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxPos,g.target+12-g.fish.zone/2));
 g.vel=0;
 g.progress=.27;
 e16Paint();
};
/* Para las nuevas cañas de misión, acelerar picadas con la misma mecánica de espera existente. */
const e171PreviousNextBite=e161NextBite;
e161NextBite=function(){
 e171PreviousNextBite();
 const q=E171_QUESTS.find(e171RodSelected);
 if(!q||e161Waiting===null||!e161Fishing)return;
 const factor=q.id==='e171_rio'?.88:.85;
 const remaining=Math.max(1200,e161CatchAt-Date.now());
 clearTimeout(e161Waiting);
 const wait=Math.max(1200,Math.round(remaining*factor));
 e161CatchAt=Date.now()+wait;
 e161Waiting=setTimeout(()=>{e161Waiting=null;e161StopWait();if(!e161Fishing||!e161OnPage()||!e161CanFish())return;const f=e161RandomFish();e16Start(f.id);},wait);
};
function e171QuestMarkup(q){
 const done=e171Done(q);
 const items=q.needs.map(n=>{
  const f=E16_FISH.find(f=>f.id===n.id),owned=state?.inventory?.['pez_'+n.id]||0;
  return `<span>${f?.icon||'🐟'} ${f?.name||n.id}: <b>${Math.min(owned,n.qty)}/${n.qty}</b></span>`;
 }).join('');
 return `<section class="e171Quest"><h3>📜 ${q.title}</h3><div class="fishMuted">Habilidad de Pesca ${q.level} · Guardá estos peces en el inventario y entregalos juntos. Cualquier tamaño cuenta; se consumen al reclamar.</div>
 <div class="e171Needs">${items}</div><div class="e171Footer"><div><b>🎁 ${q.rod.name}</b><div class="fishMuted">${q.rod.desc}</div></div>
 <button class="small primary" data-e171-deliver="${q.id}" ${e171CanDeliver(q)||done?'':'disabled'}>${done?'✓ Completada':e16FishLevel()<q.level?'🔒 Pesca '+q.level:e171CanDeliver(q)?'Entregar peces':'Faltan peces'}</button></div></section>`;
}
const e171PreviousPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){
 e171PreviousPage();

 if(originalQuest)originalQuest.remove();

 /* En el listado de cañas, indicar claramente una sola equipada. */

 if(rodDetails){
  const active=e161Rod();

   const name=row.querySelector('b')?.textContent?.replace(/^✓\s*/,'').trim()||'';
   const equipped=name===active?.name;

   const title=row.querySelector('b');if(title)title.textContent=(equipped?'✓ ':'')+name;
  });
  for(const q of E171_QUESTS.filter(e171Done)){
   const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='fishRod';
   const equipped=e171RodSelected(q);
   row.classList.toggle('e164RodActive',equipped);
   row.innerHTML=`<div><b>${equipped?'✓ ':''}${q.rod.name}</b><div class="fishMuted">Recompensa de misión · ${q.rod.desc}</div></div><button class="small" data-e171-equip="${q.id}" ${equipped?'disabled':''}>${equipped?'✓ Equipada':'Equipar'}</button>`;
   rodDetails.append(row);
  }
  const summary=rodDetails.querySelector('summary');if(summary)summary.textContent='🎣 Cañas y estadísticas · '+(active?.name||'Sin caña');
 }
};

 if(!state||!ready||busy||managing||!storageReady||simulationLocked()||e16Session)return;

  for(const n of q.needs){const id='pez_'+n.id;state.inventory[id]-=n.qty;if(!state.inventory[id])delete state.inventory[id];}

 }else if(!e171Done(q)||e16FishLevel()<q.level)return;

===== src/js/fishing/minigame/031-e172-fixes.js =====

"use strict";
/* El id de la caña en ui es la única fuente de verdad: nunca hay dos equipadas. */
function e172RodId(){
 const id=state?.ui?.fishingRodId;
 const all=[...E161_RODS,E17_ROD,...E171_QUESTS.map(q=>q.rod)];
 const valid=all.find(r=>r.id===id && (state?.owned?.[r.id]||r.id===E17_ROD.id&&e17Done()));
 return valid?.id||e161Rod()?.id||null;
}
const e172PriorPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){
 e172PriorPage();
 const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');if(!page||!state)return;
 const activeId=e172RodId();
 const rods=page.querySelector('details:has(.fishRod)');
 if(rods){
  // Reconstruir solo los estados de equipamiento, incluidos los premios de quest.
  rods.querySelectorAll('.fishRod').forEach(row=>{
   const b=row.querySelector('b');if(!b)return;
   const name=b.textContent.replace(/^[✓🏆\s]+/u,'').trim();
   const rod=[...E161_RODS,E17_ROD,...E171_QUESTS.map(q=>q.rod)].find(r=>r.name===name);
   if(!rod)return;
   const equipped=rod.id===activeId;
   b.textContent=(equipped?'✓ ':'')+rod.name;
   row.classList.toggle('e164RodActive',equipped);
   row.classList.toggle('e172Equipped',equipped);
   const btn=row.querySelector('[data-e164-equip],[data-e17-equip],[data-e171-equip]');
   if(btn){btn.textContent=equipped?'✓ Equipada':'Equipar';btn.disabled=equipped;}
   row.querySelectorAll('.e172Label').forEach(el=>el.remove());
   if(equipped&&!btn){const label=document.createElement('span');label.className='e172Label';label.textContent='✓ Equipada';row.append(label);}
  });
  const summary=rods.querySelector('summary');
  if(summary)summary.textContent='🎣 Cañas y estadísticas · '+(e161Rod()?.name||'Sin caña');
 }
 const dock=page.querySelector('.e162Dock');
 if(dock){
  const waiting=dock.querySelector('#e161WaitingText');
  if(waiting&&!e161Fishing&&!e16Session){
   waiting.textContent='Elegí una zona y empezá a pescar con el botón «Tirar la caña».';
  }
  // La notificación de captura ya aparece en un recuadro: evitar duplicarla.
  if(dock.querySelector('.fishCatch')&&waiting&&!e161Fishing&&!e16Session)waiting.style.display='none';
 }
};
/* Cambiar de zona es una acción directa: prepara la caña y comienza la espera automáticamente.
   Se mantienen las restricciones existentes (combate, descanso, navegación). */
document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
 const btn=e.target.closest('[data-e165-zone]');if(!btn)return;
 // Se ejecuta antes del listener E1.6.5 y lo sustituye.
 e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
 if(!state||!ready||busy||managing||!storageReady||simulationLocked()||e16Session)return;
 const zone=E165_ZONES.find(z=>z.id===btn.dataset.e165Zone);
 if(!zone||e16FishLevel()<zone.req)return;
 const same=e165Zone().id===zone.id;
 if(!same){e161Fishing=false;e161StopWait();state.ui=state.ui||{};state.ui.fishingZoneId=zone.id;persist();}
 e16Result='';e173ZoneChanged=true;e173SpeciesOpen=false;
 if(e161Rod()&&e161CanFish()){
  e161Fishing=true;
  e161NextBite();
  toast((same?'🎣 Pescando en ':'🌊 Entraste a ')+zone.name+' · Esperando una picada');
 }else{
  e161Fishing=false;e16Page();
  toast('Zona: '+zone.name+'. Detené el combate o descanso para poder pescar.');
 }
},true);

/* E1.8: récords de capturas y primer encargo por habilidad. No cambia el inventario ni el formato v7. */
/* @include src/catalogs/035-e18-trophies-e18_rod.js */
function e18Record(){
 if(!state)return null;
 state.ui=state.ui||{};
 if(!state.ui.e18Fishing||typeof state.ui.e18Fishing!=='object')state.ui.e18Fishing={total:0,large:0,huge:0,legendary:0,riverHuge:false,bySpecies:{}};
 const r=state.ui.e18Fishing;
 if(!r.bySpecies||typeof r.bySpecies!=='object')r.bySpecies={};
 return r;
}
function e18RodSelected(){return !!state?.owned?.[E18_ROD.id]&&state?.ui?.fishingRodId===E18_ROD.id;}
const e18OldRod=e161Rod;
e161Rod=function(){return e18RodSelected()?E18_ROD:e18OldRod();};
const e18OldFinish=e16Finish;
e16Finish=function(won){
 const fish=won&&e16Session?.fish;
 const zone=fish?e165Zone().id:null;
 let quality=null;
 if(fish){
  const roll=Number.isFinite(e16Session.qualityRoll)?e16Session.qualityRoll:e186QualityRoll();
  quality=roll<.018?'legendary':roll<.115?'huge':roll<.34?'large':'normal';
  const r=e18Record();r.total=Math.max(0,Number(r.total)||0)+1;
  if(quality==='large')r.large=(Number(r.large)||0)+1;
  if(quality==='huge'){r.huge=(Number(r.huge)||0)+1;if(zone==='rio')r.riverHuge=true;}
  if(quality==='legendary'){r.legendary=(Number(r.legendary)||0)+1;if(zone==='rio')r.riverHuge=true;}
  const previous=r.bySpecies[fish.id]||{total:0,best:'normal'};
  const rank={normal:0,large:1,huge:2,legendary:3};
  r.bySpecies[fish.id]={...previous,total:(Number(previous.total)||0)+1,best:rank[quality]>rank[previous.best||'normal']?quality:previous.best};
 }
 e18OldFinish(won);
 if(fish){
  const labels={normal:'Común',large:'Grande',huge:'¡ENORME!',legendary:'¡LEGENDARIO!'};
  const msg=quality==='normal'?'':` 🏆 Ejemplar ${labels[quality]}`;
  if(msg){e16Result+=msg;addLog('Pesca: '+fish.name+' · '+labels[quality]);/* Trofeo mostrado en el panel, sin toast duplicado. */}
  persist();e16Page();
 }
};
const e18OldPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){
 e18OldPage();
 const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');if(!page||!state)return;
 const dock=page.querySelector('.e162Dock');if(!dock)return;
 const r=e18Record(),lvl=e16FishLevel(),done=!!state.owned?.[E18_ROD.id],eligible=lvl>=12;
 const q=document.createElement('details');q.className='e18Panel e181Journal';
 q.innerHTML=`<summary>📖 Diario de pesca · ${r.total||0} capturas · ${Object.keys(r.bySpecies).length||0} especies</summary>
 <div class="e18Pills"><span>🐟 Grandes: ${r.large||0}</span><span>🏆 Enormes: ${r.huge||0}</span><span>✨ Legendarios: ${r.legendary||0}</span></div>
 <p>Tu colección de trofeos. Los tamaños no modifican el valor de venta.</p>`;
 const quests=page.querySelector('.e171Quests');if(quests)quests.after(q);else dock.after(q);
 if(done){
  const rods=page.querySelector('details:has(.fishRod)');
  if(rods){const equipped=e18RodSelected(),row=document.createElement('div');row.className='fishRod';row.classList.toggle('e172Equipped',equipped);
   row.innerHTML=`<div><b>${equipped?'✓ ':''}${E18_ROD.name}</b><div class="fishMuted">Premio de Pesca · ${E18_ROD.desc}</div></div><button class="small" data-e18-equip ${equipped?'disabled':''}>${equipped?'✓ Equipada':'Equipar'}</button>`;
   rods.append(row);const summary=rods.querySelector('summary');if(summary)summary.textContent='🎣 Cañas y estadísticas · '+(e161Rod()?.name||'Sin caña');
  }
 }
};
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
 const claim=e.target.closest('[data-e18-claim]'),equip=e.target.closest('[data-e18-equip]');if(!claim&&!equip)return;
 e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
 if(!state||!ready||busy||managing||!storageReady||simulationLocked()||e16Session)return;
 if(claim){if(state.owned?.[E18_ROD.id]||!e190CollectorReady())return;
  state.owned=state.owned||{};state.owned[E18_ROD.id]=true;addLog('Completaste el desafío del ejemplar extraordinario.');
 }else if(!state.owned?.[E18_ROD.id])return;
 if(e161Fishing){e161Fishing=false;e161StopWait();}
 state.ui=state.ui||{};state.ui.fishingRodId=E18_ROD.id;
 persist();renderGame();toast(claim?'🏆 ¡Ganaste la Caña del Coleccionista!':'🎣 Caña del Coleccionista equipada');
},true);


===== src/js/fishing/minigame/037-e181-balance.js =====

"use strict";
/* Conserva XP ya obtenida, nivel actual, guardados y recompensas de misiones anteriores.
   A partir del nivel 16 la XP por captura disminuye gradualmente. */
function e181XpFactor(){
 const level=e16FishLevel();
 if(level<=15)return 1;
 return Math.max(.22,1/(1+(level-15)*.095));
}

 rio:{accent:'#77cf91',deep:'#153c32',shallow:'#377a59',zone:'rgba(112,211,125,.36)',edge:'#b6f1a1',glow:'rgba(112,211,125,.3)',name:'Corriente del bosque'},
 costa:{accent:'#bda1e8',deep:'#262d59',shallow:'#45638d',zone:'rgba(170,132,239,.37)',edge:'#ddbdff',glow:'rgba(170,132,239,.34)',name:'Mareas de las Brumas'}
};

const e185PriorFinish=e16Finish;

 const quality=label.includes('LEGENDARIO')?'legendary':label.includes('ENORME')?'huge':label.includes('Grande')?'large':null;

  const base='pez_'+fish.id,variant=base+'__'+quality;
  if((state.inventory?.[base]||0)>0){state.inventory[base]--;if(!state.inventory[base])delete state.inventory[base];state.inventory[variant]=(state.inventory[variant]||0)+1;}
  if(e182LastCatch&&e182LastCatch.name===fish.name){e182LastCatch.price=fish.price*data.mult;e182LastCatch.quality=data.name;}
  e16Result='🎉 '+fish.name+' '+data.name+' · '+(e182LastCatch?.xp||0)+' XP · '+(fish.price*data.mult)+' oro al vender';
  persist();updateInv();
 }
 e16Page();
};

/* Los peces especiales ocupan entradas propias: no se mezclan con ejemplares normales al vender. */
function e185Inventory(){

  const controls=document.createElement('div');for(const [label,qty] of [['Vender 1','1'],['Vender todos','all']]){const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.className='small';btn.textContent=label;btn.dataset.e185Sell=id;btn.dataset.e185Qty=qty;controls.append(btn);}row.append(controls);panel.append(row);
 }
 const inventory=page.querySelector('#e164Inventory');if(inventory)inventory.after(panel);else page.prepend(panel);
}

},true);


===== src/js/fishing/minigame/045-e186-fishing.js =====

"use strict";
/* E1.8.6: descubrimientos por zona + especialización en trofeos de cañas.
   Usa exclusivamente el registro opcional existente state.ui.e18Fishing. */
let e186BookOpen=false;
function e186LuckPercent(){
 if(!state)return 0;
 const id=e161Rod()?.id||'';
 const rodLuck={e16_rod:0,e161_rod_bamboo:3,e161_rod_fiber:6,e161_rod_steel:12,e17_rod_viejo:4,e171_rod_rio:8,e171_rod_costa:13,e18_rod_trofeo:16};
 const base=Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rodLuck,id)?rodLuck[id]:id.includes('brumas')?13:id.includes('guard')?8:4;
 return Math.min(30,base+Math.floor((e16FishLevel()-1)/10));
}
/* Conserva exactamente la distribución original con caña básica a nivel 1.
   Aumenta moderadamente la frecuencia de calidad grande, enorme y legendaria
   con cañas avanzadas y nivel. La categoría se muestrea una vez por intento y modula los movimientos. */
function e186QualityRoll(){
 const boost=1+e186LuckPercent()/100;
 const roll=Math.random();
 if(roll<.018*boost)return .009;
 if(roll<.115*boost)return .06;
 if(roll<.34*boost)return .20;
 return .8;
}
const e186PriorPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){
 const existing=document.querySelector('#page_fishing .e186Book');
 if(existing)e186BookOpen=existing.open;
 e186PriorPage();
 const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');if(!page||!state)return;
 const dock=page.querySelector('.e162Dock');if(!dock)return;
 const summary=dock.querySelector('.e185Summary');
 if(summary){
  const badge=document.createElement('span');badge.className='e186Luck';
  badge.textContent='✨ Trofeos +'+e186LuckPercent()+'% de probabilidad';
  summary.append(badge);
 }
 const zone=e165Zone(),record=e18Record(),species=E16_FISH.filter(f=>zone.fish.includes(f.id));
 const found=species.filter(f=>(record.bySpecies[f.id]?.total||0)>0).length;
 const book=document.createElement('details');book.className='e186Book';book.open=e186BookOpen;
 const header=document.createElement('summary');header.textContent='📘 Colección de '+zone.name+' · '+found+'/'+species.length+' especies descubiertas';book.append(header);
 const progress=document.createElement('div');progress.className='e186Progress';
 const fill=document.createElement('div');fill.style.width=(species.length?100*found/species.length:0)+'%';progress.append(fill);book.append(progress);
 const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='e186BookGrid';
 const bestLabels={normal:'Común',large:'Grande',huge:'Enorme',legendary:'Legendario'};
 for(const f of species){
  const data=record.bySpecies[f.id],count=Number(data?.total)||0;
  const unlocked=e16FishLevel()>=f.req;
  const card=document.createElement('div');card.className='e186Fish';card.dataset.speciesId=f.id;
  if(!count)card.classList.add('e186Unknown');
  if(data?.best==='legendary')card.classList.add('e186Legend');
  const title=document.createElement('strong');title.textContent=count?f.name:(unlocked?'Sin descubrir':'Bloqueado · nivel '+f.req);
  const info=document.createElement('small');info.textContent=count?'Capturados: '+count+' · Récord: '+(bestLabels[data.best]||'Común'):(unlocked?'¡Seguí pescando para descubrirlo!':'Disponible al alcanzar Pesca '+f.req);
  card.append(title,info);grid.append(card);
 }
 book.append(grid);
 const old=page.querySelector('.e186Book');if(old)old.remove();
 const speciesDetails=dock.querySelector('.e173Species');
 if(speciesDetails)speciesDetails.after(book);else if(summary)summary.after(book);else dock.append(book);
};
// Cambiar de zona cierra la colección, mostrando el nuevo conjunto de especies.
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest('[data-e165-zone]'))e186BookOpen=false;},true);


===== src/js/fishing/minigame/049-e188-fishing.js =====

"use strict";
/* E1.8.8: quest ingredients accept all quality variants, rods are level-free,
   and the existing combat dock switches to a fishing HUD only on the fishing tab. */
const E188_QUALITY=['legendary','huge','large','normal'];
function e188FishStock(id){
 const inv=state?.inventory||{},base='pez_'+id;
 return E188_QUALITY.reduce((sum,q)=>sum+Math.max(0,Number(inv[q==='normal'?base:base+'__'+q])||0),0);
}
function e188Consume(needs){
 if(!needs.every(n=>e188FishStock(n.id)>=n.qty))return false;
 const inv=state.inventory;
 for(const n of needs){
  let left=n.qty,base='pez_'+n.id;
  // Common fish first, preserving valuable trophies whenever possible.
  for(const q of ['normal','large','huge','legendary']){
   const key=q==='normal'?base:base+'__'+q,have=Math.max(0,Number(inv[key])||0),take=Math.min(left,have);
   if(take){inv[key]-=take;if(!inv[key])delete inv[key];left-=take;}
   if(!left)break;
  }
 }
 return true;
}
// Keep all existing quest buttons, but let rare fish count towards delivery.
e17HasFish=function(){return E17_QUEST.needs.every(n=>e188FishStock(n.id)>=n.qty);};
e171CanDeliver=function(q){return e16FishLevel()>=q.level&&q.needs.every(n=>e188FishStock(n.id)>=n.qty);};
const e188PriorQuestPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){
 e188PriorQuestPage();
 const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');if(!page||!state)return;
 // Replace counts displayed by previous versions with quality-inclusive counts.
 const oldQuest=page.querySelector('.e17Quest');
 if(oldQuest&&!e17Done()){
  oldQuest.querySelectorAll('.e17QuestFish').forEach((el,i)=>{const n=E17_QUEST.needs[i];if(!n)return;
   const total=e188FishStock(n.id),f=E16_FISH.find(x=>x.id===n.id);
   el.innerHTML='<b>'+(f?.icon||'🐟')+' '+(f?.name||n.id)+'</b> '+fmt(Math.min(total,n.qty))+' / '+n.qty+' en inventario<div class="e17QuestBar"><div style="width:'+100*Math.min(1,total/n.qty)+'%"></div></div>';
  });
  const btn=oldQuest.querySelector('[data-e17-deliver]');if(btn){btn.disabled=e16FishLevel()<E17_QUEST.level||!e17HasFish();btn.textContent=e17HasFish()?'Entregar peces y reclamar caña':'Faltan peces';}
 }
 for(const q of E171_QUESTS){const btn=page.querySelector('[data-e171-deliver="'+q.id+'"]');if(!btn)continue;
  const panel=btn.closest('.e171Quest');panel?.querySelectorAll('.e171Needs span').forEach((span,i)=>{const n=q.needs[i],f=E16_FISH.find(x=>x.id===n?.id);if(n)span.innerHTML=(f?.icon||'🐟')+' '+(f?.name||n.id)+': <b>'+Math.min(e188FishStock(n.id),n.qty)+'/'+n.qty+'</b>';});
  btn.disabled=!e171CanDeliver(q);btn.textContent=e171CanDeliver(q)?'Entregar peces':'Faltan peces';
 }
 for(const q of E187_QUESTS){const btn=page.querySelector('[data-e187-claim="'+q.id+'"]');if(!btn)continue;
  const panel=btn.closest('.e187Quest');panel?.querySelectorAll('.e187QuestNeeds span').forEach((span,i)=>{const n=q.needs[i],f=E16_FISH.find(x=>x.id===n?.id);if(n)span.textContent=(f?.icon||'🐟')+' '+(f?.name||n.id)+' '+Math.min(e188FishStock(n.id),n.qty)+'/'+n.qty;});
  btn.disabled=!q.needs.every(n=>e188FishStock(n.id)>=n.qty);btn.textContent=btn.disabled?'Faltan peces':'Completar encargo';
 }
 // Remove the level requirement from purchased rods, both visually and in the action.
 const rods=page.querySelector('details:has(.fishRod)');
 if(rods){rods.querySelectorAll('[data-e161-buy]').forEach(btn=>{const i=Number(btn.dataset.e161Buy),r=E161_RODS[i];if(!r)return;
  btn.disabled=state.gold<r.price;const row=btn.closest('.fishRod');const info=row?.querySelector('.fishMuted');if(info)info.textContent='Sin nivel mínimo · '+r.desc+' · '+fmt(r.price)+' oro';
 });
  rods.querySelectorAll('.fishRod .fishMuted').forEach(el=>{if(/Pesca \d+/.test(el.textContent||'')&&/oro/.test(el.textContent||''))el.textContent=el.textContent.replace(/Pesca \d+\s*·\s*/,'Sin nivel mínimo · ');});
 }
 const next=page.querySelector('.fishPanel p.fishMuted');
 if(next&&next.textContent?.startsWith('Próxima caña:'))next.textContent='Podés comprar cualquier caña si tenés el oro necesario.';
 e188UpdateHud();
};
// The previous click handlers run at capture phase. Intercept only the actions we replace.
window.addEventListener('click',e=>{
 const buy=e.target.closest('[data-e161-buy]'),quest=e.target.closest('[data-e17-deliver],[data-e171-deliver],[data-e187-claim]');
 if(!buy&&!quest)return;
 e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
 if(!state||!ready||busy||managing||!storageReady||simulationLocked()||e16Session)return;
 if(buy){
  const r=E161_RODS[Number(buy.dataset.e161Buy)];if(!r||r.unlock||state.owned?.[r.id]||state.gold<r.price)return;
  state.gold-=r.price;state.owned=state.owned||{};state.owned[r.id]=true;
  state.ui=state.ui||{};state.ui.fishingRodId=r.id;

 }
 if(quest.hasAttribute('data-e17-deliver')){

  state.owned=state.owned||{};state.owned[E17_ROD.id]=true;state.ui=state.ui||{};state.ui.fishingRodId=E17_ROD.id;
  if(e161Fishing){e161Fishing=false;e161StopWait();}
  addLog('Pesca: misión '+E17_QUEST.title+' completada.');persist();renderGame();toast('🏆 ¡Caña del Viejo Pescador conseguida!');return;
 }
 if(quest.hasAttribute('data-e171-deliver')){
  const q=E171_QUESTS.find(x=>x.id===quest.dataset.e171Deliver);
  if(!q||e171Done(q)||e165Zone().id!==q.zone||e16FishLevel()<q.level||!e188Consume(q.needs))return;
  state.owned=state.owned||{};state.owned[q.rod.id]=true;state.ui=state.ui||{};state.ui.fishingRodId=q.rod.id;
  if(e161Fishing){e161Fishing=false;e161StopWait();}
  addLog('Pesca: misión '+q.title+' completada.');persist();renderGame();toast('🏆 ¡Conseguiste '+q.rod.name+'!');return;
 }
 const q=E187_QUESTS.find(x=>x.id===quest.dataset.e187Claim);
 if(!q||e187QuestDone(q)||e165Zone().id!==q.zone||e16FishLevel()<q.req||!e188Consume(q.needs))return;
 e187Rewards()[q.id]=true;
 if(q.kind==='rod'){state.owned=state.owned||{};state.owned[E187_ROD.id]=true;state.ui.fishingRodId=E187_ROD.id;}
 addLog('Pesca: misión '+q.title+' completada.');persist();renderGame();toast('🏆 '+q.reward);
},true);
// Fishing species still require fishing skill, but not an owned rod tier.
const e188PreviousRandom=e161RandomFish;
e161RandomFish=function(){
 const z=e165Zone(),lvl=e16FishLevel(),idx=e164SelectedRodIndex();
 const pool=E16_FISH.filter(f=>z.fish.includes(f.id)&&lvl>=f.req);
 if(!pool.length)return E16_FISH.find(f=>z.fish.includes(f.id))||E16_FISH[0];
 const rare=(E164_ROD_STATS[idx]?.rare||0)/100;
 const weights=pool.map(f=>f.rarity?f.rarity*(1+rare*5):Math.max(.12,1/Math.pow(1+(lvl-f.req)/4,.4)/Math.pow(E161_DIFFICULTY[f.id]||1,2.3)));
 let roll=Math.random()*weights.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
 for(let i=0;i<pool.length;i++){roll-=weights[i];if(roll<=0)return pool[i];}
 return pool[pool.length-1];
};
// Fixed HUD: reuse the combat dock; only replace its contents while fishing.
function e188HudNode(){
 const dock=document.getElementById('combatDock');if(!dock)return null;
 let node=document.getElementById('e188FishingHud');if(!node){
  node=document.createElement('div');node.id='e188FishingHud';node.setAttribute('aria-label','Estado de pesca');
  node.innerHTML='<div><div class="e188Head" id="e188HudTitle">🎣 Pesca</div><div class="e188Sub" id="e188HudSub">Esperando una captura</div></div><div><div class="e188Result" id="e188HudResult" role="status">Preparado para pescar</div><div class="e188Track"><div class="e188Fill" id="e188HudFill"></div></div><div class="e188Meta"><span id="e188HudPercent">Captura 0%</span><span id="e188HudZone">—</span></div></div>';
  dock.querySelector('.dockInner')?.append(node);
 }
 return node;
}
function e188UpdateHud(){

 const active=!!(state&&ready&&e161OnPage()&&!document.getElementById('gameScreen')?.classList.contains('hidden'));
 dock.classList.toggle('e188FishingMode',active);
 if(!active){e215PlaceXp(false);measureDock();return;}
 const node=e188HudNode();if(!node)return;
 measureDock();
}
const e188PriorFinish=e16Finish;

/* Ilustración horizontal de la caña: empuñadura, carrete, guías, línea y señuelo. */
e189RodSVG=function(id){
 const key=(id||'').toLowerCase();
 const metal=/coral/.test(key)?'#ff9d82':/bruma|abis/.test(key)?'#bba2ff':/guard|viejo/.test(key)?'#91d8ad':/fibra|reforz/.test(key)?'#b9d7e9':/colecc/.test(key)?'#f7d57c':'#e0b576';
 const glow=/bruma|abis|coral|colecc/.test(key);
 return '<svg viewBox="0 0 145 44" role="img" aria-label="Caña equipada vista horizontal" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><defs><linearGradient id="e190rod" x1="0" y1="1" x2="1" y2="0"><stop stop-color="#533522"/><stop offset=".23" stop-color="'+metal+'"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#f1e6b3"/></linearGradient></defs><path d="M7 36 Q66 28 130 7" fill="none" stroke="#0c1117" stroke-width="6" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M7 36 Q66 28 130 7" fill="none" stroke="url(#e190rod)" stroke-width="3.2" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M7 36 L32 33" stroke="#72503c" stroke-width="7" stroke-linecap="round"/><path d="M12 34 L28 32" stroke="#c79b6b" stroke-width="1" stroke-dasharray="3 3"/><g stroke="'+metal+'" fill="none" stroke-width="1.6"><ellipse cx="55" cy="26" rx="3" ry="5"/><ellipse cx="86" cy="18" rx="2" ry="4"/><ellipse cx="114" cy="11" rx="1.6" ry="3"/></g><circle cx="39" cy="33" r="8" fill="#344854" stroke="'+metal+'" stroke-width="2.5"/><circle cx="39" cy="33" r="3" fill="'+metal+'"/><path d="M39 33 l-7 10" stroke="'+metal+'" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M130 7 Q140 12 136 29" stroke="#c8dce7" stroke-width=".8" stroke-dasharray="2 2" fill="none"/><path d="M136 29 l-4 5 4 5 4-5Z" fill="'+metal+'"/>'+(glow?'<circle cx="133" cy="7" r="4" fill="'+metal+'" opacity=".65"/>':'')+'</svg>';
};
/* Una silueta genérica compartida: nunca depende de la especie seleccionada. */
function e190MysteryArt(){
 return '<svg class="fishMysteryArt" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 120 80" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" role="img" aria-label="Pez por descubrir"><path d="M16 40 Q37 17 74 28 L104 15 L97 40 L104 65 L74 52 Q37 63 16 40Z" fill="#8c8c8c"/><path d="M42 27 L50 14 L66 27 M44 54 L56 66 L70 53" fill="#696969"/><text x="57" y="48" text-anchor="middle" font-family="system-ui,sans-serif" font-size="25" font-weight="800" fill="#e2e2e2">?</text></svg>';
}
/* El HUD no revela especie durante la picada; solo al capturarla. */
function e189UpdateArt(){
 const dock=document.getElementById('combatDock');if(!dock?.classList.contains('e188FishingMode'))return;
 const hud=document.getElementById('e188FishingHud');if(!hud)return;
 let art=hud.querySelector('.e189Art'),data=hud.querySelector('.e189Data');
 if(!art){art=document.createElement('div');art.className='e189Art';art.setAttribute('role','button');art.tabIndex=0;art.title='Volver a la pesca · experiencia y minijuego';hud.prepend(art);}
 if(!data){data=document.createElement('div');data.className='e189Data';[...hud.children].filter(n=>n!==art&&!n.classList.contains('dockProgress')).forEach(n=>data.append(n));hud.append(data);}
}
/* Reemplazar el texto que muestra el nombre en el panel mientras está picando. */
const e190PreviousPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){e190PreviousPage();if(!e16Session)return;const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');if(!page)return;const waiting=page.querySelector('#e161WaitingText');if(waiting)waiting.textContent='🐟 ¡Picó un pez misterioso!';const status=page.querySelector('#e16Status');if(status)status.textContent='🐟 ¡Picó un pez misterioso!';};
/* Recorridos continuos y anticipación de impulso. La dificultad, control y resiliencia
   se miden por separado de la selección aleatoria y las recompensas. */
// Capture behavior is separate from rarity weights: demanding fish need sustained tracking.
/* @include src/catalogs/052-e190-polish-e210_capture_profiles.js */
const E232_DEFAULT_CAPTURE={speed:1,run:1,gain:1,loss:1};
function e233MotionRandom(g){let n=g.motionRng;n^=n<<13;n^=n>>>17;n^=n<<5;g.motionRng=n>>>0;return g.motionRng/4294967296;}
function e190FishingStep(g,holding,dt,width,random=Math.random){
 if(Number.isFinite(g.controlFraction))g.fish.zone=Math.min(width*.55,Math.max(16,width*(g.controlFraction+(g.controlRewardFraction||0))+(g.controlExtra||0)));
 // Player input stays proportional to the track on desktop and touch.
 const limit=Math.max(0,width-g.fish.zone-4),scale=width/520;
 g.vel+=(holding?600:-570)*scale*dt;g.vel=Math.max(-220*scale,Math.min(220*scale,g.vel));g.pos=Math.max(0,Math.min(limit,g.pos+g.vel*dt));if(g.pos===0||g.pos===limit)g.vel=0;
 const diff=E161_DIFFICULTY[g.fish.id]||1,motion=g.motion||{distance:1,interval:1};
 const profile=E210_CAPTURE_PROFILES[g.fish.id]||E232_DEFAULT_CAPTURE;
 // Local zigzags with occasional changes of hunting ground. Randomness is
 // attempt-local; the smooth velocity/acceleration limits prevent teleporting.
 if(g===e16Session&&!Number.isFinite(g.qualityRoll)){g.qualityRoll=e186QualityRoll();g.qualityRank=g.qualityRoll<.018?3:g.qualityRoll<.115?2:g.qualityRoll<.34?1:0;}
 if(!g.motionRng)g.motionRng=((Math.floor((g.seed||1)*1e7)^g.fish.id.split('').reduce((n,c)=>Math.imul(n,31)+c.charCodeAt(0),7))>>>0)||1;
 const capture=g.capture||e232CaptureProfile(g.fish,e161Rod(),e16FishLevel(),g.weightKg),rank=g.qualityRank||0;
 g.time+=dt;
 // Restore the older heading/burst model. There is no distant waypoint that
 // keeps pulling the fish in the same direction after it decides to reverse.
 if(g.time>=(g.turnAt||0)){
  const reverse=e233MotionRandom(g)<.66+Math.min(.18,g.fish.wiggle*.2);if(reverse)g.dir=-(g.dir||1);
  const feint=e233MotionRandom(g)<.12+rank*.06;
  g.turnAt=g.time+(feint?.18+.12*e233MotionRandom(g):.35+.55*e233MotionRandom(g))*Math.pow(motion.interval,.65)/Math.sqrt(diff)/(1+rank*.10);
 }
 if(g.time>=(g.burstAt||0)){g.burst=.72+e233MotionRandom(g)*.55;g.burstAt=g.time+(.28+e233MotionRandom(g)*.48)*Math.pow(motion.interval,.4);}
 const speed=Math.min(.40,(.18+g.fish.speed*.12)*Math.sqrt(diff)*Math.sqrt(profile.speed)*capture.pressure*(1+rank*.06))*Math.pow(motion.distance,1.25);
 g.travelMin=Math.min(g.travelMin??g.target,g.target);g.travelMax=Math.max(g.travelMax??g.target,g.target);
 if(g.time>=(g.travelAt??1.7)&&g.time>=(g.escapeUntil||0)){
  if(g.travelMax-g.travelMin<width*.28){g.escapeDir=g.target+12<width*.5?1:-1;g.escapeGoal=width*(g.escapeDir>0?.88:.12)-12;g.escapeUntil=g.time+Math.abs(g.escapeGoal-g.target)/(width*Math.max(.12,speed))+.2;}
  g.travelMin=g.travelMax=g.target;g.travelAt=g.time+1.7;
 }
 const direction=g.time<(g.escapeUntil||0)?g.escapeDir:g.dir;

 const desiredVelocity=direction*speed*width*(g.burst||1)+Math.sin(g.time*(4+g.fish.wiggle*5)+(g.seed||0))*g.fish.wiggle*width*.025;
 const ceiling=width*.42*Math.pow(motion.distance,1.25),capped=Math.max(-ceiling,Math.min(ceiling,desiredVelocity));
 const response=width*3.8*Math.sqrt(motion.distance);
 g.targetVelocity=(g.targetVelocity||0)+Math.max(-response*dt,Math.min(response*dt,capped-(g.targetVelocity||0)));
 g.target=Math.max(3,Math.min(width-30,g.target+g.targetVelocity*dt));
 if(g.target===3||g.target===width-30){g.dir=g.target===3?1:-1;g.escapeDir=g.dir;g.targetVelocity=Math.abs(g.targetVelocity)*g.dir;}
 const inside=g.target+12>=g.pos&&g.target+12<=g.pos+g.fish.zone;
 const endurance=1+Math.max(0,(g.fish.req||1)-32)*.003;
 const gain=.13/(1+Math.max(0,diff-1)*.20)/endurance*profile.gain/capture.endurance,loss=Math.max(.13,.10*(.85+diff*.14)*profile.loss)*capture.loss*(g.time<.9?.4:1);
 g.progress=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,g.progress+(inside?gain:-loss)*dt));
 return g.progress>=1?'won':g.time>4&&g.progress<=0?'escaped':null;
}
e16Animate=function(now){
 if(!e16Session)return;
 const g=e16Session,dt=Math.min(.05,Math.max(0,(now-e16Last)/1000));e16Last=now;
 const outcome=e190FishingStep(g,e16Holding,dt,e190TrackWidth());
 e16Paint();if(outcome)return e16Finish(outcome==='won');
 e16Frame=requestAnimationFrame(e16Animate);
};

function e193RodScore(r){
 // Valoración orientativa fija del equipo, sin incluir el nivel de Pesca del personaje.
 const st=e1101Stats(r),trophy=E1103_SPECIAL[r.id]?.trophy??Math.max(0,st.trophy-Math.floor((e16FishLevel()-1)/10));
 const score=.30*st.control/64+.25*st.resilience/45+.15*st.speed/40+.10*st.luck/43+.10*trophy/45+.10*Math.log1p(e190Capacity(r))/Math.log1p(3000);
 return clamp(Math.round(1+9*score),1,10);
}

 });

 });
 const total=entries.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.qty*x.price,0);

function e194RodArt(id){
 const k=String(id||'').toLowerCase(),mist=/bruma/.test(k),river=/guard|rio/.test(k),old=/viejo|pescador/.test(k),reef=/coral|nacar/.test(k),emerald=/laguna|esmeralda/.test(k),abyss=/abis|abyss|astral/.test(k),collector=/colecc/.test(k);

function e209CompletedQuests(){
 if(!state)return [];
 const rows=[];
 if(e17Done())rows.push({id:'e17_viejo',zone:'muelle',title:E17_QUEST.title,reward:E17_ROD.name,detail:E17_ROD.desc});
 for(const q of E171_QUESTS)if(e171Done(q))rows.push({id:q.id,zone:q.zone,title:q.title,reward:q.rod.name,detail:q.rod.desc});
 for(const q of E187_QUESTS)if(e187QuestDone(q))rows.push({id:q.id,zone:q.zone,title:q.title,reward:q.reward,detail:q.kind==='xp'?'Mejora permanente activa: se aplica automáticamente a la XP de cada captura.':q.kind==='control'?'Mejora permanente activa: se aplica automáticamente a tu barra de captura.':'Caña obtenida: disponible para equipar en la tienda.'});

 return rows;
}

function e195ShakeCount(fish){
 const id=(e161Rod()?.id||'').toLowerCase();
 // Cañas de gama alta: picada automática. Las demás: 1-3 toques.
 if(/astral|nacar|abisal|abyss/.test(id))return 0;
 const difficulty=Number(E161_DIFFICULTY[fish.id]||1);
 const advanced=/coral|esmeralda|reforz|bruma|colecc/.test(id);
 return Math.max(1,Math.min(3,Math.round(1+difficulty*.48-(advanced?1:0))));
}
function e195DrawShake(){

 dock.querySelector('.e195Shake')?.remove();
 if(!e195Pending||!e161Fishing||e16Session)return;

 const title=document.createElement('div');title.className='e195ShakeTitle';title.textContent='💧 ¡Movimiento en el agua!';
 const sub=document.createElement('div');sub.className='e195ShakeSub';sub.textContent='Tocá el círculo para atraer al pez';
 const count=document.createElement('div');count.className='e195ShakeCount';count.textContent='Toques restantes: '+e195Pending.left;
 const bubble=document.createElement('button');bubble.type='button';bubble.className='e195Bubble';bubble.textContent='Picar';bubble.setAttribute('aria-label','Picar: atraer al pez');
 // Distribución acotada para no ocultar el texto y permitir uso táctil.
 bubble.style.left=(15+Math.random()*68)+'%';bubble.style.top=(55+Math.random()*23)+'%';
 wrap.append(title,sub,count,bubble);
 const game=dock.querySelector('#e16GamePanel');if(game)game.before(wrap);else dock.append(wrap);
}

 // Attraction affects preparation, never fish motion or capture progress.

};

 // Con sacudidas hay una espera breve, luego el jugador atrae la picada.

 e161CatchAt=Date.now()+delay;

 },delay);

===== src/js/fishing/minigame/071-e1101-balance.js =====

"use strict";
/* Valores explícitos y legibles: cinco atributos, ventajas especializadas. */
function e1101Stats(rod){
 const id=String(rod?.id||'').toLowerCase();
 const index=E161_RODS.findIndex(r=>r.id===rod?.id);
 const old=E164_ROD_STATS[index]||{};
 const speed=Math.max(0,Number(old.wait||0));
 const luck=Math.max(0,Number(old.rare||0));
 const control=Math.max(0,Number(old.control??rod?.bonus??0));
 const trophy=0; // Se calcula por caña en e1102/e1103; nunca llamar e186LuckPercent desde e1101Stats.
 const resilience=/astral|abisal|abyss/.test(id)?22:/coral|nacar/.test(id)?16:/esmeralda|reforz/.test(id)?11:/bruma|costa/.test(id)?8:3;
 return {speed,luck,control,resilience,maxKg:e190Capacity(rod),trophy:Math.min(35,trophy)};
}
function e1101View(r){
 const st=e1101Stats(r);
 return '<div class="e1101Stats">'+[
 ['⚡ Atracción',st.speed+'%'],['🍀 Suerte',st.luck+'%'],['🎯 Control','+'+e190ControlPercent(st.control)+'%'],
 ['🛡 Resiliencia',st.resilience+'%'],['🏆 Trofeos',st.trophy+'%']
 ].map(([k,v])=>'<div class="e1101Stat">'+k+'<b>'+v+'</b></div>').join('')+'</div>';
}
const e1101OldPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){
 const result=e1101OldPage();
 const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');
 if(!page||!state)return result;
 const panel=page.querySelector('details:has(.fishRod)');
 if(panel)panel.querySelectorAll('.fishRod').forEach(row=>{
  const name=row.querySelector('b')?.textContent?.replace(/^✓\s*/,'').trim();
  const rod=E161_RODS.find(r=>r.name===name);
  if(!rod||row.querySelector('.e1101Stats'))return;
  const stats=document.createElement('div');stats.innerHTML=e1101View(rod);
  const info=row.querySelector('div');if(info)info.append(stats);
  const equipped=e161Rod(),a=e1101Stats(rod),b=e1101Stats(equipped);
  const diff=document.createElement('div');diff.className='e1101Compare';
  diff.textContent=equipped&&rod.id!==equipped.id?
    'Vs. tu caña: Atracción '+(a.speed-b.speed>=0?'+':'')+(a.speed-b.speed)+'% · Suerte '+(a.luck-b.luck>=0?'+':'')+(a.luck-b.luck)+'% · Control '+(a.control-b.control>=0?'+':'')+(a.control-b.control):
    'Estadísticas de tu caña actual';
  if(info)info.append(diff);
 });
 // Una sola referencia al estado de la picada dentro del HUD.
 const hud=document.getElementById('e188FishingHud');
 if(hud){
  const labels=hud.querySelectorAll('small,.fishMuted,.e188Status');
  let seen=false;
  labels.forEach(el=>{
   if(/pescando|esperando.*picada|pez.*luchando/i.test(el.textContent||'')){
    if(seen)el.style.display='none';else seen=true;
   }
  });
 }
 return result;
};


===== src/js/fishing/minigame/073-e1102-quest-balance.js =====

"use strict";
/* Los premios de misión no están en E161_RODS. No tratarlos como cañas de
   estadística cero: usar sus bonificaciones existentes, sin cambiar capturas. */
const E1102_QUEST_VALUES={
 e17_rod_viejo:{wait:10,rare:0,resilience:7,trophy:4},
 e171_rod_rio:{wait:12,rare:0,resilience:9,trophy:8},
 e171_rod_costa:{wait:15,rare:0,resilience:12,trophy:13},
 e18_rod_trofeo:{wait:0,rare:0,resilience:10,trophy:16},
 e187_rod_coral:{wait:16,rare:0,resilience:16,trophy:4}
};
const e1102PreviousStats=e1101Stats;
e1101Stats=function(rod){
 const base=e1102PreviousStats(rod),id=rod?.id||'';
 const special=E1102_QUEST_VALUES[id];
 const baseTrophy={e16_rod:0,e161_rod_bamboo:3,e161_rod_fiber:6,e161_rod_steel:12};
 const trophy=Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(baseTrophy,id)?baseTrophy[id]:
  special?.trophy??(id.includes('brumas')?13:id.includes('guard')?8:4);
 return {...base,speed:special?.wait??base.speed,luck:special?.rare??base.luck,
  resilience:special?.resilience??base.resilience,
  trophy:Math.min(30,trophy+(state?Math.floor((e16FishLevel()-1)/10):0))};
};
const e1102PreviousPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){
 const result=e1102PreviousPage();
 const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');
 const panel=page?.querySelector('details:has(.fishRod)');
 if(!panel||!state)return result;
 const all=[...E161_RODS,E17_ROD,...E171_QUESTS.map(q=>q.rod),E18_ROD,E187_ROD];
 const active=e161Rod();
 for(const row of panel.querySelectorAll('.fishRod')){
  const name=row.querySelector('b')?.textContent?.replace(/^✓\s*/,'').trim();
  const rod=all.find(r=>r.name===name);
  if(!rod)continue;
  row.querySelectorAll('.e1101Stats,.e1101Compare,.e1102QuestTag').forEach(el=>el.remove());
  const target=row.querySelector('div');if(!target)continue;
  const wrapper=document.createElement('div');wrapper.innerHTML=e1101View(rod);
  target.append(wrapper.firstElementChild);
  const a=e1101Stats(rod),b=e1101Stats(active);
  const names=[['Atracción','speed','%'],['Suerte','luck','%'],['Control','control',' px'],['Resiliencia','resilience','%'],['Trofeos','trophy','%']];
  const cmp=document.createElement('div');cmp.className='e1101Compare';
  cmp.textContent=rod.id===active?.id?'✓ Caña equipada':
   'Vs. equipada: '+names.map(([name,key,unit])=>{const delta=a[key]-b[key];return name+' '+(delta>0?'+':'')+delta+unit}).join(' · ');
  target.append(cmp);
  if(E1102_QUEST_VALUES[rod.id]){
   row.classList.add('e1102Quest');
   const tag=document.createElement('div');tag.className='e1102QuestTag';
   tag.textContent='🏆 Caña de misión · Estadísticas incluidas en la comparación';
   target.append(tag);
  }
 }
 return result;
};


===== src/js/fishing/minigame/075-e1103-progression.js =====

"use strict";
/* Especializaciones de cañas con efectos reales; se conservan todos los IDs
   y el formato de guardado anterior. */
/* @include src/catalogs/075-e1103-progression-e1103_special.js */
const e1103PrevStats=e1101Stats;
e1101Stats=function(rod){
 const base=e1103PrevStats(rod),special=E1103_SPECIAL[rod?.id];
 if(!special)return base;
 const bonus=Math.floor((e16FishLevel()-1)/10);
 return {...base,speed:special.speed,luck:special.luck,resilience:special.resilience,maxKg:special.maxKg,
  trophy:Math.min(rod?.family?45:35,special.trophy+bonus)};
};
/* Distribución efectiva: pesos relativos, normalizados por zona y nivel.
   El factor 0.42 del atún ya existía en data-e1122-fishing-fixes; se conserva sin rebalancear. */
function e222SpeciesWeight(fish,level,luck){
 const legacy=E234_BY_ID.get(fish.id);if(legacy)fish=E16_FISH.find(f=>f.id===legacy.from);
 const base=fish.rarity?fish.rarity*(1+luck/20):Math.max(.12,1/Math.pow(1+(level-fish.req)/4,.4)/Math.pow(E161_DIFFICULTY[fish.id]||1,2.3));
 return base*(fish.id==='atun'?.42:1);
}
function e161FishDistribution(zone=e165Zone(),level=e16FishLevel(),luck=e1101Stats(e161Rod()).luck){
 const pool=E16_FISH.filter(f=>zone.fish.includes(f.id)&&level>=f.req&&e190CanLift(f));
 const entries=pool.map(fish=>{
  return {fish,weight:e222SpeciesWeight(fish,level,luck)};
 });
 const total=entries.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.weight,0);
 return entries.map(x=>({...x,probability:x.weight/total}));
}
e161RandomFish=function(random=Math.random){
 const entries=e161FishDistribution();
 if(!entries.length)return null;
 let roll=random()*entries.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.weight,0);
 for(const entry of entries){roll-=entry.weight;if(roll<=0)return entry.fish;}
 return entries[entries.length-1].fish;
};
/* Atracción: afecta el tiempo previo a los círculos y respeta la ventaja de
   las cañas que evitan las sacudidas. */
const e1103OldNext=e161NextBite;
e161NextBite=function(){
 e1103OldNext();
 if(!e161Fishing||!e161OnPage()||e16Session||e195Pending||e161Waiting===null)return;
 const rod=e161Rod(),id=String(rod?.id||'').toLowerCase();
 const speed=e1101Stats(rod).speed;
 const delay=Math.max(450,Math.round(900*(1-speed/100)));
 clearTimeout(e161Waiting);
 e161CatchAt=Date.now()+delay;
 e161Waiting=setTimeout(()=>{
  e161Waiting=null;e161StopWait();
  if(!e161Fishing||!e161OnPage()||!e161CanFish()||e16Session)return;
  const fish=e161RandomFish();
  if(fish)e16Start(fish.id);
 },delay);
};
/* Trofeos: usar la estadística mostrada en el cálculo de ejemplares especiales. */
e186LuckPercent=function(){
 if(!state)return 0;
 return e1101Stats(e161Rod()).trophy;
};
/* Mostrar rol y ventajas claras en la tienda. */
