===== src/js/fishing/minigame/077-e1105-clean-fishing.js =====

"use strict";
/* El panel de captura queda exclusivamente para el minijuego.
   Datos de zona, caña y bonificaciones disponibles en un desplegable aparte. */
const e1105PriorPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){
 const result=e1105PriorPage();
 const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');
 const dock=page?.querySelector('.e162Dock');
 if(!dock)return result;
 const stats=dock.querySelector('.e185Summary');
 if(stats){
  let details=page.querySelector('.e1105Details');
  if(!details){
   details=document.createElement('details');
   details.className='e1105Details';
   const title=document.createElement('summary');
   title.textContent='🎣 Ver datos de zona, caña y bonificaciones';
   details.append(title);
   dock.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',details);
  }
  details.querySelector('.e185Summary')?.remove();
  details.append(stats);
 }
 // Una sola línea de estado antes del minijuego, sin mensajes duplicados.
 const status=page.querySelector('.e162DockHead');
 if(status){
  const nodes=[...status.querySelectorAll('*')].filter(n=>n.children.length===0);
  let seen=false;
  for(const n of nodes){
   if(/esperando picada|caña lanzada/i.test(n.textContent||'')){
    if(seen)n.style.visibility='hidden';else seen=true;
   }
  }
 }
 return result;
};

"use strict";
function e1107Sync(){
 const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing');
 const on=!!(state&&page&&e161OnPage()&&!page.classList.contains('hidden'));
 document.body.classList.toggle('e1107Fishing',on);
 const equipment=document.querySelector('.hybridSidebarBody details[data-ui-panel="equipment"]');
 if(equipment){
  const summary=equipment.querySelector('summary');
  if(summary){summary.textContent=on?'':'🛡 Equipamiento actual';summary.hidden=on;if(on)summary.parentElement.open=true;}
  let info=equipment.querySelector('.e1107RodPanel');
  if(!info){info=document.createElement('div');info.className='e1107RodPanel';equipment.append(info);}
  if(on){
   const rod=e161Rod(),st=rod?e1101Stats(rod):null;
   const fields=st?[['speed|Atracción',st.speed+'%'],['luck|Suerte',st.luck+'%'],['control|Control','+'+e190ControlPercent(st.control)+'% de barra'],['resilience|Resiliencia',st.resilience+'%'],['trophy|Trofeos',st.trophy+'%'],['maxKg|Peso máximo',e190Kg(e190Capacity(rod))]]:[];
   const key=JSON.stringify([rod?.name,fields]);
   if(info.dataset.renderKey!==key){info.dataset.renderKey=key;
   info.innerHTML='<div class="e1107RodName">'+(rod?'🎣 '+rod.name:'Sin caña equipada')+'</div>'+
    (rod?'<div class="e230RodHero">'+e230HudArt('rod',rod.id,250,84)+'</div>':'')+'<div class="e1107RodStats">'+fields.map(([name,value])=>'<div>'+e228Icon(name.split('|')[0])+name.split('|')[1]+'<b>'+value+'</b></div>').join('')+'</div>'+
    '<details class="e230StatHelp"><summary>Qué mejora cada atributo</summary><div class="e1107RodHelp">Atracción acelera la picada y reduce los toques previos; suerte mejora la aparición de peces raros; control amplía la barra; resiliencia ayuda con peces difíciles; trofeos mejora los ejemplares especiales.</div></details>';}
  }
 }
 if(!on)return;
 const dock=page.querySelector('.e162Dock'),game=dock?.querySelector('#e16GamePanel');
 if(!dock||!game)return;
 const waiting=dock.querySelector('#e161WaitingText');
 if(waiting&&e161Fishing)waiting.classList.add('e1107Quiet');
 // Mantener una sola explicación: en el minijuego de captura.
 const title=game.querySelector('.e162FightTitle');
 if(title&&e16Session)title.textContent='Mantené al pez dentro de la zona verde';
 // Botón disponible también durante los círculos.
 game.querySelectorAll('.e1107StopShake').forEach(n=>n.remove());
 if(e195Pending&&e161Fishing&&!e16Session){
  const stop=document.createElement('button');stop.type='button';stop.className='e1107StopShake';
  stop.textContent='Dejar de pescar';stop.setAttribute('aria-label','Dejar de pescar');
  game.append(stop);
 }
}
const e1107OldPage=e16Page;
e16Page=function(){const result=e1107OldPage();e1107Sync();return result;};

===== src/js/fishing/minigame/110-e208-catalog.js =====

"use strict";
// Stable IDs and explicit family budgets. req only groups the display; it never gates a purchase.
/* @include src/catalogs/110-e208-catalog-e208_families.js */
/* @include src/catalogs/110-e208-catalog-e208_specialties.js */
const E208_CATALOG=[];
for(const family of E208_FAMILIES)for(const specialty of E208_SPECIALTIES){
 const [control,speed,luck,resilience,trophy]=family.power.slice(0,5).map((n,i)=>clamp(n+specialty.delta[i],0,[64,40,43,45,45][i]));
 const rod={id:`e208_rod_${family.id}_${specialty.id}`,name:`${family.name} · ${specialty.name}`,price:Math.round(family.price*specialty.cost/5)*5,req:family.req,bonus:control,desc:specialty.role,family:family.id,art:family.art,specialty:specialty.id};
 if(family.quest){
  const zone=E165_ZONES.find(z=>z.id===family.zone),maxReachable=Math.max(...zone.fish.map(id=>e190WeightRange(E16_FISH.find(f=>f.id===id))[1]));
  rod.unlock={zone:family.zone,catches:specialty.goal[0],species:specialty.goal[1],bestKg:Math.round(maxReachable*specialty.goal[2]),title:`${family.name}: ${specialty.name}`};
 }
 E1103_SPECIAL[rod.id]={speed,luck,resilience,trophy,maxKg:Math.min(3000,Math.round(family.power[5]*specialty.capacity)),role:specialty.role};
 E161_RODS.push(rod);E164_ROD_STATS.push({control,wait:speed,rare:luck,label:specialty.role});E208_CATALOG.push(rod);
}
function e208QuestProgress(rod){
 const q=rod.unlock,record=state?.ui?.fishingWeights?.byZone?.[q?.zone]||{};
 const catches=Number(record.catches||0),species=e234SpeciesCount(record,q?.zone),bestKg=Number(record.bestKg||0);
 return {catches,species,bestKg,ready:!!q&&e16FishLevel()>=rod.req&&catches>=q.catches&&species>=q.species&&bestKg>=q.bestKg};
}
function e208QuestText(rod){
 const q=rod.unlock,p=e208QuestProgress(rod),zone=E165_ZONES.find(z=>z.id===q.zone);
 return `${zone.name}: alcanzá Pesca ${rod.req}; capturá ${q.catches} peces (${Math.min(p.catches,q.catches)}/${q.catches}), ${q.species} especies distintas (${Math.min(p.species,q.species)}/${q.species}) y un pez de ${e190Kg(q.bestKg)} o más (récord: ${e190Kg(p.bestKg)}).`;
}
function e212QuestGoals(rod){
 const q=rod.unlock,p=e208QuestProgress(rod),zone=E165_ZONES.find(z=>z.id===q.zone);
 const goals=[[e16FishLevel()>=rod.req,'Alcanzá habilidad de Pesca '+rod.req,e16FishLevel()+' de '+rod.req],[p.catches>=q.catches,`Capturá ${q.catches} peces en total`,`${Math.min(p.catches,q.catches)} de ${q.catches}`],[p.species>=q.species,`Capturá ${q.species} especies distintas`,`${Math.min(p.species,q.species)} de ${q.species}`],[p.bestKg>=q.bestKg,`Capturá un pez de ${e190Kg(q.bestKg)} o más`,`Tu récord: ${e190Kg(p.bestKg)}`]];
 return `<p>Completá estos tres objetivos en <b>${esc(zone.name)}</b>:</p><ul class="e212Goals">${goals.map(([done,label,progress])=>`<li class="${done?'done':''}"><span>${done?'✓':'○'} ${esc(label)}</span><b>${esc(progress)}</b></li>`).join('')}</ul><small>Cuenta lo que ya pescaste en esta zona. No se consumen peces; podés venderlos.</small>`;
}

function e208FilterShop(shop,expand=false){
 const query=(shop.querySelector('[data-rod-search]')?.value||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().trim();
 const mine=shop.dataset.mine==='true',source=shop.querySelector('[data-rod-filter]')?.value||'all',zone=shop.querySelector('[data-rod-zone]')?.value||'all',sort=shop.querySelector('[data-rod-sort]')?.value||'price';let count=0;
 shop.querySelectorAll('.rodTier').forEach(tier=>{
  let visible=0;const container=tier.querySelector('.rodRows'),rows=[...tier.querySelectorAll('.rodShopRow')];
  rows.sort((a,b)=>{
   const owned=r=>Number(!!state.owned?.[r.dataset.rodId]);
   if(sort==='owned')return owned(b)-owned(a)||Number(b.classList.contains('equipped'))-Number(a.classList.contains('equipped'))||Number(a.dataset.price)-Number(b.dataset.price);
   if(sort==='price')return Number(a.dataset.price)-Number(b.dataset.price);
   if(sort==='levelAsc'||sort==='levelDesc'){const x=a.dataset.requiredLevel,y=b.dataset.requiredLevel;return x===''||y===''?(x===y?a.dataset.rodId.localeCompare(b.dataset.rodId):x===''?1:-1):(sort==='levelDesc'?Number(y)-Number(x):Number(x)-Number(y))||a.dataset.rodId.localeCompare(b.dataset.rodId);}
   return Number(b.dataset[sort])-Number(a.dataset[sort])||owned(b)-owned(a);
  }).forEach(row=>container.append(row));
  for(const row of rows){
   const owned=!!state.owned?.[row.dataset.rodId],text=(row.textContent+' '+(e232RodAliases.get(row.dataset.rodId)||'')).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase();
   const matches=source==='all'||source==='owned'&&owned||source==='locked'&&!owned||source==='affordable'&&row.dataset.source==='gold'&&!owned&&state.gold>=Number(row.dataset.price)||source===row.dataset.source;
   row.hidden=!(text.includes(query)&&matches&&(!mine||owned)&&(zone==='all'||row.dataset.zone===zone));if(!row.hidden)visible++;
  }
  count+=visible;tier.hidden=visible===0;if(expand&&(query||mine||source!=='all'||zone!=='all')&&visible)tier.open=true;
 });
 const mineButton=shop.querySelector('[data-rod-mine]');if(mineButton)mineButton.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(mine));
 const result=shop.querySelector('[data-rod-results]');if(result)result.textContent=count+' cañas · '+[...shop.querySelectorAll('.rodShopRow')].filter(row=>state.owned?.[row.dataset.rodId]).length+' en tu colección';
}

function e208CatalogAction(event){
 const button=event.target.closest('[data-catalog-claim],[data-rod-quest]');if(!button)return;
 event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();
 if(!state||!ready||busy||managing||!storageReady||simulationLocked()||e16Session||e195Pending)return;

  e161Fishing=false;e161StopWait();state.ui.fishingZoneId=zone;selectWorkspaceTab('fishing');e16Page();

 }
 const rod=E208_CATALOG.find(r=>r.id===button.dataset.catalogClaim);
 if(!rod?.unlock||state.owned?.[rod.id]||!e208QuestProgress(rod).ready)return;
 state.owned[rod.id]=true;state.ui.fishingRodId=rod.id;
 e161Fishing=false;e161StopWait();persist();renderGame();toast('🏆 Desafío completado: '+rod.name);
}

'use strict';
// Stable acquisition reference, not the player's live chance. No quality/price proxy.
function e222DifficultyReference(){
 const result=new Map();
 for(const zone of E165_ZONES){
  const pool=E16_FISH.filter(f=>zone.fish.includes(f.id)&&f.req<=100&&zone.req<=100),total=pool.reduce((n,f)=>n+e222SpeciesWeight(f,100,0),0);
  for(const fish of pool){const chance=e222SpeciesWeight(fish,100,0)/total,level=Math.max(zone.req,fish.req),old=result.get(fish.id);if(!old||chance>old.chance||chance===old.chance&&level<old.level)result.set(fish.id,{chance,level});}
 }
 return result;
}
function e222QuestForRod(rod){return e216QuestDefinitions().find(q=>q.rodId===rod.id);}

 const q=progress.quests.find(q=>q.id===view.focusQuest);if(!q)return '';
 return '<section class="e222QuestPreview" tabindex="-1" data-quest-preview="'+esc(q.id)+'"><h4>'+esc(q.title)+'</h4><ul>'+q.goals.map(g=>'<li>'+esc(g.label)+' · '+fmt(Math.min(g.have,g.need))+' / '+fmt(g.need)+'</li>').join('')+'</ul><p>Recompensa: '+esc(q.reward)+(q.done?' · Ya obtenida':'')+'</p><small>'+esc(q.note)+'</small></section>';
}
function e222Highlight(node){if(!node)return;node.classList.remove('e222Highlight');void node.offsetWidth;node.classList.add('e222Highlight');node.onanimationend=()=>{node.classList.remove('e222Highlight');node.onanimationend=null;};node.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches?'instant':'smooth'});}
function e222FindRod(rod){
 const quest=e222QuestForRod(rod);
 if(quest){const root=document.getElementById('fishingAtlas');if(!root)return;const view=e216View();view.open=true;view.search='';view.filter='all';view.zone=quest.zone;view.focusQuest=quest.id;root.open=true;root.querySelector('[data-atlas-search]').value='';root.querySelector('[data-atlas-filter]').value='all';e216RenderAtlas(root);const target=root.querySelector('[data-quest-preview]');target?.focus({preventScroll:true});e222Highlight(target);return;}
 const shop=document.getElementById('fishingRodShop');if(!shop)return;shop.open=true;shop.dataset.mine='false';for(const [key,value] of [['search',''],['filter','all'],['zone','all']])shop.querySelector('[data-rod-'+key+']').value=value;shop.querySelector('[data-rod-search]').dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));const row=[...shop.querySelectorAll('[data-rod-id]')].find(n=>n.dataset.rodId===rod.id);if(row){row.closest('.rodTier').open=true;e222Highlight(row);}
}
function e222PickerStatus(rod){
 const provenance=e220RodProvenance(rod),level=e217RequiredLevel(rod),owned=!!state.owned?.[rod.id],locked=level!==null&&e16FishLevel()<level;
 return provenance.label+' · '+(locked?'Bloqueada · Requiere Pesca '+level:rod.id===e161Rod()?.id?'✓ Equipada':owned?'Disponible':'No obtenida')+(!owned?(provenance.quest?' · '+e210RodRequirement(rod):' · '+fmt(rod.price)+' oro'):'');
}
function e222RefreshPicker(popup){
 if(!popup)return;const rods=new Map(e221Rods().map(r=>[r.id,r]));for(const row of popup.querySelectorAll('.e1120Choice')){const rod=rods.get(row.dataset.rodId);if(!rod)continue;const active=rod.id===e161Rod()?.id;row.classList.toggle('active',active);row.querySelector('b').textContent=rod.name+(active?'  ✓':'');row.querySelector('.rodComparisons').innerHTML=e216RodComparison(rod);row.querySelector('.rodProvenance').textContent=e222PickerStatus(rod);}
 popup.e222Update?.();

(function(){
 const old=e16Page,viewStates=new WeakMap();
 let renderedState=null;
 // Presentation recommendations only. Buying/equipping keeps the existing rules.

  {key:'inicio',name:'Aprendiz',range:'Para tus primeras capturas'},

 ];
 function placeShop(){

  if(state.ui?.tab==='fishing')document.querySelector('#page_fishing .e171Quests')?.after(shop);
  else document.getElementById('page_shop')?.prepend(shop);
 }
 function remember(){

  const shop=document.getElementById('fishingRodShop');if(!shop)return;
  viewStates.set(state,{open:shop.open,mine:shop.dataset.mine==='true',search:shop.querySelector('[data-rod-search]')?.value||'',filter:shop.querySelector('[data-rod-filter]')?.value||'all',location:e165Zone().id,zone:shop.querySelector('[data-rod-zone]')?.value||'all',sort:shop.querySelector('[data-rod-sort]')?.value||'price',tiers:Object.fromEntries([...shop.querySelectorAll('.rodTier')].map(d=>[d.dataset.tier,d.open]))});
 }
 function rodRow(rod){
  const index=E161_RODS.findIndex(r=>r.id===rod.id),owned=!!state.owned?.[rod.id],equipped=e161Rod()?.id===rod.id;
  const st=e1101Stats(rod),stats=e216RodComparison(rod);
  let action;
  if(rod.unlock&&!owned)action=e208QuestProgress(rod).ready?`data-catalog-claim="${rod.id}"`:`data-rod-quest="${rod.id}"`;
  else if(index>=0)action=owned?`data-e164-equip="${index}"`:`data-e161-buy="${index}"`;
  else if(!owned)action=`data-rod-quest="${rod.id}"`;
  else if(rod.id===E17_ROD.id)action='data-e17-equip';
  else if(rod.id===E18_ROD.id)action='data-e18-equip';
  else if(rod.id===E187_ROD.id)action='data-e187-equip';
  else action=`data-e171-equip="${E171_QUESTS.find(q=>q.rod.id===rod.id).id}"`;
  const quest=index>=0?null:rod.id===E17_ROD.id?{title:E17_QUEST.title}:E171_QUESTS.find(q=>q.rod.id===rod.id)||(rod.id===E187_ROD.id?E187_QUESTS.find(q=>q.kind==='rod'):null);
  const isQuest=!!rod.unlock||index<0;
  const requirement=isQuest?e210RodRequirement(rod):'Disponible por oro';
  const score=e193RodScore(rod),stars=`<span class="rodShopStars" role="img" aria-label="Valoración general: ${score} de 10 estrellas" title="Valoración orientativa: ${score}/10. Compará cada atributo según el pez."><span aria-hidden="true">${'★'.repeat(score)}<span class="emptyStars">${'☆'.repeat(10-score)}</span></span> ${score}/10</span>`;
  const disabled=equipped||!!e16Session||!!e195Pending||(!owned&&(!isQuest&&state.gold<rod.price));
  return `<article class="rodShopRow${isQuest?' questRod':''}${equipped?' equipped':''}" data-rod-id="${rod.id}" data-source="${isQuest?'quest':'gold'}" data-zone="${e210RodZone(rod)}" data-price="${rod.price||0}" data-required-level="${e217RequiredLevel(rod)??''}" data-control="${st.control}" data-resilience="${st.resilience}" data-speed="${st.speed}" data-luck="${st.luck}" data-trophy="${st.trophy}" data-max-kg="${st.maxKg}"><div class="rodShopArt">${e194RodArt(rod.id)}</div><div class="rodShopInfo"><div class="rodShopTitle"><b>${esc(rod.name)}</b>${stars}<span class="rodSource${isQuest?' quest':''}">${isQuest?'📜 Misión':'🪙 Compra'}</span></div><div class="rodShopStats">${stats}</div><small>${esc(requirement)}${rod.unlock?'':' · '+esc(E1103_SPECIAL[rod.id]?.role||rod.desc)}</small></div><div class="rodShopAction"><span>${!isQuest?fmt(rod.price)+' oro':'Premio de misión'}</span><button class="small" ${action} ${disabled?'disabled':''}>${equipped?'✓ Equipada':owned?'Equipar':rod.unlock?(e208QuestProgress(rod).ready?'Reclamar':'Ver encargo'):isQuest?'Ver encargo':'Comprar'}</button></div></article>`;
 }
 e16Page=function(){
  if(fishingFinishDepth)return;
  fishingRenderDepth++;
  try {
   e216Remember();remember();
   const previousShop=document.getElementById('fishingRodShop');
   const result=old.apply(this,arguments);
   const page=document.getElementById('page_fishing'),shop=page?.querySelector('details:has(.fishRod)');
   if(!shop||!state)return result;

   const sameLocation=view?.location===e165Zone().id;

Compará sus atributos según el pez.<small>Atracción: menos espera y toques · Suerte: especies raras · Control: barra más ancha · Resiliencia: movimiento más suave · Trofeos: mejores ejemplares.</small></p>`;
   for(const tier of tiers){

    shop.append(detail);
   }
   shop.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',`<div class="rodCatalogTools"><input type="search" data-rod-search aria-label="Buscar cañas por nombre, especialidad o zona" placeholder="Buscar caña, especialidad o zona…"><select data-rod-filter aria-label="Filtrar cañas"><option value="all">Todas</option><option value="gold">Por oro</option><option value="quest">De misión</option><option value="owned">Mis cañas</option><option value="locked">Por desbloquear</option><option value="affordable">Puedo comprar</option></select><select data-rod-zone aria-label="Filtrar por zona"><option value="all">Todas las zonas</option>${e221ZoneOptions()}</select><select data-rod-sort aria-label="Ordenar cañas"><option value="price">Precio: menor a mayor</option><option value="control">Mayor control</option><option value="resilience">Mayor resiliencia</option><option value="speed">Mayor atracción</option><option value="luck">Mayor suerte</option><option value="trophy">Más trofeos</option><option value="maxKg">Mayor capacidad</option></select><button type="button" class="small" data-rod-mine>Mis cañas</button><small data-rod-results aria-live="polite"></small></div>`);
   // Keep summary first for native details semantics.
   shop.prepend(shop.querySelector('summary'));
   shop.querySelector('[data-rod-search]').value=view?.search||'';
   shop.querySelector('[data-rod-filter]').value=view?.filter||'all';
   shop.querySelector('[data-rod-zone]').value=view?.zone||'all';
   shop.querySelector('[data-rod-sort]').value=view?.sort==='owned'?'price':view?.sort||'price';
   const filter=()=>{e208FilterShop(shop,true);remember();};

   shop.addEventListener('input',filter);shop.addEventListener('change',filter);e208FilterShop(shop);
   if(previousShop&&previousShop!==shop)previousShop.remove();
   placeShop();
   e190DecorateFishingPage();e216CompactQuests();
   page.querySelector('h2')?.remove();
   renderedState=state;
   shop.addEventListener('toggle',()=>{if(shop.isConnected)remember();},true);
   return result;
  } finally {fishingRenderDepth--;if(!fishingRenderDepth){e16Paint();measureDock();}}
 };
 const previousTab=selectWorkspaceTab;
 selectWorkspaceTab=function(id,save=true){const result=previousTab(id,save);placeShop();return result;};
 window.addEventListener('click',event=>{

 },true);

===== src/js/fishing/shop/119-e217-rod-ux.js =====

'use strict';
function e220RodProvenance(rod){
 const zoneId=e210RodZone(rod),zone=E165_ZONES.find(z=>z.id===zoneId),family=E208_FAMILIES.find(f=>f.id===rod.family);
 const quest=rod.unlock?.title||(rod.id===E17_ROD.id?E17_QUEST.title:rod.id===E18_ROD.id?'El trofeo de las corrientes':rod.id===E187_ROD.id?E187_QUESTS.find(q=>q.kind==='rod').title:E171_QUESTS.find(q=>q.rod.id===rod.id)?.title);
 const method=quest?'Encargo':'Compra por oro';
 return {zone:zone?.name||'',family:family?.name||'',quest:quest||'',method,label:[zone?.name,method].filter(Boolean).join(' · '),detail:[family?'Familia: '+family.name:null,quest?'Encargo: '+quest:null,zone?'Zona: '+zone.name:null,'Obtención: '+method].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')};
}
const E217_STAT_KEYS=['speed','luck','control','resilience','trophy','maxKg'];
const E217_SORT_OPTIONS=[['recommended','Recomendadas'],['speed','Mayor atracción'],['luck','Mayor suerte'],['control','Mayor control'],['resilience','Mayor resiliencia'],['maxKg','Mayor capacidad de peso']];
function e217RequiredLevel(rod){
 // Character skill gates only quest acquisition, never gold purchases or owned equipment.
 if(rod.unlock)return rod.req;
 if(rod.id===E17_ROD.id)return E17_QUEST.level;
 const quest=E171_QUESTS.find(q=>q.rod.id===rod.id);if(quest)return quest.level;
 if(rod.id===E187_ROD.id)return E187_QUESTS.find(q=>q.kind==='rod').req;
 return null;
}
function e217LevelCompare(a,b,descending=false){
 const x=e217RequiredLevel(a),y=e217RequiredLevel(b);
 if(x===null||y===null)return x===y?0:x===null?1:-1;
 return descending?y-x:x-y;
}
function e217Dominates(a,b){return E217_STAT_KEYS.every(k=>a[k]>=b[k])&&E217_STAT_KEYS.some(k=>a[k]>b[k]);}
function e217Recommended(rods){
 const current=e161Rod(),stats=new Map(rods.map(r=>[r.id,e1101Stats(r)])),result=[];
 for(const owned of [true,false]){
  const group=rods.filter(r=>!!state.owned?.[r.id]===owned),edges=group.map(()=>[]),incoming=group.map(()=>0);
  for(let i=0;i<group.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<group.length;j++){
   if(e217Dominates(stats.get(group[i].id),stats.get(group[j].id))){edges[i].push(j);incoming[j]++;}
   else if(e217Dominates(stats.get(group[j].id),stats.get(group[i].id))){edges[j].push(i);incoming[i]++;}
  }
  let front=group.map((_,i)=>i).filter(i=>incoming[i]===0);
  while(front.length){front.sort((a,b)=>Number(group[b].id===current?.id)-Number(group[a].id===current?.id)||a-b);const next=[];
   for(const i of front){result.push(group[i]);for(const j of edges[i])if(--incoming[j]===0)next.push(j);}front=next;
  }
 }
 return result;
}
function e217SortRods(rods,sort='recommended'){
 if(sort==='recommended')return e217Recommended(rods);
 const position=new Map(rods.map((r,i)=>[r.id,i]));
 return [...rods].sort((a,b)=>(sort==='levelAsc'||sort==='levelDesc'?e217LevelCompare(a,b,sort==='levelDesc'):e1101Stats(b)[sort]-e1101Stats(a)[sort])||position.get(a.id)-position.get(b.id));
}
function e217MenuTools(popup,anchor){
 const toolbar=document.createElement('div');toolbar.className='e217RodTools';
 toolbar.innerHTML=`<input type="search" aria-label="Buscar caña" placeholder="Buscar caña…"><select aria-label="Ordenar cañas" title="Compará atributos y especialidades de las cañas">${E217_SORT_OPTIONS.map(([id,label])=>`<option value="${id}">${label}</option>`).join('')}</select><button type="button" aria-label="Cerrar selector de cañas">✕</button><label class="e222Mine"><input type="checkbox" data-picker-mine> Solo mis cañas</label>`;
 popup.prepend(toolbar);popup.setAttribute('aria-label','Seleccionar caña');
 const search=toolbar.querySelector('input'),sort=toolbar.querySelector('select');
 const normalize=t=>t.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase();
 const choices=new Map([...popup.querySelectorAll('.e1120Choice')].map(row=>[row.dataset.rodId,row])),rods=e221Rods().filter(r=>choices.has(r.id));
 let orderKey='';
 const update=()=>{
  const key=sort.value+'|'+e16FishLevel()+'|'+e161Rod()?.id+'|'+rods.map(r=>Number(!!state.owned?.[r.id])).join('');
  if(key!==orderKey){for(const rod of e217SortRods(rods,sort.value))popup.append(choices.get(rod.id).closest('.e222RodEntry'));orderKey=key;}
  const query=normalize(search.value.trim()),mine=toolbar.querySelector('[data-picker-mine]').checked;for(const rod of rods){const row=choices.get(rod.id),hidden=!normalize(rod.name+' '+(e232RodAliases.get(rod.id)||'')).includes(query)||(mine&&!state.owned?.[rod.id]);row.hidden=hidden;row.closest('.e222RodEntry').hidden=hidden;}
  popup.scrollTop=0;
 };

'use strict';
// Presentation and read-only recognitions. The v7 records remain authoritative.
const e228TrophyViews=new WeakMap();
function e228TrophyView(){if(!e228TrophyViews.has(state))e228TrophyViews.set(state,{open:false,filter:'earned',zone:'all',kind:'all',sort:'rare',selected:null});return e228TrophyViews.get(state);}
function e228FishName(f){return f.name.replace(/[★☆✨]/gu,'').trim();}
function e228Icon(key){const paths={speed:'M3 12h14m-5-5 5 5-5 5M3 7h5M3 17h5',luck:'M12 3 15 9 21 12 15 15 12 21 9 15 3 12 9 9Z',control:'M3 8v8m18-8v8M3 12h18m-14-3v6m10-6v6',resilience:'M12 3 20 6v6q-1 6-8 9-7-3-8-9V6Z',trophy:'M7 3h10v7q0 5-5 5t-5-5ZM7 6H3v4q0 3 5 3m9-7h4v4q0 3-5 3m-4 2v6m-5 0h10',maxKg:'M4 8h16l2 13H2ZM9 8V5q3-4 6 0v3',lock:'M6 10h12v11H6Zm3 0V6q3-6 6 0v4',hook:'M14 3v12q0 7-7 5-4-2-2-6l2 3'};return '<svg class="e228Icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="'+(paths[key]||paths.hook)+'" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>';}
function e228Rarity(chance){return chance>=.25?'Frecuente':chance>=.12?'Poco común':chance>=.06?'Rara':chance>=.025?'Muy rara':chance>=.01?'Excepcional':'Mítica';}
function e228Mystery(zone,id){
 const seed=[...id].reduce((n,c)=>n+c.charCodeAt(0),0)%3;
 return '<span class="e228Habitat variant'+seed+'" role="img" aria-label="Hábitat misterioso de '+esc(zone.name)+'">'+e227ZoneArt(zone,'e228MysteryLandscape')+'<svg viewBox="0 0 240 120" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 105Q30 35 42 104Q65 65 76 118M160 120Q170 42 184 109Q203 72 214 118" fill="none" stroke="#a8c5ad" stroke-width="5" opacity=".35"/><path d="M0 68Q60 43 120 65T240 64M0 82Q60 61 120 80T240 83" fill="none" stroke="#b5d9d1" opacity=".28"/><circle cx="'+(45+seed*55)+'" cy="35" r="3" fill="#d7dab3" opacity=".7"/></svg></span>';
}
function e228Collection(){
 const zone=e165Zone(),grid=document.querySelector('#page_fishing .e186BookGrid');if(!grid)return;
 const refs=e222DifficultyReference(),pool=E16_FISH.filter(f=>zone.fish.includes(f.id)),chance=new Map(pool.map(f=>[f.id,refs.get(f.id)?.chance||0]));
 const found=pool.filter(f=>e227Discovered(state,f.id)).length,book=grid.closest('.e186Book'),caption=book?.querySelector(':scope>summary .e221HeaderWords small'),fill=book?.querySelector('.e186Progress>div');if(caption)caption.textContent=found+'/'+pool.length+' especies descubiertas';if(fill)fill.style.width=(pool.length?100*found/pool.length:0)+'%';
 const cards=[...grid.querySelectorAll('[data-species-id]')];cards.sort((a,b)=>chance.get(b.dataset.speciesId)-chance.get(a.dataset.speciesId)||(refs.get(a.dataset.speciesId)?.level||0)-(refs.get(b.dataset.speciesId)?.level||0)||a.dataset.speciesId.localeCompare(b.dataset.speciesId));
 for(const card of cards){const f=pool.find(f=>f.id===card.dataset.speciesId);if(!f)continue;const known=e227Discovered(state,f.id),locked=e16FishLevel()<Math.max(zone.req,f.req),rarity=e228Rarity(chance.get(f.id));card.classList.toggle('e186Unknown',!known);card.classList.toggle('e228LevelLocked',!known&&locked);
  const sprite=card.querySelector('.e192Sprite'),title=card.querySelector('strong');if(sprite)sprite.innerHTML=known?e189FishSVG(f.id):e228Mystery(zone,f.id);
  if(title)title.textContent=known?e228FishName(f):locked?'Fuera de alcance · Pesca '+f.req:'Especie desconocida';
  card.querySelector('.e228Rarity')?.remove();const tag=document.createElement('span');tag.className='e228Rarity';tag.textContent=rarity;title?.after(tag);
  card.title=known?e228FishName(f)+' · Rareza de especie: '+rarity+' · Referencia: nivel 100, sin suerte, capacidad suficiente y mejor zona. No es tu probabilidad actual. La calidad del ejemplar es independiente.':(locked?'Necesitás Pesca '+f.req+'. ':'')+'Capturá esta especie para descubrirla. Rareza de especie: '+rarity;grid.append(card);
 }
 for(const card of document.querySelectorAll('[data-journal-fish]')){const f=E16_FISH.find(f=>f.id===card.dataset.journalFish);const name=card.querySelector('.e225JournalCaption>b');if(name)name.textContent=e228FishName(f);card.title+=' · Rareza de especie: '+e228Rarity(refs.get(f.id)?.chance||1);}
}
function e228Recognitions(){
 const defs=e216QuestDefinitions(),items=[];
 for(const z of E165_ZONES){const p=e216ZoneProgress(z,defs),difficulty=z.req;
  for(const q of p.quests){const theme=q.goals.some(g=>/Entregá/.test(g.label))?'quest-delivery':q.goals.some(g=>/kg|peso|Récord/.test(g.label))?'quest-weight':'quest-exploration';items.push({id:'quest:'+q.id,kind:'quest',zone:z,title:q.title,earned:q.done,difficulty:difficulty+1,artKind:'quest',artId:q.id,goals:q.goals,reward:q.reward,note:q.note,quest:q.id});}
  items.push({id:'collection:'+z.id,kind:'collection',zone:z,title:'Explorador de '+z.name,earned:p.accredited,difficulty:difficulty+2,artKind:'collection',artId:z.id,goals:[{label:'Descubrir todas las especies',have:p.discovered.length,need:p.species.length}],reward:'Insignia de colección · premio visual',note:'Se reclama en la colección de zona. Los premios históricos ya obtenidos se conservan.'});
  items.push({id:'mastery:'+z.id,kind:'mastery',zone:z,title:'Maestría de '+z.name,earned:p.complete,difficulty:difficulty+3,artKind:'medal',artId:z.id,goals:p.stars.map((yes,i)=>({label:['Descubrir '+p.threshold+' especies','Completar los encargos principales','Completar la colección'][i],have:Number(yes),need:1})),reward:'Medalla exclusiva de maestría · premio visual',note:'Tres hitos independientes. Incluye tus estrellas históricas; los encargos complementarios no las revocan.'});
 }
 // Prestige is a catalogue ranking, never a percentage of players.
 for(const i of items)i.prestige=4*i.zone.req+({quest:0,collection:45,mastery:90}[i.kind])+5*i.goals.length+Math.min(30,i.goals.reduce((n,g)=>n+Math.log2(1+Math.max(0,g.need)),0));
 return items;
}

===== src/js/layout/006-hybrid-upgrade.js =====

"use strict";
/* Interfaz híbrida; motor y guardados conservados. */
const compactWorkspaceStable = organizeWorkspace;
const compactLiveStable = updateLive;
const hybridDesktop = window.matchMedia('(min-width:851px)');
function hybridSkillCard(name,xp,weapon=false){
  const level=weapon?levelFromXp(xp):skillLvl(xp);
  const low=weapon?xpFor(level):10*Math.pow(level-1,2);
  const high=weapon?xpFor(level+1):10*Math.pow(level,2);
  const percent=clamp((xp-low)/(high-low)*100,0,100);
  return `<div class="skillCard"><div class="row"><span>${esc(name)}</span><b>Nv ${level}</b></div><div class="bar xp" role="progressbar" aria-label="Progreso de ${esc(name)}" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100" aria-valuenow="${Math.floor(percent)}"><div style="width:${percent}%"></div></div><small>${fmt(xp-low)} / ${fmt(high-low)} XP para el próximo nivel</small></div>`;
}
function sidebarLabel(s){return (s.ui?.tab==='fishing'?'Progreso de Pesca':'Atributos y habilidades')+' · '+(document.getElementById('hybridSidebar')?.open?'Contraer':'Expandir');}
function hybridRefresh(){
  if(!state)return;
  const box=document.getElementById('skillsBox');
  if(box){
    const rows=[['weapon','⚔ Combate con Armas',state.skill.xp],...SKILLS.map(k=>[k.key,k.name,state.skills[k.key].xp])];
    if(box.children.length!==6){box.innerHTML=rows.map(([k,n,x])=>hybridSkillCard(n,x,k==='weapon')).join('');Array.from(box.children).forEach((c,i)=>c.dataset.skillKey=rows[i][0]);}
    rows.forEach(([k,n,x],i)=>{const c=box.children[i],l=k==='weapon'?levelFromXp(x):skillLvl(x),a=progressThreshold(k,l),b=progressThreshold(k,l+1),p=clamp(100*(x-a)/(b-a),0,100);c.querySelector('.row b').textContent='Nv '+l+' → '+(l+1);c.querySelector('.bar > div').style.width=p+'%';c.querySelector('.bar').setAttribute('aria-valuenow',p.toFixed(1));c.querySelector('small').textContent=`${fmt(x-a)} / ${fmt(b-a)} XP · ${p.toFixed(1)}% · faltan ${fmt(b-x)} XP`;});
  }
  const summary=document.getElementById('hybridProfileSummary');
  if(summary){
    const d=derive(state);
    summary.innerHTML='<span class="hybridProfileLabel">'+(sidebarLabel(state))+'</span>';
  }
  const start=document.getElementById('dockStart'),stop=document.getElementById('dockStop');
  if(start)start.disabled=busy||managing||(state.combat.active&&!navActive(state))||state.restLeft>0;
  if(stop)stop.disabled=busy||managing||!state.combat.active;
  measureDock();
}
organizeWorkspace=function(){
  const prefs=uiPreferences(state);
    if(prefs.tab==='character'||prefs.tab==='equipment')prefs.tab='combat';
  compactWorkspaceStable();
  const root=document.getElementById('uiWorkspace');
  const layout=document.createElement('div');layout.className='hybridLayout';
  const sidebar=document.createElement('details');sidebar.id='hybridSidebar';sidebar.className='hybridSidebar';
  sidebar.innerHTML='<summary id="hybridProfileSummary" aria-label="Expandir o contraer ficha del personaje"></summary><div class="hybridSidebarBody"></div>';
  const body=sidebar.querySelector('.hybridSidebarBody');
  body.innerHTML='<div class="sidebarQuickActions"><button class="small" data-act="switchCharacter">Cambiar personaje</button></div>';
  const main=document.createElement('div');main.className='hybridMain';
  for(const node of Array.from(root.children))main.append(node);
  layout.append(sidebar,main);root.append(layout);
  for(const key of ['attributes','skills','equipment','save']){
    const panel=root.querySelector(`details[data-ui-panel="${key}"]`);
    if(!panel)continue;
    body.append(panel);
    const label=panel.querySelector('summary');
    if(key==='attributes'){
      panel.open=true;
      if(label){label.textContent='👤 '+state.name;label.hidden=true;}
    }else if(key==='skills'){
      panel.open=true;if(label)label.textContent='✦ Habilidades';
    }else if(key==='equipment'){
      panel.open=true;if(label)label.textContent='🛡 Equipamiento actual';
    }else if(label)label.textContent='⚙ Guardado y respaldo';
  }
    for(const id of ['character','equipment']){
    document.getElementById('tab_'+id)?.remove();
    document.getElementById('page_'+id)?.remove();
  }
  const saveBody=body.querySelector('details[data-ui-panel="save"] .sectionBody');
  if(saveBody){
    const stable=document.createElement('button');stable.className='small';
    stable.type='button';stable.textContent='Descargar copia completa';
    stable.addEventListener('click',downloadCompactStable);saveBody.append(stable);
  }
  const attributes=body.querySelector('details[data-ui-panel="attributes"]');
  const skills=body.querySelector('details[data-ui-panel="skills"]');
  const equipment=body.querySelector('details[data-ui-panel="equipment"]');
  for(const panel of [attributes,skills,equipment]){
    if(!panel)continue;
    const label=panel.querySelector('summary');
    if(label)label.style.pointerEvents='none';
    panel.addEventListener('toggle',()=>{if(panel.isConnected&&!panel.open)panel.open=true;});
  }
  sidebar.open=hybridDesktop.matches||prefs.panels.mobileProfile===true;
  sidebar.addEventListener('toggle',()=>{
    if(!sidebar.isConnected||!state||busy||managing)return;
    if(hybridDesktop.matches){if(!sidebar.open)sidebar.open=true;return;}
    const current=uiPreferences(state);
    if(current.panels.mobileProfile!==sidebar.open){current.panels.mobileProfile=sidebar.open;persist();}
    hybridRefresh();
  });
  const dock=document.querySelector('.dockInner');
  if(!document.getElementById('dockControls')){
    const controls=document.createElement('div');controls.id='dockControls';controls.className='dockControls';
    controls.innerHTML='<button id="dockStart" class="primary" data-act="start">▶ Combatir</button><button id="dockStop" data-act="stop">■ Detener</button>';
    dock.append(controls);
  }
  hybridRefresh();
};
updateLive=function(){compactLiveStable();hybridRefresh();};
function hybridMediaChange(){
  const sidebar=document.getElementById('hybridSidebar');
  if(sidebar&&state)sidebar.open=hybridDesktop.matches||uiPreferences(state).panels.mobileProfile===true;
  measureDock();
}
if(hybridDesktop.addEventListener)hybridDesktop.addEventListener('change',hybridMediaChange);
else hybridDesktop.addListener(hybridMediaChange);
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  const tab=e.target.closest('[data-ui-tab]');if(!tab||busy||managing)return;
  const tabs=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-ui-tab]'));
  const index=tabs.indexOf(tab);let next=index;
  if(e.key==='ArrowRight')next=(index+1)%tabs.length;
  else if(e.key==='ArrowLeft')next=(index+tabs.length-1)%tabs.length;
  else if(e.key==='Home')next=0;
  else if(e.key==='End')next=tabs.length-1;
  else return;
  e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  selectWorkspaceTab(tabs[next].dataset.uiTab);tabs[next].focus();
},true);
function downloadCompactStable(){
  // Every download must preserve the complete, executable standalone application.
  downloadHTML();
}


===== src/js/shop/014-e15-merchant.js =====

"use strict";
/* E1.5: mercader de materiales. No añade campos al guardado v7 ni cambia las recompensas. */
const E15_PRICES=Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(ENEMIES.map(en=>[
  en.drop.itemId, Math.max(3,Math.round((6+en.level*1.65)*(en.boss?2:1)))
])));
function e15MerchantPanel(){
 const page=document.getElementById('page_inventory');
 if(!page||!state)return;
 let panel=document.getElementById('e15Merchant');
 if(!panel){
   panel=document.createElement('section');panel.id='e15Merchant';panel.className='panel e15Merchant';
   panel.style.cssText='margin-top:12px;padding:14px;border:1px solid #80632f;border-radius:9px';
   page.append(panel);
 }
 const inv=state.inventory||{};
 const rows=Object.keys(inv).filter(id=>Number.isSafeInteger(inv[id])&&inv[id]>0&&Object.hasOwn(E15_PRICES,id));
 const total=rows.reduce((n,id)=>n+inv[id]*E15_PRICES[id],0);
 panel.innerHTML=`<h3 style="margin:0 0 8px">⚖ Mercader de recursos</h3>
 <p class="muted" style="margin:0 0 12px">Vendé materiales de enemigos para comprar tu primer equipo. Los materiales podrían servir para fabricar objetos en futuras versiones; guardá los que quieras conservar.</p>
 <div class="muted" style="margin-bottom:8px">Tu oro: <b>${fmt(state.gold)}</b> · Valor de los materiales: <b>${fmt(total)}</b> oro</div>
 ${rows.length?rows.map(id=>{
 const count=inv[id],price=E15_PRICES[id];
 return `<div class="shopRow" style="flex-wrap:wrap"><div><b>${esc(itemNameOf(id))}</b><br><small class="muted">Tenés ${fmt(count)} · ${fmt(price)} oro por unidad</small></div>
 <div style="display:flex;gap:5px;flex-wrap:wrap"><button type="button" class="small" data-e15-sell="${esc(id)}" data-e15-qty="1">Vender 1</button><button type="button" class="small" data-e15-sell="${esc(id)}" data-e15-qty="all">Vender todo (+${fmt(count*price)})</button></div></div>`;
 }).join(''):'<p class="muted">Todavía no tenés materiales para vender. Los enemigos pueden soltarlos al derrotarlos.</p>'}
 <small class="muted">Los precios son fijos por tipo de material. Vender no otorga XP ni consume tiempo de combate.</small>`;
}
const e15OldRenderGame=renderGame;
renderGame=function(){e15OldRenderGame();e15MerchantPanel();};
const e15OldUpdateInv=updateInv;
updateInv=function(){e15OldUpdateInv();if(document.getElementById('e15Merchant'))e15MerchantPanel();};
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
 const btn=e.target.closest('[data-e15-sell]');if(!btn)return;
 e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
 if(!state||!ready||busy||managing||!storageReady||btn.disabled)return;
 if(typeof simulationLocked==='function'&&simulationLocked()){toast('Terminá la simulación antes de vender.');return;}
 syncRuntime();if(busy)return;
 const id=btn.dataset.e15Sell;
 if(!Object.hasOwn(E15_PRICES,id))return;
 const owned=state.inventory?.[id]||0;
 const qty=btn.dataset.e15Qty==='all'?owned:1;
 if(!Number.isSafeInteger(owned)||!Number.isSafeInteger(qty)||qty<1||qty>owned)return;
 const reward=qty*E15_PRICES[id];
 if(!Number.isSafeInteger(reward)||!Number.isSafeInteger(state.gold+reward))return toast('No se puede completar la venta.');
 state.inventory[id]=owned-qty;
 if(!state.inventory[id])delete state.inventory[id];
 state.gold+=reward;
 addLog('Vendiste '+qty+' × '+itemNameOf(id)+' por '+fmt(reward)+' oro.');
 persist();renderGame();toast('Venta realizada: +'+fmt(reward)+' oro.');
},true);


===== src/js/ui/132-global-header.js =====
'use strict';
// Header is presentation only: real character XP, original save/import/DEV actions.
let e227GeneralDropTimer=0,e227GeneralPending=0,e227GeneralFrame=0,e227GeneralOwner=null;
function e227ClearGeneralDrop(){clearTimeout(e227GeneralDropTimer);cancelAnimationFrame(e227GeneralFrame);e227GeneralFrame=0;e227GeneralPending=0;e227GeneralOwner=null;document.querySelector('.generalXPDrop')?.remove();}
function e227TopShell(){
 const header=document.querySelector('body>header');if(header.querySelector('.generalIdentity'))return header;
 header.classList.add('globalHeader');
 const top=document.createElement('div');top.className='globalHeaderTop';
 const identity=document.createElement('div');identity.className='generalIdentity';identity.innerHTML='<b id="generalCharacter"></b><div class="generalXP"><span id="generalXPText"></span><div class="generalXPTrack" role="progressbar" aria-label="Experiencia general del personaje" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100"><span id="generalXPBar"></span></div></div>';
 const title=header.querySelector('h1');top.append(identity,title);header.prepend(top);
 const nav=document.createElement('div');nav.className='globalHeaderNav';
 const settings=document.createElement('details');settings.id='gameSettings';settings.innerHTML='<summary aria-label="Configuración" title="Configuración"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m9 2-1 3-3 1-2 3 2 3-1 3 3 3 3-1 3 2 3-2 3 1 3-3-1-3 2-3-2-3-3-1-1-3Z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5"/><circle cx="12" cy="11" r="3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5"/></svg><span class="saveIndicator" aria-hidden="true"></span></summary><div class="settingsPanel"><h2>Configuración</h2><div id="settingsSaveState" role="status"></div><div class="settingsActions"></div><div class="settingsTechnical"><small>Estable 34 · Guardado automático · v7 · hasta cinco personajes</small><div id="settingsLastSave"></div></div></div>';
 nav.append(settings);header.append(nav);
 if(typeof ResizeObserver!=='undefined')new ResizeObserver(()=>document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--global-header-height',header.getBoundingClientRect().height+'px')).observe(header);
 const status=header.querySelector('#saveStatus');if(status){settings.querySelector('#settingsSaveState').append(status);if(status.classList.contains('warn')){settings.querySelector('summary').classList.add('saveError');settings.querySelector('summary').setAttribute('aria-label','Configuración · Atención: '+status.textContent);}}
 e227MoveDev();return header;
}
function e227MoveDev(){const button=document.getElementById('devModeToggle'),host=document.querySelector('.settingsActions');if(button&&host)host.append(button);}
function e227InstallTop(){
 const header=e227TopShell(),root=document.getElementById('uiWorkspace');
 const active=!!(state&&ready&&!document.getElementById('gameScreen').classList.contains('hidden'));
 document.body.classList.toggle('hasActiveCharacter',active);
 if(!root||!state){e227UpdateTop();return;}
 const tabs=root.querySelector('.workspaceTabs');if(tabs){header.querySelector('.workspaceTabs')?.remove();header.querySelector('.globalHeaderNav').prepend(tabs);}
 // Move the original controls, preserving data actions and file inputs.
 const save=root.querySelector('details[data-ui-panel="save"]'),host=header.querySelector('.settingsActions');
 if(save){for(const child of [...host.children])if(child.id!=='devModeToggle')child.remove();const body=save.querySelector('.sectionBody');if(body)host.prepend(...body.childNodes);save.remove();}
 root.querySelector('.sidebarQuickActions')?.remove();
 const workspaceHeader=root.querySelector('.workspaceHeader');if(workspaceHeader){const rest=root.querySelector('#restBtn')||workspaceHeader.querySelector('[data-act="rest"]');if(rest){const contextual=document.createElement('div');contextual.className='combatContextActions';contextual.append(rest);root.querySelector('#page_combat')?.prepend(contextual);}workspaceHeader.remove();}
 const session=document.getElementById('e13SessionBox');if(session)root.querySelector('#page_combat')?.prepend(session);
 // Duplicate identity/progression is now in the top row. Keep HP, MP, stats and rest.
 const attributes=root.querySelector('details[data-ui-panel="attributes"] .sectionBody');if(attributes){for(const id of ['lvlText','xpText','xpBar']){const n=attributes.querySelector('#'+id);if(n){if(id==='xpBar')n.parentElement.hidden=true;else n.closest('.row').hidden=true;}}attributes.querySelector(':scope>.muted')?.setAttribute('hidden','');}
 if(!host.querySelector('[data-previous-stable]'))host.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<p><a data-previous-stable href="https://github.com/marianoczer-afk/Idle-Czerniak/blob/59e988e2ab95b7d6a7c719f5b500ffe20f9ab4d7/index.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Versión anterior · Estable 33</a></p>');
 e227MoveDev();e227UpdateTop();
}
function e227UpdateTop(){
 const header=document.querySelector('.globalHeader');if(!header)return;
 e215SetText(document.getElementById('settingsLastSave'),lastSave?'Último guardado correcto: '+lastSave:'Todavía no hay un guardado confirmado');
 const active=!!(state&&ready&&!document.getElementById('gameScreen').classList.contains('hidden'));document.body.classList.toggle('hasActiveCharacter',active);
 const title=header.querySelector('h1');title.textContent=active?'ARGENTUM IDLE ONLINE':'Argentum Idle Online · Estable 34';
 if(!active){e227ClearGeneralDrop();return;}
 const level=levelFromXp(state.xp),low=xpFor(level),high=xpFor(level+1),earned=Math.max(0,state.xp-low),need=high-low,percent=clamp(earned/need*100,0,100);
 e215SetText(document.getElementById('generalCharacter'),`${state.name} · ${classById(state.cls).name} · ${raceById(state.race).name} · Nv ${level}`);
 e215SetText(document.getElementById('generalXPText'),`${fmt(earned)} / ${fmt(need)} XP`);
 const bar=document.getElementById('generalXPBar');bar.style.width=percent+'%';bar.parentElement.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',percent.toFixed(1));bar.parentElement.setAttribute('aria-valuetext',`${fmt(earned)} de ${fmt(need)} XP para nivel ${level+1}`);
 if(e227GeneralOwner&&e227GeneralOwner!==state)e227ClearGeneralDrop();
}
function e227GeneralDrop(s,amount){
 if(!(amount>0)||s!==state||!ready||busy||managing||OFF.active||document.hidden||gameMode==='dev')return;
 e227GeneralOwner=s;e227GeneralPending+=amount;if(e227GeneralFrame)return;
 e227GeneralFrame=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{e227GeneralFrame=0;const value=e227GeneralPending;e227GeneralPending=0;if(state!==s||document.hidden)return;
 const host=document.querySelector('.generalXP');if(!host)return;host.querySelector('.generalXPDrop')?.remove();const drop=document.createElement('span');drop.className='generalXPDrop';drop.textContent='+'+fmt(value)+' XP';drop.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');host.append(drop);clearTimeout(e227GeneralDropTimer);e227GeneralDropTimer=setTimeout(()=>drop.remove(),1750);});
}
const e227PreviousXP=gainCharacterXP;
gainCharacterXP=function(s,n){const amount=e227PreviousXP(s,n);e227GeneralDrop(s,amount);return amount;};
const e227PreviousStatus=setStatus;
setStatus=function(msg,warn){e227PreviousStatus(msg,warn);const summary=document.querySelector('#gameSettings>summary');if(summary){summary.classList.toggle('saveError',!!warn);summary.setAttribute('aria-label',warn?'Configuración · Atención: '+msg:'Configuración · '+(lastSave?'Último guardado '+lastSave:'Guardado automático'));}const panel=document.querySelector('.settingsTechnical');if(panel)panel.title=msg;};
const e227PreviousRender=renderGame;
renderGame=function(){e227ClearGeneralDrop();document.querySelector('.globalHeader .workspaceTabs')?.remove();const result=e227PreviousRender();e227InstallTop();return result;};
const e227PreviousLive=updateLive;
updateLive=function(){const result=e227PreviousLive();e227UpdateTop();return result;};
const e227PreviousShowGame=showGame;
showGame=function(){const result=e227PreviousShowGame();e227InstallTop();return result;};
const e227PreviousCharacters=showCharacters;
showCharacters=function(){e227ClearGeneralDrop();const result=e227PreviousCharacters();e227TopShell();e227UpdateTop();return result;};
const e227PreviousModeChrome=refreshModeChrome;
refreshModeChrome=function(){const result=e227PreviousModeChrome();e227MoveDev();return result;};
function e227CleanDownload(page){const header=page.querySelector('body>header');header.className='';header.innerHTML='<h1>Argentum Idle Online · Estable 34</h1><div id="saveStatus">Cargando...</div>';page.querySelectorAll('.generalXPDrop').forEach(n=>n.remove());page.querySelector('body').classList.remove('hasActiveCharacter');}
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{const panel=document.getElementById('gameSettings');if(panel?.open&&!panel.contains(event.target))panel.open=false;});
document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Escape'){const panel=document.getElementById('gameSettings');if(panel?.open){panel.open=false;panel.querySelector('summary').focus();}}});
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)e227ClearGeneralDrop();});

===== package.json =====
{
  "name": "argentum-idle-online",
  "private": true,
  "scripts": {
    "dev": "npm run build && python3 -m http.server 8000 --bind 0.0.0.0",
    "test": "node --test tests/build.test.cjs tests/expansion27.test.cjs tests/stability.test.cjs tests/trophies28.test.cjs tests/polish29.test.cjs tests/presentation30.test.cjs tests/world31.test.cjs tests/progression32.test.cjs tests/polish33.test.cjs tests/fishing34.test.cjs",
    "audit": "node scripts/audit-source.cjs",
    "build": "node scripts/build.cjs",
    "build:check": "node scripts/build.cjs --check",
    "assets:optimize": "python3 scripts/optimize-assets.py",
    "art:prepare": "node scripts/production-art.cjs",
    "art:gallery": "node scripts/build-art-gallery.cjs",
    "test:polish33": "node --test tests/polish33.test.cjs",
    "test:fishing34": "node --test tests/fishing34.test.cjs"

===== scripts/build.cjs =====
'use strict';
const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),crypto=require('node:crypto'),{Script}=require('node:vm');
const root=path.join(__dirname,'..'),hash=b=>crypto.createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
function local(file){const target=path.resolve(root,file);if(!target.startsWith(root+path.sep))throw Error('Path outside project: '+file);return target;}
function expand(file,stack=[]){if(stack.includes(file))throw Error('Circular source include: '+stack.concat(file));return fs.readFileSync(local(file),'utf8').replace(/\/\* @include ([\w/.-]+) \*\//g,(_,f)=>expand(f,stack.concat(file)));}
function generate(){const manifest=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(local('assets/manifest.json'))),blocks=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(local('src/blocks.json'))),uris=new Map();let html=fs.readFileSync(local('src/template.html'),'utf8');
 html=html.replace('@@ART_LIBRARY@@',()=>require('./production-art.cjs').library());
 for(const b of blocks){const token='@@BLOCK:'+b.id+'@@';if(!html.includes(token))throw Error('Missing ordered block '+b.id);let code=expand(b.file);if(b.type==='script')new Script(code,{filename:b.file});html=html.replace(token,()=>code);}
 html=html.replace(/@@ASSET:([^@]+)@@/g,(_,id)=>{if(uris.has(id))return uris.get(id);const a=manifest.resources[id];if(!a)throw Error('Missing resource '+id);const bytes=fs.readFileSync(local(a.runtime));if(!bytes.length)throw Error('Empty resource '+id);
 const same=a.runtime===a.source;if(!same&&a.runtimeSha256&&hash(bytes)!==a.runtimeSha256)throw Error('Runtime asset modified without updating manifest: '+id);if(same&&hash(bytes)!==a.sha256)throw Error('Original asset modified without updating manifest: '+id);
 const key=hash(Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(same?a.originalPrefix+':'+a.encoding:a.runtimeMime),bytes])),cache=local('.build-cache/'+key+'.uri');let uri;if(fs.existsSync(cache))uri=fs.readFileSync(cache,'utf8');else{uri=same?(a.encoding==='percent'?a.originalPrefix+a.originalPayload:a.originalPrefix+bytes.toString('base64')):'data:'+a.runtimeMime+';base64,'+bytes.toString('base64');fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(cache),{recursive:true});fs.writeFileSync(cache,uri);}uris.set(id,uri);return uri;});
 if(/@@(?:ASSET|BLOCK):|@@ART_LIBRARY@@|\/\* @include /.test(html))throw Error('Unresolved build token');
 new Script([...html.matchAll(/<script\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/g)].map(m=>m[1]).join('\n;\n'));
 return {html,manifest,blocks};
}
function build({check=false}={}){const {html,manifest,blocks}=generate(),target=local('index.html'),bytes=Buffer.byteLength(html),sha256=hash(html);if(check){if(!fs.existsSync(target)||fs.readFileSync(target,'utf8')!==html)throw Error('index.html is stale or not generated from these sources');}else{const temp=target+'.build-tmp';fs.writeFileSync(temp,html);fs.renameSync(temp,target);}console.log(`${check?'Verified':'Built'} index.html: ${bytes} bytes, ${blocks.length} ordered blocks, SHA-256 ${sha256}`);return{bytes,sha256};}
module.exports={build,generate,expand,local};if(require.main===module)build({check:process.argv.includes('--check')});


===== scripts/production-art.cjs =====
'use strict';
const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path');const root=path.join(__dirname,'..');
function data(){const base=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'arte/produccion-estable31.json'),'utf8')),extra=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'arte/produccion-estable34.json'),'utf8'));return {...base,build:extra.build,sheets:base.sheets.concat(extra.sheets),entries:base.entries.concat(extra.entries)};}
function clipShape(e){return e.clipPath?`<path d="${e.clipPath}"/>`:`<rect x="${e.bounds[0]}" y="${e.bounds[1]}" width="${e.bounds[2]}" height="${e.bounds[3]}"/>`;}
function library(){const p=data(),frames=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'src/catalogs/133-art-frames.js'),'utf8').replace(/^const E227_ART=/,'').replace(/;\s*$/,'')),expected={};
 const seen=new Set();for(const e of p.entries){const key=e.kind+':'+e.id,sheet=p.sheets.find(s=>s.id===e.sheetId),[x,y,w,h]=e.bounds;if(seen.has(key)||!sheet||x<0||y<0||w<=0||h<=0||x+w>sheet.width||y+h>sheet.height)throw Error('Invalid production illustration '+key);seen.add(key);expected[key]={symbol:e.symbol,width:w,height:h};}
 if(JSON.stringify(frames)!==JSON.stringify(expected))throw Error('Stale art frames: run npm run art:prepare before building');
 const water=p.entries.find(e=>e.kind==='ui'&&e.id==='water'),waterPattern=`<pattern id="argentum-water-skin" patternUnits="objectBoundingBox" width="1" height="1" viewBox="0 0 ${water.bounds[2]} ${water.bounds[3]}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice"><use href="#${water.symbol}" width="${water.bounds[2]}" height="${water.bounds[3]}"/></pattern>`;
 return '<svg id="argentum-art-27" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="0" height="0" aria-hidden="true" style="position:absolute;pointer-events:none"><defs>'+p.sheets.map(s=>`<image id="${s.id}" width="${s.width}" height="${s.height}" href="@@ASSET:${s.assetId}@@"/>`).join('')+p.entries.map(e=>`<clipPath id="clip-${e.symbol}">${clipShape(e)}</clipPath><symbol id="${e.symbol}" viewBox="${e.bounds.join(' ')}" overflow="hidden"><use href="#${e.sheetId}" clip-path="url(#clip-${e.symbol})"/></symbol>`).join('')+waterPattern+'<filter id="argentum-specimen-edge" color-interpolation-filters="sRGB"><feComponentTransfer><feFuncA type="discrete" tableValues="0 0 0 1"/></feComponentTransfer></filter></defs></svg>';}
function prepare(){const p=data(),manifest=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root,'assets/manifest.json')));const frames={};for(const e of p.entries){frames[e.kind+':'+e.id]={symbol:e.symbol,width:e.bounds[2],height:e.bounds[3]};const folder=path.join(root,'assets/illustrations',e.kind);fs.mkdirSync(folder,{recursive:true});const sheet=p.sheets.find(s=>s.id===e.sheetId),original=manifest.resources[e.assetId].source.split('/').pop();fs.writeFileSync(path.join(folder,e.id+'.svg'),`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="${e.bounds.join(' ')}" width="${e.bounds[2]}" height="${e.bounds[3]}"><defs><clipPath id="viewport">${clipShape(e)}</clipPath></defs><image width="${sheet.width}" height="${sheet.height}" href="../../originals/${original}" clip-path="url(#viewport)"/></svg>\n`);}
 fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root,'src/catalogs/133-art-frames.js'),'const E227_ART='+JSON.stringify(frames,null,1)+';\n');console.log('Prepared '+p.entries.length+' individually editable SVG viewports; runtime shares '+p.sheets.length+' images.');}
module.exports={library,data};if(require.main===module)prepare();


===== scripts/package-release.py =====
"""Create a deterministic, offline single-file ZIP from the verified build."""
import hashlib,json,re,subprocess,zipfile
from pathlib import Path
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
subprocess.run(['node','scripts/build.cjs','--check'],cwd=root,check=True)
data=(root/'index.html').read_bytes();version=re.search(rb'name="argentum-build" content="(estable-\d+)"',data)[1].decode();folder=root/'descargas';folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True);target=folder/('argentum-idle-'+version.replace('estable-','estable')+'.zip')
entry=zipfile.ZipInfo('index.html',(1980,1,1,0,0,0));entry.compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED;entry.external_attr=0o644<<16
with zipfile.ZipFile(target,'w',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,compresslevel=9) as archive:archive.writestr(entry,data,compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,compresslevel=9)
with zipfile.ZipFile(target) as archive:
 assert archive.namelist()==['index.html'];assert archive.testzip() is None;assert archive.read('index.html')==data
info={'build':version,'htmlBytes':len(data),'htmlSha256':hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),'zipFile':target.name,'zipBytes':target.stat().st_size,'zipSha256':hashlib.sha256(target.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'contents':['index.html'],'selfContained':True}
(folder/(version.replace('estable-','estable')+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(info,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(info,indent=2))
